export async function checkAndAwardAchievements(userId: string): Promise<AchievementDef[]> {
  // Получаем уже полученные достижения
  const { data: existing } = await supabase
    .from('user_achievements')
    .select('achievement_id')
    .eq('user_id', userId);
  const earnedIds = new Set((existing ?? []).map(e => e.achievement_id));

  // Собираем статистику
  const [examsRes, sessRes, matsRes, mistakesRes, xp] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from('exam_sessions').select('score').eq('user_id', userId).eq('status', 'completed'),
    supabase.from('study_sessions').select('date, cards_reviewed').eq('user_id', userId).order('date', { ascending: false }),
    supabase.from('materials').select('id').eq('user_id', userId),
    supabase.from('mistake_diary').select('is_resolved').eq('user_id', userId).eq('is_resolved', true),
    getUserXP(userId),
  ]);

  const exams = examsRes.data ?? [];
  const sessions = sessRes.data ?? [];
  const materials = matsRes.data ?? [];

  // Считаем стрик
  const dates = new Set(sessions.map((s: StudySession) => s.date));
  let streak = 0;
  let d = new Date();
  while (dates.has(d.toISOString().split('T')[0])) {
    streak++;
    d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1);
  }

  const stats: UserStats = {
    totalExams: exams.length,
    totalReviews: sessions.reduce((a: number, s: StudySession) => a + (s.cards_reviewed || 0), 0),
    totalAnalyze: materials.length,
    streak,
    exam5Count: exams.filter((e: any) => e.score >= 85).length,
    totalMistakesResolved: (mistakesRes.data ?? []).length,
    totalXP: xp,
  };

  // Находим новые достижения
  const newAchievements: AchievementDef[] = [];
  for (const ach of ACHIEVEMENTS) {
    if (!earnedIds.has(ach.id) && ach.check(stats)) {
      newAchievements.push(ach);
      await supabase.from('user_achievements').insert({ user_id: userId, achievement_id: ach.id });
      await addXP(userId, ach.xp);
    }
  }

  return newAchievements;
}

// ── Квесты ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function getQuestsWithProgress(userId: string) {
  const { data: quests, error: qErr } = await supabase.from('quests').select('*');
  if (qErr || !quests) return [];

  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const now = new Date();
  const day = now.getDay();
  const diff = now.getDate() - day + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1);
  const monday = new Date(now.setDate(diff)).toISOString().split('T')[0];

  const { data: progress, error: pErr } = await supabase
    .from('user_quest_progress')
    .select('*')
    .eq('user_id', userId);

  if (pErr) return quests.map(q => ({ ...q, progress: 0, is_completed: false }));

  return quests.map(q => {
    const resetDate = q.type === 'daily' ? today : monday;
    const prog = progress?.find(p => p.quest_id === q.id && p.reset_at === resetDate);
    return { ...q, current_count: prog?.current_count || 0, is_completed: prog?.is_completed || false, reset_at: resetDate };
  });
}

export async function updateQuestProgress(userId: string, targetType: TargetType, increment: number = 1): Promise<boolean> {
  const { data: targetQuests } = await supabase.from('quests').select('*').eq('target_type', targetType);
  if (!targetQuests || targetQuests.length === 0) return false;

  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const now = new Date();
  const day = now.getDay();
  const diff = now.getDate() - day + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1);
  const monday = new Date(now.setDate(diff)).toISOString().split('T')[0];

  let anyCompleted = false;

  for (const quest of targetQuests) {
    const resetAt = quest.type === 'daily' ? today : monday;
    const { data: existing } = await supabase
      .from('user_quest_progress')
      .select('*')
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .eq('quest_id', quest.id)
      .eq('reset_at', resetAt)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (existing) {
      if (existing.is_completed) continue;
      const newCount = existing.current_count + increment;
      const isCompleted = newCount >= quest.target_count;
      if (isCompleted) anyCompleted = true;
      await supabase
        .from('user_quest_progress')
        .update({ current_count: newCount, is_completed: isCompleted, completed_at: isCompleted ? new Date().toISOString() : null, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
        .eq('id', existing.id);
      if (isCompleted) await addXP(userId, quest.xp_reward ?? 20);
    } else {
      const isCompleted = increment >= quest.target_count;
      if (isCompleted) anyCompleted = true;
      await supabase.from('user_quest_progress').insert({
        user_id: userId, quest_id: quest.id, current_count: increment,
        is_completed: isCompleted, completed_at: isCompleted ? new Date().toISOString() : null, reset_at: resetAt,
      });
      if (isCompleted) await addXP(userId, quest.xp_reward ?? 20);
    }
  }

  return anyCompleted;
}

import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';

export interface CareerProfile {
  skills: string[];
  professions: { title: string; match: number }[];
  resume_summary: string;
  projects: { title: string; description: string }[];
}

// Получить профиль из базы данных
export async function getCareerProfile(userId: string): Promise<CareerProfile | null> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('career_profiles')
    .select('profile_json')
    .eq('user_id', userId)
    .single();

  if (error && error.code !== 'PGRST116') { // PGRST116 = no rows returned
    console.error('Error fetching career profile:', error);
    return null;
  }
  
  return data ? (data.profile_json as CareerProfile) : null;
}

// Сгенерировать профиль через AI и сохранить в базу
export async function generateCareerProfile(userId: string, context: string): Promise<CareerProfile | null> {
  try {
    // 1. Вызов AI бэкенда
    const res = await fetch('/api/career/generate', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ materials_context: context })
    });
    
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error('Не удалось сгенерировать карьерный профиль');
    }
    
    const profileData: CareerProfile = await res.json();
    
    // 2. Сохранение в Supabase (Upsert)
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('career_profiles')
      .upsert({
        user_id: userId,
        profile_json: profileData,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      }, { onConflict: 'user_id' });
      
    if (error) {
      console.error('Error saving career profile:', error);
    }
    
    return profileData;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('generateCareerProfile error:', error);
    return null;
  }
}

import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';

export interface Friendship {
  id: string;
  requester_id: string;
  addressee_id: string;
  status: 'pending' | 'accepted' | 'declined';
  created_at: string;
  friend_id?: string;
  friend_name?: string;
  friend_avatar?: string;
}

export interface DirectMessage {
  id: string;
  sender_id: string;
  receiver_id: string;
  content: string;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface Notification {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  type: 'friend_request' | 'message' | 'like' | 'invite';
  title: string;
  body?: string;
  related_id?: string;
  is_read: boolean;
  created_at: string;
}

// ── Друзья ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function getFriends(userId: string): Promise<Friendship[]> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('friendships')
    .select('*')
    .or(`requester_id.eq.${userId},addressee_id.eq.${userId}`)
    .neq('status', 'declined');

  if (error || !data) return [];

  const friendIds = data.map(f => f.requester_id === userId ? f.addressee_id : f.requester_id);
  if (friendIds.length === 0) return [];

  const { data: profiles } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('id, full_name, avatar_url').in('id', friendIds);
  const profileMap = new Map((profiles || []).map(p => [p.id, p]));

  return data.map(f => {
    const friendId = f.requester_id === userId ? f.addressee_id : f.requester_id;
    const p = profileMap.get(friendId);
    return {
      ...f,
      friend_id: friendId,
      friend_name: p?.full_name || 'Неизвестный',
      friend_avatar: p?.avatar_url
    };
  });
}

export async function sendFriendRequest(requesterId: string, addresseeId: string) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('friendships').insert({
    requester_id: requesterId,
    addressee_id: addresseeId,
    status: 'pending'
  });
  if (!error) {
    // Создаём уведомление адресату
    await supabase.from('notifications').insert({
      user_id: addresseeId,
      type: 'friend_request',
      title: 'Новая заявка в друзья',
      body: 'Кто-то хочет добавить вас в друзья',
      related_id: requesterId
    });
  }
  return !error;
}

export async function respondFriendRequest(friendshipId: string, status: 'accepted' | 'declined') {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('friendships').update({ status }).eq('id', friendshipId);
  return !error;
}

export async function searchUsers(query: string, currentUserId: string) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('profiles')
    .select('id, full_name, avatar_url, university')
    .ilike('full_name', `%${query}%`)
    .neq('id', currentUserId)
    .limit(20);
  if (error) return [];
  return data || [];
}

// ── Чат (Realtime) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function getMessages(user1: string, user2: string): Promise<DirectMessage[]> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('direct_messages')
    .select('*')
    .or(`and(sender_id.eq.${user1},receiver_id.eq.${user2}),and(sender_id.eq.${user2},receiver_id.eq.${user1})`)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
    .limit(100);
  if (error) console.error('getMessages error:', error);
  return data || [];
}

export async function sendMessage(senderId: string, receiverId: string, content: string) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('direct_messages').insert({
    sender_id: senderId,
    receiver_id: receiverId,
    content
  }).select('*').single();

  if (!error) {
    // Уведомление получателю
    await supabase.from('notifications').insert({
      user_id: receiverId,
      type: 'message',
      title: 'Новое сообщение',
      body: content.slice(0, 80),
      related_id: senderId
    });
  }
  if (error) console.error('sendMessage error:', error);
  return data;
}

export function subscribeToMessages(userId: string, onMessage: (msg: DirectMessage) => void) {
  const channel = supabase
    .channel(`dm_${userId}`)
    .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'direct_messages', filter: `receiver_id=eq.${userId}` },
      (payload) => onMessage(payload.new as DirectMessage))
    .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'direct_messages', filter: `sender_id=eq.${userId}` },
      (payload) => onMessage(payload.new as DirectMessage))
    .subscribe();
  return () => supabase.removeChannel(channel);
}

// ── Уведомления ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function getNotifications(userId: string): Promise<Notification[]> {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('notifications')
    .select('*')
    .eq('user_id', userId)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(30);
  return data || [];
}

export async function getUnreadCount(userId: string): Promise<number> {
  const { count } = await supabase
    .from('notifications')
    .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
    .eq('user_id', userId)
    .eq('is_read', false);
  return count || 0;
}

export async function markAllRead(userId: string) {
  await supabase.from('notifications').update({ is_read: true }).eq('user_id', userId).eq('is_read', false);
}

export function subscribeToNotifications(userId: string, onChange: () => void) {
  const channel = supabase
    .channel(`notif_${userId}`)
    .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'notifications', filter: `user_id=eq.${userId}` },
      onChange)
    .subscribe();
  return () => supabase.removeChannel(channel);
}

import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';

export interface UserProfile {
  id: string;
  full_name: string;
  avatar_url?: string;
  bio?: string;
  university?: string;
  streak_days: number;
  total_reviews: number;
  total_materials: number;
  role?: string;
  onboarding_status?: Record<string, boolean>;
  is_pro?: boolean;
  pro_until?: string;
}

export interface PublicMaterial {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  subject: string;
  user_id: string;
  created_at: string;
  likes_count: number;
  author_name?: string;
  author_avatar?: string;
  is_liked_by_me?: boolean;
}

export interface LeaderboardEntry {
  id: string;
  full_name: string;
  avatar_url?: string;
  total_xp: number;
  level: number;
}

export async function getProfile(userId: string): Promise<UserProfile | null> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id', userId).single();
  if (error) { console.error('getProfile error:', error); return null; }
  return data;
}

export async function updateProfile(userId: string, updates: Partial<UserProfile>) {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('profiles').update(updates).eq('id', userId);
  if (error) console.error('updateProfile error:', error);
  return !error;
}

export async function markOnboardingTourSeen(userId: string, tourKey: string) {
  // We need to merge the existing JSONB. Wait, supabase update with JSONB replaces it unless we use rpc.
  // Actually, we can fetch, merge, and update.
  const p = await getProfile(userId);
  if (!p) return;
  const current = p.onboarding_status || {};
  if (current[tourKey]) return; // already seen

  const next = { ...current, [tourKey]: true };
  await updateProfile(userId, { onboarding_status: next });
}

export async function getLeaderboard(): Promise<LeaderboardEntry[]> {
  const { data: xpData, error: xpError } = await supabase
    .from('user_xp')
    .select('user_id, total_xp')
    .order('total_xp', { ascending: false })
    .limit(50);
  if (xpError || !xpData) return [];

  const userIds = xpData.map(x => x.user_id);
  const { data: profiles } = await supabase
    .from('profiles')
    .select('id, full_name, avatar_url')
    .in('id', userIds);

  const profileMap = new Map((profiles || []).map(p => [p.id, p]));

  return xpData.map(xp => {
    const p = profileMap.get(xp.user_id);
    return {
      id: xp.user_id,
      full_name: p?.full_name || 'Студент',
      avatar_url: p?.avatar_url,
      total_xp: xp.total_xp,
      level: 1,
    };
  });
}

export async function getPublicMaterials(currentUserId: string): Promise<PublicMaterial[]> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('materials')
    .select('id, title, subject, user_id, created_at, likes_count')
    .eq('is_public', true)
    .order('likes_count', { ascending: false })
    .limit(50);

  if (error || !data) return [];

  const { data: myLikes } = await supabase
    .from('material_likes')
    .select('material_id')
    .eq('user_id', currentUserId);
  const myLikedIds = new Set((myLikes || []).map(l => l.material_id));

  const authorIds = [...new Set(data.map(m => m.user_id))];
  const { data: profiles } = await supabase
    .from('profiles')
    .select('id, full_name, avatar_url')
    .in('id', authorIds);
  const profileMap = new Map((profiles || []).map(p => [p.id, p]));

  return data.map(m => {
    const author = profileMap.get(m.user_id);
    return {
      ...m,
      author_name: author?.full_name || 'Студент',
      author_avatar: author?.avatar_url,
      is_liked_by_me: myLikedIds.has(m.id)
    };
  });
}

// Атомарный лайк через RPC — без race condition
export async function toggleLike(userId: string, materialId: string, isCurrentlyLiked: boolean) {
  if (isCurrentlyLiked) {
    await supabase.from('material_likes').delete().match({ user_id: userId, material_id: materialId });
    await supabase.rpc('decrement_likes', { material_id: materialId });
  } else {
    const { error } = await supabase.from('material_likes').insert({ user_id: userId, material_id: materialId });
    if (!error) await supabase.rpc('increment_likes', { material_id: materialId });
  }
}

// Скопировать чужой публичный материал в свою библиотеку
export async function copyMaterialToLibrary(materialId: string, targetUserId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const { data: src, error: srcErr } = await supabase
    .from('materials')
    .select('title, subject, original_text, summary_json, flashcards_json, quiz_json, missions_json')
    .eq('id', materialId)
    .single();
  if (srcErr || !src) return false;

  const { error } = await supabase.from('materials').insert({
    user_id: targetUserId,
    title: src.title + ' (копия)',
    subject: src.subject,
    original_text: src.original_text,
    summary_json: src.summary_json,
    flashcards_json: src.flashcards_json,
    quiz_json: src.quiz_json,
    missions_json: src.missions_json,
    is_public: false,
    likes_count: 0,
  });
  return !error;
}

export async function getPublicMaterialPreview(materialId: string) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('materials')
    .select('summary_json, flashcards_json, quiz_json')
    .eq('id', materialId)
    .single();
    
  if (error) {
    console.error('getPublicMaterialPreview error:', error);
    return null;
  }
  return data;
}

export async function uploadAvatar(userId: string, file: File): Promise<string | null> {
  const fileExt = file.name.split('.').pop() || 'png';
  const fileName = `${userId}-${Date.now()}.${fileExt}`;
  
  const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
    .from('avatars')
    .upload(fileName, file, { cacheControl: '3600', upsert: true });

  if (uploadError) {
    console.error('Avatar upload error:', uploadError);
    return null;
  }

  const { data } = supabase.storage.from('avatars').getPublicUrl(fileName);
  return data.publicUrl;
}

  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<Notification[]>([]);
  const [usedTokens, setUsedTokens] = useState(0);
  const [isTokenModalOpen, setIsTokenModalOpen] = useState(false);
  
  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();
  const isDarkActive = theme === 'dark' || (theme === 'system' && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches);

  // Mobile menu state
  const [isMobileMenuOpen, setIsMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  // Close mobile menu on navigation
  useEffect(() => {
    setIsMobileMenuOpen(false);
  }, [location.pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;

    const fetchXP = () => getUserXP(user.id).then(v => { setXp(v); setLevelInfo(calcLevel(v)); });
    fetchXP();
    window.addEventListener('xp_updated', fetchXP);

    const fetchProfile = () => getProfile(user.id).then(p => { if (p) setProfile(p); });
    fetchProfile();
    window.addEventListener('profile_updated', fetchProfile);

    getUnreadCount(user.id).then(setUnreadCount);

    const unsub = subscribeToNotifications(user.id, () => {
      getUnreadCount(user.id).then(setUnreadCount);
    });

    const fetchTokens = () => checkAILimit(user.id, 0).then(res => setUsedTokens(res.count));
    fetchTokens();
    // It's possible to add event listener for 'ai_usage_updated' but for now fetching on mount is fine.

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('xp_updated', fetchXP);
      window.removeEventListener('profile_updated', fetchProfile);
      unsub();
    };
  }, [user?.id]);

  const handleOpenNotifications = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    setShowNotifications(v => !v);
    if (!showNotifications) {
      const notifs = await getNotifications(user.id);
      setNotifications(notifs);
      if (unreadCount > 0) {
        await markAllRead(user.id);
        setUnreadCount(0);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleToggleRole = async () => {
    if (!user || !profile) return;
    const newRole = profile.role === 'teacher' ? 'student' : 'teacher';
    const { supabase } = await import('../lib/supabase');
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .update({ role: newRole })
      .eq('id', user.id);
    if (!error) {
      setProfile({ ...profile, role: newRole });
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event('profile_updated'));
      if (newRole === 'teacher') navigate('/teacher');
      else navigate('/dashboard');
    }
  };

  const avatarUrl = profile?.avatar_url;
  const isTeacher = profile?.role === 'teacher';
  const currentNavGroups = isTeacher ? teacherNavGroups : navGroups;

  const notificationDropdown = showNotifications && (
    <div className="fixed inset-x-4 top-16 md:bottom-20 md:top-auto md:left-6 md:right-auto md:w-80 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 z-[70] overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[60vh]">
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between shrink-0 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100">Уведомления</h3>
        {notifications.length > 0 && (
          <button className="text-xs text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 font-medium hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300">Отметить все</button>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-2 space-y-1 custom-scrollbar">
        {notifications.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-6 text-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-sm">Нет новых уведомлений</div>
        ) : (
          notifications.map(n => (
            <div key={n.id} className="p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 transition cursor-pointer">
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 font-medium mb-1 line-clamp-2 break-words">{n.title}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 line-clamp-2 break-words">{n.body}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 mt-2">
                {new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
              </p>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 flex bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 overflow-hidden">
      <OnboardingManager />
      
      {/* Token Modal */}
      <TokenInfoModal 
        isOpen={isTokenModalOpen} 
        onClose={() => setIsTokenModalOpen(false)} 
        usedTokens={usedTokens} 
      />

      <ForgeyAssistant />
      {/* ═══ MOBILE OVERLAY ══════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      {isMobileMenuOpen && (
        <div 
          className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm z-40 md:hidden" 
          onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      {/* ═══ SIDEBAR ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      <aside className={`fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-50 w-72 md:w-64 bg-slate-900/95 dark:bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-xl flex flex-col shrink-0 transform transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out md:relative md:translate-x-0 ${isMobileMenuOpen ? 'translate-x-0 shadow-2xl' : '-translate-x-full'}`}>
        {/* Logo */}
        <div className="p-4 md:p-5 flex items-center justify-between w-full border-b border-slate-800">
          <button onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')} className="flex items-center gap-3 hover:opacity-80 transition text-left">
            <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded bg-indigo-500 flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
              <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
            </div>
            <span className="font-semibold tracking-tight text-white text-lg">StudyForge</span>
          </button>
          
          <button onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)} className="md:hidden p-2 -mr-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition">
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* XP / Level блок */}
        <div className="mx-4 mt-4 mb-4 p-3 bg-slate-800/50 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/50 tour-step-xp">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">Level {levelInfo.level}</p>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200">{levelInfo.name}</p>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate('/analytics')}
              className="text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-300 transition bg-slate-800 p-1.5 rounded-md"
              title="Аналитика"
            >
              <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="h-1.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-700/50 mb-1.5">
            <div
              className="h-full bg-indigo-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-700"
              style={{ width: `${levelInfo.progress}%` }}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-between mb-4">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-500">{xp} XP</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-500">{levelInfo.currentXP}/{levelInfo.nextXP}</span>
          </div>

          {/* AI Tokens Bar */}
          <div 
            onClick={() => setIsTokenModalOpen(true)}
            className="group cursor-pointer p-1.5 -mx-1.5 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-700/30 transition"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Zap className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${profile?.is_pro ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-slate-400 group-hover:text-amber-400 transition'}`} />
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 group-hover:text-slate-300 transition uppercase tracking-widest">Энергия ИИ</p>
              </div>
              {profile?.is_pro ? (
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-400 uppercase tracking-widest border border-amber-400/30 bg-amber-400/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(251,191,36,0.3)]">PRO</span>
              ) : null}
            </div>
            {!profile?.is_pro && (
              <>
                <div className="h-1.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-700/50">
                  <div
                    className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-700 ${usedTokens >= FREE_DAILY_LIMIT ? 'bg-rose-500' : 'bg-amber-400'}`}
                    style={{ width: `${Math.min((usedTokens / FREE_DAILY_LIMIT) * 100, 100)}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between mt-1.5">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-500">Потрачено</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-500">{usedTokens} / {FREE_DAILY_LIMIT}</span>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Navigation */}
        <nav className="flex-1 px-3 pb-6 space-y-5 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">
          {currentNavGroups.map((group: any, idx: number) => (
            <div key={idx}>
              <h3 className="px-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                {group.title}
              </h3>
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                {group.items.map((item: any) => (
                  <NavLink
                    key={item.to}
                    to={item.to}
                    className={({ isActive }) =>
                      `flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium rounded-lg transition-colors ${(item as any).className || ''} ` +
                      (isActive
                        ? 'bg-slate-800 text-white shadow-sm ring-1 ring-white/5'
                        : 'text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800/50 hover:text-slate-200')
                    }
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <item.icon className="w-4 h-4 opacity-80" />
                      {item.label}
                    </div>
                    {(item as any).isNew && (
                      <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-400 border border-indigo-500/20 uppercase tracking-widest">
                        New
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </NavLink>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </nav>

        {/* User section */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-slate-800">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button onClick={() => navigate('/profile')} className="shrink-0 relative">
              {avatarUrl ? (
                <img src={avatarUrl} alt="avatar" className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full object-cover ring-2 ${profile?.is_pro ? 'ring-amber-400 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(251,191,36,0.6)]' : 'ring-indigo-500/50'}`} />
              ) : (
                <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white text-sm font-bold ring-2 ${profile?.is_pro ? 'bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-400 to-amber-600 ring-amber-400 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(251,191,36,0.6)]' : 'bg-indigo-600 ring-indigo-500/50'}`}>
                  {initial}
                </div>
              )}
            </button>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="text-slate-300 text-xs font-medium truncate flex items-center gap-1">
                {name} 
              </p>
              <p className="text-slate-500 text-[10px] truncate">{email}</p>
            </div>
            
            <div className="relative hidden md:flex items-center gap-1">
              <button onClick={() => setTheme(isDarkActive ? 'light' : 'dark')} className="text-slate-500 hover:text-indigo-400 transition relative p-1" title="Переключить тему">
                {isDarkActive ? <Sun className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Moon className="w-4 h-4" />}
              </button>
              
              <button onClick={handleOpenNotifications} className="text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-200 transition relative p-1">
                <Bell className="w-4 h-4" />
                {unreadCount > 0 && (
                  <span className="absolute -top-0.5 -right-0.5 w-3.5 h-3.5 bg-rose-500 text-white text-[8px] font-black rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                    {unreadCount > 9 ? '9+' : unreadCount}
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>

            <button onClick={signOut} className="text-slate-500 hover:text-rose-400 transition p-1">
              <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* ═══ MAIN CONTENT ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 h-full relative">
        {/* ═══ MOBILE HEADER ════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
        <header className="md:hidden flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shrink-0 z-30">
          <button onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')} className="flex items-center gap-2 hover:opacity-80 transition text-left">
            <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-indigo-500 flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
              <GraduationCap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-white" />
            </div>
            <span className="font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 text-base">StudyForge</span>
          </button>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button 
              onClick={() => setIsTokenModalOpen(true)}
              className="mr-1 flex items-center hover:scale-105 transition"
            >
              {profile?.is_pro ? (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-500 bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-500/10 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/20 shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(251,191,36,0.2)]">
                  <Zap className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-black tracking-wider uppercase">PRO</span>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:border-amber-300 dark:hover:border-amber-700 transition">
                  <Zap className={`w-4 h-4 ${usedTokens >= FREE_DAILY_LIMIT ? 'text-rose-500' : 'text-amber-500'}`} />
                  <span className={`text-[11px] font-bold ${usedTokens >= FREE_DAILY_LIMIT ? 'text-rose-500' : ''}`}>{FREE_DAILY_LIMIT - usedTokens}</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => setTheme(isDarkActive ? 'light' : 'dark')} className="p-2 text-slate-500 hover:text-indigo-600 transition" title="Переключить тему">
              {isDarkActive ? <Sun className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Moon className="w-5 h-5" />}
            </button>
            <button onClick={handleOpenNotifications} className="p-2 text-slate-500 hover:text-indigo-600 transition relative">
              <Bell className="w-5 h-5" />
              {unreadCount > 0 && (
                <span className="absolute top-1 right-1 w-3.5 h-3.5 bg-rose-500 text-white text-[9px] font-black rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                  {unreadCount > 9 ? '9+' : unreadCount}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(true)} className="p-2 -mr-2 text-slate-500 hover:text-indigo-600 transition">
              <Menu className="w-6 h-6" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        <main className="flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto pb-[70px] md:pb-0 custom-scrollbar relative scroll-smooth">
          <ErrorBoundary>
            <Outlet />
          </ErrorBoundary>
        </main>

        {/* ═══ MOBILE BOTTOM NAV ════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
        <nav className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-30 bg-white/90 dark:bg-slate-900/90 backdrop-blur-md border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex md:hidden pb-safe">
          {mobileNavItems.map(item => (
            <NavLink
              key={item.to}
              to={item.to}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-2 min-h-[56px] gap-1 text-[10px] font-bold transition ${item.className || ''} ` +
                (isActive ? 'text-indigo-600' : 'text-slate-400')
              }
            >
              {({ isActive }) => (
                <>
                  <item.icon className={`w-5 h-5 mb-0.5 ${isActive ? 'text-indigo-600' : 'text-slate-400'}`} />
                  {item.label}
                </>
              )}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>
      </div>

      {/* Global Notification Dropdown */}
      {notificationDropdown}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { GraduationCap, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';

export default function Header({ onReset }: { onReset: () => void }) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'ok' | 'error' | 'checking'>('checking');

  useEffect(() => {
    const check = () => {
      fetch('/api/health')
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => setStatus(d.status === 'ok' ? 'ok' : 'error'))
        .catch(() => setStatus('error'));
    };
    check();
    const id = setInterval(check, 30000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  const dotClass = status === 'ok'
    ? 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400'
    : status === 'error'
    ? 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-rose-400 animate-pulse'
    : 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-600 animate-pulse';
  const dotTitle = status === 'ok' ? 'Бэкенд работает' : status === 'error' ? 'Бэкенд недоступен' : 'Проверка...';

  return (
    <header className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm sticky top-0 z-50">
      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 h-14 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 flex items-center justify-center">
            <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
          </div>
          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900 text-[15px]">StudyForge</span>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 text-xs font-medium">
            Статус системы
            <span className={dotClass} title={dotTitle} />
          </span>
        </div>
        <button onClick={onReset}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 transition text-sm">
          <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Заново
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/UploadZone.tsx

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { Upload, FileText, X, Zap, Youtube, Video, FileAudio, Layers, CheckCircle2, FileVideo, Music, Camera } from 'lucide-react';
import SettingsPanel from './SettingsPanel';
import { AnalysisSettings } from '../types';
import { hasBadWords } from '../utils/moderation';

interface Props {
  settings: AnalysisSettings;
  onSettingsChange: (s: AnalysisSettings) => void;
  onUpload: (f: File | null, youtubeUrl?: string) => void;
  onDemo: () => void;
}

export default function UploadZone({ settings, onSettingsChange, onUpload, onDemo }: Props) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'docs' | 'media'>('docs');
  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [youtubeUrl, setYoutubeUrl] = useState('');
  const [sizeError, setSizeError] = useState('');

  const MAX_SIZE = 50 * 1024 * 1024; // 50MB

  const validateAndSet = (f: File) => {
    if (f.size > MAX_SIZE) {
      setSizeError(`Файл слишком большой (${(f.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} МБ). Максимум — 50 МБ.`);
      setFile(null);
    } else if (hasBadWords(f.name)) {
      setSizeError('Название файла содержит недопустимые слова. Пожалуйста, переименуйте файл.');
      setFile(null);
    } else {
      setSizeError('');
      setFile(f);
      setYoutubeUrl('');
    }
  };

  const handleDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(false);
    const d = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
    if (d) validateAndSet(d);
  }, []);

  const handleStart = () => { 
    if (tab === 'docs' && file) onUpload(file); 
    if (tab === 'media' && (file || youtubeUrl.trim())) onUpload(file, youtubeUrl.trim() || undefined);
  };

  const isReady = tab === 'docs' ? !!file : (!!file || !!youtubeUrl.trim());

  return (
    <div className="py-10 space-y-8">
      {/* Hero */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} className="text-center">
        <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-3 leading-tight">
          Превратите любую лекцию<br />в готовый учебный материал
        </h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-lg mb-8">
          Три AI-агента параллельно создают конспект, карточки и тест — за 15 секунд
        </p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
            <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-500" />
            <span className="text-sm font-medium">Структурированный конспект</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
            <Layers className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-500" />
            <span className="text-sm font-medium">Карточки для запоминания</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-500" />
            <span className="text-sm font-medium">Проверочный тест</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Tabs */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.05 }}>
         <div className="flex bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900/50 p-1.5 rounded-2xl max-w-sm mx-auto">
            <button
              onClick={() => { setTab('docs'); setFile(null); setYoutubeUrl(''); }}
              className={`flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold transition ${tab === 'docs' ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400 shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-slate-200'}`}
            >
              📄 Документы
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => { setTab('media'); setFile(null); setYoutubeUrl(''); }}
              className={`flex-1 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold transition ${tab === 'media' ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400 shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-slate-200'}`}
            >
              ▶️ Видео / Аудио
            </button>
         </div>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Шаг 1: Загрузка файла */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.1 }}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <span className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-brand-600 dark:bg-brand-500 text-white text-sm flex items-center justify-center font-bold">1</span>
          <span className="font-medium text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
            {tab === 'docs' ? 'Выберите файл' : 'Вставьте ссылку или загрузите файл'}
          </span>
        </div>

        {tab === 'media' && !file && (
            <div className="mb-4">
                <div className="flex items-center bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-brand-500 transition">
                    <div className="pl-4 text-slate-400"><Youtube className="w-5 h-5"/></div>
                    <input 
                        type="text" 
                        placeholder="Ссылка на YouTube..." 
                        value={youtubeUrl}
                        onChange={e => setYoutubeUrl(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full py-3 px-3 outline-none text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 bg-transparent text-sm placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-600"
                    />
                    {youtubeUrl && (
                        <button onClick={() => setYoutubeUrl('')} className="pr-4 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-500 transition">
                            <X className="w-5 h-5"/>
                        </button>
                    )}
                </div>
                {!youtubeUrl && <div className="text-center text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-600 my-4 text-sm font-medium">ИЛИ</div>}
            </div>
        )}

        {(!file && (!youtubeUrl || tab === 'docs')) ? (
          <>
            <div
              onDragOver={e => { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); }}
              onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}
              onDrop={handleDrop}
              onClick={() => document.getElementById('fi')?.click()}
              className={'card p-14 text-center cursor-pointer transition border-2 border-dashed ' +
                (dragOver ? 'border-brand-400 bg-brand-50 dark:bg-brand-900/20' : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:border-brand-300 dark:hover:border-brand-500 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800')}
            >
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-brand-50 dark:bg-brand-900/30 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                {tab === 'docs' ? <Upload className="w-7 h-7 text-brand-500 dark:text-brand-400" /> : <Video className="w-7 h-7 text-brand-500 dark:text-brand-400" />}
              </div>
              <p className="text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 font-semibold text-lg mb-1">Перетащите файл сюда</p>
              <p className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">или нажмите для выбора</p>
              <p className="text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600 text-sm mt-3">
                {tab === 'docs' ? 'Поддерживаются PDF, DOCX, TXT, MD, JPG, PNG — до 50 МБ' : 'Поддерживаются MP4, MP3, WAV — до 50 МБ'}
              </p>
            </div>
            
            {tab === 'docs' && (
              <div className="mt-3 flex justify-center sm:hidden">
                <button
                  onClick={() => document.getElementById('camera-input')?.click()}
                  className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/50 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition border border-indigo-100 dark:border-indigo-800"
                >
                  <Camera className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-500 dark:text-indigo-400" /> Сфотографировать конспект
                </button>
                <input id="camera-input" type="file" accept="image/*" capture="environment" className="hidden" onChange={e => { if (e.target.files?.[0]) validateAndSet(e.target.files[0]); }} />
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        ) : file ? (
          <div className="card p-5 flex items-center gap-4">
            {tab === 'docs' ? <FileText className="w-10 h-10 text-brand-500 shrink-0" /> : <FileAudio className="w-10 h-10 text-brand-500 shrink-0" />}
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 font-medium truncate text-lg">{file.name}</p>
              <p className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 text-sm">{(file.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB</p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => setFile(null)}
              className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-300 transition">
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        <input id="fi" type="file" className="hidden" 
          accept={tab === 'docs' ? ".pdf,.docx,.txt,.md,.doc,.jpg,.jpeg,.png" : ".mp4,.mp3,.wav,.mpeg,.m4a"}
          onChange={e => { if (e.target.files?.[0]) validateAndSet(e.target.files[0]); }} />
        
        {sizeError && (
          <p className="mt-3 text-center text-sm text-rose-600">{sizeError}</p>
        )}
        
        {tab === 'docs' && (
          <div className="mt-3 text-center">
            <button onClick={onDemo}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-600 transition font-medium">
              ▶ Демо без файла
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </motion.div>

      {/* Шаг 2: Настройки */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.2 }}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <span className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-brand-600 dark:bg-brand-500 text-white text-sm flex items-center justify-center font-bold">2</span>
          <span className="font-medium text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">Настройте параметры</span>
        </div>
        <div className="card p-6">
          <SettingsPanel settings={settings} onChange={onSettingsChange} />
        </div>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Кнопка запуска */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} transition={{ delay: 0.3 }}>
        <button
          onClick={handleStart}
          disabled={!isReady}
          className={'w-full h-14 rounded-xl font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition text-base ' +
            (isReady
              ? 'bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 text-white cursor-pointer shadow-sm hover:shadow-md active:scale-[1.01]'
              : 'bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 cursor-not-allowed')}
        >
          <Zap className="w-5 h-5" />
          {isReady ? 'Запустить анализ' : 'Сначала выберите файл или вставьте ссылку'}
        </button>
      </motion.div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { Loader2, CheckCircle2, Clock, FileText, HelpCircle, BookOpen, Sparkles, Mic, Compass } from 'lucide-react';
import { AgentStatus } from '../types';

function UnifiedProgressBar({ value }: { value: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-xl mx-auto">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
        <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">Общий прогресс анализа</span>
        <span className="text-sm font-black text-indigo-600 tabular-nums">{value}%</span>
      </div>
      <div className="h-3 w-full bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden p-[2px] border border-slate-200 shadow-inner">
        <motion.div
          className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 via-violet-500 to-emerald-500 rounded-full shadow-sm"
          initial={{ width: '0%' }}
          animate={{ width: `${value}%` }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: 'easeOut' }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AgentTimer({ status }: { status: string }) {
  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = useState(0);
  const startRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const frameRef = useRef<number>(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'running') {
      startRef.current = Date.now();
      const tick = () => {
        if (startRef.current) setElapsed((Date.now() - startRef.current) / 1000);
        frameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      };
      frameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frameRef.current);
    } else {
      cancelAnimationFrame(frameRef.current);
    }
  }, [status]);

  if (status === 'idle') return null;
  return status === 'done'
    ? <span className="text-xs text-emerald-600 font-bold tabular-nums">{elapsed.toFixed(1)}с</span>
    : <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold tabular-nums">{elapsed.toFixed(1)}с</span>;
}

          const IconComponent = config.icon;

          return (

              key={agent.id}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}

              transition={{ delay: i * 0.08 }}
              className={`p-5 rounded-2xl border bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between transition-all duration-300 shadow-sm ${config.glowColor} ${
                agent.status === 'running'
                  ? `border-indigo-300 dark:border-indigo-500 ring-4 ring-indigo-50 dark:ring-indigo-950/30`
                  : agent.status === 'done'
                  ? `border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 bg-emerald-50/30 dark:bg-emerald-950/10`
                  : `border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 opacity-80`
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 mb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border ${config.borderColor} ${config.bgColor} ${config.color} shadow-sm`}>
                    <IconComponent className="w-5 h-5 animate-pulse-slow" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm">{config.label}</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5 min-h-[16px]">

                  )}
                  {agent.status === 'done' && (
                     <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500 shrink-0" />
                  )}
                  {agent.status === 'idle' && (
                    <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                  )}
                  <AgentTimer status={agent.status} />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Индикатор прогресса конкретного агента */}
              <div className="w-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 h-1.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden mt-3 shadow-inner">
                {agent.status === 'idle' && (
                  <div className="h-full bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 w-0" />
                )}
                {agent.status === 'running' && (
                  <motion.div
                    className={`h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-violet-500 rounded-full`}
                    initial={{ width: '0%' }}

                  />
                )}

                    className="h-full bg-emerald-500 rounded-full shadow-sm"
                    initial={{ width: '90%' }}
                    animate={{ width: '100%' }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.3 }}
                  />
                )}
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Copy, Download, RotateCcw, FileText, Layers, HelpCircle, Hash, Clock, Cpu, Gamepad2, Mic, Smartphone, Headphones, Play } from 'lucide-react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AnalysisResults, QuizQuestion, Flashcard } from '../types';
import SummaryView from './SummaryView';
import FlashcardsView from './FlashcardsView';
import QuizView from './QuizView';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

type Tab = 'summary' | 'flashcards' | 'quiz' | 'missions' | 'podcast' | 'shorts';

function extractCleanContent(data: any): string {
  let raw = data?.content || (data as any)?.raw || '';
  if (typeof raw === 'string' && raw.trimStart().startsWith('{')) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (parsed?.content) raw = parsed.content;
    } catch {
      const match = raw.match(/"content"\s*:\s*"([\s\S]*?)(?:"\s*[,}])/);
      if (match) raw = match[1].replace(/\\n/g, '\n').replace(/\\"/g, '"');
    }
  }
  if (!raw && typeof data === 'string') {
    raw = data;
    if (raw.trimStart().startsWith('{')) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (parsed?.content) raw = parsed.content;
      } catch {}
    }
  }
  const jsonTailIdx = raw.search(/\s*[,"]?\s*"terms"\s*:/);
  if (jsonTailIdx > 0) raw = raw.slice(0, jsonTailIdx);
  return raw.trim();
}

function parseMarkdownToHtml(md: string): string {
  if (!md) return '';
  let html = md.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
  
  // Headers
  html = html.replace(/^### (.*$)/gim, '<h3>$1</h3>');
  html = html.replace(/^## (.*$)/gim, '<h2>$1</h2>');
  html = html.replace(/^# (.*$)/gim, '<h1>$1</h1>');
  
  // Bold
  html = html.replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
  
  // Italic
  html = html.replace(/\*(.*?)\*/g, '<em>$1</em>');
  
  // Bullet lists & numbered lists line-by-line
  let lines = html.split('\n');
  let inList = false;
  let inOrderedList = false;
  
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    let line = lines[i].trim();
    if (line.startsWith('* ') || line.startsWith('- ')) {
      let content = line.substring(2);
      if (!inList) {
        lines[i] = '<ul>\n<li>' + content + '</li>';
        inList = true;
      } else {
        lines[i] = '<li>' + content + '</li>';
      }
    } else if (/^\d+\.\s/.test(line)) {
      let content = line.replace(/^\d+\.\s/, '');
      if (!inOrderedList) {
        lines[i] = '<ol>\n<li>' + content + '</li>';
        inOrderedList = true;
      } else {
        lines[i] = '<li>' + content + '</li>';
      }
    } else {
      let extra = '';
      if (inList) {
        extra += '</ul>\n';
        inList = false;
      }
      if (inOrderedList) {
        extra += '</ol>\n';
        inOrderedList = false;
      }
      
      if (line !== '' && !line.startsWith('<h') && !line.startsWith('<ul') && !line.startsWith('<ol') && !line.startsWith('<li')) {
        lines[i] = extra + '<p>' + line + '</p>';
      } else {
        lines[i] = extra + lines[i];
      }
    }
  }
  
  html = lines.join('\n');
  if (inList) html += '\n</ul>';
  if (inOrderedList) html += '\n</ol>';
  
  return html;
}

function buildHtmlExport(results: AnalysisResults, sourceFileName?: string): string {
  const date = new Date().toLocaleDateString('ru-RU', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
  const time = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ru-RU', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  
  const cleanSummary = extractCleanContent(results.summarizer);
  const summaryHtml = parseMarkdownToHtml(cleanSummary) || '<p style="color:#94a3b8">Нет данных</p>';
  
  const flashcards = results.flashcards?.flashcards || [];
  const quiz = results.quiz?.quiz || [];

  const flashcardsHtml = flashcards.map((c: Flashcard, i: number) =>
    `<div class="card">
      <p class="card-q">Карточка ${i+1}: ${c.question}</p>
      <p class="card-a">${c.answer}</p>
    </div>`
  ).join('\n');

  const quizHtml = quiz.map((q: QuizQuestion, i: number) => {
    const options = (q.options || []).map((o: string) => {
      const isCorrect = o.startsWith(q.correct);
      return `<li class="${isCorrect ? 'correct' : ''}">${o}${isCorrect ? ' ✓' : ''}</li>`;
    }).join('\n');
    return `<div class="card">
      <p class="card-q">${i+1}. ${q.question}</p>
      <ul class="opt-list">${options}</ul>
      ${q.explanation ? `<p class="explanation">💡 ${q.explanation}</p>` : ''}
    </div>`;
  }).join('\n');

  return `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ru">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>StudyForge — ${sourceFileName || 'Анализ материала'}</title>
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<style>
  * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
  body {
    font-family: 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif;
    color: #1e293b;
    line-height: 1.7;
    max-width: 760px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    padding: 48px 32px 64px;
    background: #fff;
    font-size: 15px;
  }
  /* Header */
  .doc-header {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 12px;
    padding-bottom: 20px;

  </div>

${flashcardsHtml}
</div>` : ''}

${quiz.length > 0 ? `<div class="section">

${quizHtml}
</div>` : ''}

</html>`;
}

export default function ResultsPanel({ results, onReset, textLength, analysisDuration, sourceFileName, materialId }: {
  results: AnalysisResults; onReset: () => void; textLength?: number; analysisDuration?: number; sourceFileName?: string; materialId?: string;
}) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('summary');
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const [showExport, setShowExport] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!(e.target as Element).closest('.export-menu-container')) {
        setShowExport(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const flashcardCount = results.flashcards?.flashcards?.length || 0;
  const quizCount = results.quiz?.quiz?.length || 0;

  const tabs: { id: Tab; label: string; icon: JSX.Element; count?: number }[] = [
    { id: 'summary', label: 'Конспект', icon: <FileText className="w-4 h-4" /> },
    { id: 'flashcards', label: 'Карточки', icon: <Layers className="w-4 h-4" />, count: flashcardCount },
    { id: 'quiz', label: 'Тест', icon: <HelpCircle className="w-4 h-4" />, count: quizCount },
    { id: 'missions', label: 'Миссии', icon: <Gamepad2 className="w-4 h-4" />, count: results.missions?.missions?.length || 0 },
    { id: 'podcast', label: 'Подкаст', icon: <Mic className="w-4 h-4" /> },
    { id: 'shorts', label: 'ТикТок', icon: <Smartphone className="w-4 h-4" /> },
  ];

  const getContent = () => {
    if (tab === 'summary') return results.summarizer?.content || (results.summarizer as any)?.raw || '';
    if (tab === 'flashcards') return JSON.stringify(results.flashcards?.flashcards || results.flashcards, null, 2);
    if (tab === 'missions') return JSON.stringify(results.missions?.missions || results.missions, null, 2);
    return JSON.stringify(results.quiz?.quiz || results.quiz, null, 2);
  };

  const handleCopy = () => { navigator.clipboard.writeText(getContent()); setCopied(true); setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000); };

  const handleDownloadHtml = () => {
    const baseName = (sourceFileName || 'demo').replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '');
    const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const html = buildHtmlExport(results, sourceFileName);
    const b = new Blob([html], { type: 'text/html' });
    const u = URL.createObjectURL(b);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = u;
    a.download = `studyforge_${baseName}_${dateStr}.html`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(u);
  };

  const handleExportAnki = () => {
    try {
      const cards = results.flashcards?.flashcards || results.flashcards || [];
      const tsv = cards.map((c: any) => `${c.question.replace(/\n/g, ' ')}\t${c.answer.replace(/\n/g, '<br>')}`).join('\n');
      
      const b = new Blob([tsv], { type: 'text/tab-separated-values;charset=utf-8' });
      const u = URL.createObjectURL(b);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = u;
      const baseName = (sourceFileName || 'deck').replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '');
      a.download = `studyforge_${baseName}.txt`;
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(u);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      toast.error('Ошибка при экспорте Anki');
    }
  };

  const handlePrintPdf = () => {
    // Build a standalone printable HTML page in isolation so that none of the
    // app's UI (sidebar, header, audio players, etc.) leaks into the PDF.
    const html = buildHtmlExport(results, sourceFileName);
    const win = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=900,height=700');
    if (!win) {
      toast.error('Разрешите открытие всплывающих окон в браузере для печати.');
      return;
    }
    win.document.write(html);
    win.document.close();
    // Small delay to allow fonts/styles to load before printing
    setTimeout(() => {
      win.focus();
      win.print();
    }, 600);
  };

  return (
    <div className="py-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-6 no-print">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Результаты</h2>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          
          {/* Export Dropdown */}
          <div className="relative export-menu-container">
            <button 
              onClick={() => setShowExport(!showExport)}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 sm:py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition shadow-sm"
            >
              <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">Экспорт</span>
            </button>

            <AnimatePresence>
              {showExport && (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10, scale: 0.95 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
                  exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 10, scale: 0.95 }}
                  transition={{ duration: 0.15 }}
                  className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-56 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-xl shadow-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-1.5 z-50 flex flex-col gap-1"
                >
                  <button onClick={() => { handleCopy(); setShowExport(false); }}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition w-full text-left">
                    <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />{copied ? 'Скопировано' : 'Копировать'}
                  </button>
                  
                  {(tab === 'summary' || tab === 'flashcards' || tab === 'quiz') && (
                    <button onClick={() => { handlePrintPdf(); setShowExport(false); }}
                      className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition w-full text-left">
                      <Download className="w-4 h-4" />В PDF / Печать
                    </button>
                  )}
                  
                  {tab === 'flashcards' && (
                    <button onClick={() => { handleExportAnki(); setShowExport(false); }}
                      title="Anki — это популярная бесплатная программа..."
                      className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:bg-blue-50 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/30 transition w-full text-left">
                      <Download className="w-4 h-4" />В Anki (.txt)
                    </button>
                  )}
                  
                  {(tab === 'summary' || tab === 'flashcards' || tab === 'quiz') && (
                    <button onClick={() => { handleDownloadHtml(); setShowExport(false); }}
                      className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition w-full text-left">
                      <Download className="w-4 h-4" />Скачать HTML
                    </button>
                  )}
                </motion.div>
              )}
            </AnimatePresence>
          </div>

          <button onClick={onReset}
            className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold text-white bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 transition shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap">
            <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" />Новый анализ
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Статистика */}
      <div className="flex overflow-x-auto pb-4 gap-3 mb-2 snap-x -mx-4 px-4 md:mx-0 md:px-0 md:grid md:grid-cols-3 md:pb-0 hide-scrollbar no-print">
        <div className="card p-3 flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0 snap-start min-w-[160px] md:min-w-0">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 dark:bg-brand-900/30 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Hash className="w-4 h-4 text-brand-500 dark:text-brand-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">Символов</p>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">{textLength?.toLocaleString() || '—'}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">{textLength ? `~${Math.round(textLength / 5)} слов` : '—'}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="card p-3 flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0 snap-start min-w-[160px] md:min-w-0">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500 dark:text-emerald-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">Время чтения</p>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
              {textLength ? `~${Math.max(1, Math.round(textLength / 800))} мин` : (analysisDuration ? `${analysisDuration} сек` : '—')}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="card p-3 flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0 snap-start min-w-[160px] md:min-w-0">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-900/30 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Cpu className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 dark:text-amber-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">Агенты</p>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">3 параллельно</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Pill-вкладки */}
      <div className="flex overflow-x-auto gap-1.5 mb-6 bg-slate-100/80 dark:bg-slate-900/80 p-1.5 rounded-xl w-full md:w-fit snap-x -mx-4 px-4 md:mx-0 md:px-0 hide-scrollbar no-print">
        {tabs.map(t => (
          <button key={t.id} onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
            className={'flex shrink-0 snap-start items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm transition font-bold ' +
              (tab === t.id
                ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-md'
                : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-200 hover:bg-white/50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/50')}>
            {t.icon}
            {t.label}
            {t.count !== undefined && (
              <span className={'ml-1 text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md ' + (tab === t.id ? 'bg-indigo-500 text-white' : 'bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400')}>
                {t.count}
              </span>
            )}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <motion.div key={tab} initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} className="min-h-[300px] printable-section">
        {tab === 'summary' && <div className="card p-6"><SummaryView data={results.summarizer} /></div>}
        {tab === 'flashcards' && <FlashcardsView data={results.flashcards} />}
        {tab === 'quiz' && <QuizView data={results.quiz} />}
        {tab === 'missions' && (
          <div className="card p-10 flex flex-col items-center text-center justify-center min-h-[300px]">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
              <Gamepad2 className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2">Сюжетные миссии готовы!</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-6 max-w-sm">
              ИИ создал для вас персональный мини-квест на основе этого материала. Сыграем?
            </p>
            <Link to={materialId ? `/play?materialId=${materialId}` : '/play'} className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-lg font-medium transition shadow-sm inline-flex items-center gap-2">
              <Gamepad2 className="w-5 h-5" />
              Начать игру
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}
        {tab === 'podcast' && (
          <div className="card p-10 flex flex-col items-center text-center justify-center min-h-[300px]">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-purple-100 dark:bg-purple-900/30 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
              <Mic className="w-8 h-8 text-purple-600 dark:text-purple-400" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2">Аудио-подкаст готов</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-6 max-w-sm">
              ИИ сгенерировал увлекательный диалог ведущих по этой теме. Слушайте его в библиотеке.
            </p>
            <Link to={materialId ? `/library/${materialId}` : '/library'} className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 text-white rounded-lg font-medium transition shadow-sm inline-flex items-center gap-2">
              <Headphones className="w-5 h-5" />
              Слушать в библиотеке
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}
        {tab === 'shorts' && (
          <div className="card p-10 flex flex-col items-center text-center justify-center min-h-[300px]">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-900/30 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
              <Smartphone className="w-8 h-8 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2">Учебный TikTok</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-6 max-w-sm">
              Изучайте ключевые концепции через короткие вертикальные видео с озвучкой.
            </p>
            {materialId ? (
              <Link to={`/shorts/${materialId}`} className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white rounded-lg font-medium transition shadow-sm inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                <Play className="w-5 h-5" />
                Смотреть Shorts
              </Link>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-sm text-amber-600">Сначала сохраните материал в библиотеку, чтобы смотреть Shorts</p>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </motion.div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/SummaryView.tsx
