import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkMath from 'remark-math';
import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex';
import 'katex/dist/katex.min.css';
import { SummaryInfo } from '../types';

/** Извлекает чистый текст конспекта, защищаясь от сырого JSON в content */
function extractCleanContent(data: any): string {
  // Нормальный случай: объект с полем content
  let raw = data?.content || (data as any)?.raw || '';

  // Если content сам является строкой с JSON — пробуем распарсить
  if (typeof raw === 'string' && raw.trimStart().startsWith('{')) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (parsed?.content) raw = parsed.content;
    } catch {
      // Попробуем вырезать content из raw вручную
      const match = raw.match(/"content"\s*:\s*"([\s\S]*?)(?:"\s*[,}])/);
      if (match) raw = match[1].replace(/\\n/g, '\n').replace(/\\"/g, '"');
    }
  }

  // Если data сам — строка (старые записи)
  if (!raw && typeof data === 'string') {
    raw = data;
    if (raw.trimStart().startsWith('{')) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (parsed?.content) raw = parsed.content;
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  // Обрезаем JSON-хвост с terms/терминами, если он приклеился к концу
  const jsonTailIdx = raw.search(/\s*[,"]?\s*"terms"\s*:/);
  if (jsonTailIdx > 0) raw = raw.slice(0, jsonTailIdx);

  return raw.trim();
}

function extractTerms(data: any): { term: string; definition: string }[] {
  return data?.terms || data?.summary_json?.terms || [];
}

function SectionBlock({ icon, title, children }: { icon: string; title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
        <span className="text-lg">{icon}</span>
        <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">{title}</h3>
      </div>
      <div className="pl-7 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-[15px] leading-relaxed">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function SummaryView({ data }: { data: SummaryInfo }) {
  const content = extractCleanContent(data);
  const terms = extractTerms(data);

  if (!content && terms.length === 0) {
    return <p className="text-slate-400">Нет данных для отображения</p>;
  }

  // Разбиваем по заголовкам уровня 2 (## Название)
  const sections = content ? content.split(/(?=^## )/gm).filter(Boolean) : [];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-1">
      {sections.map((sec: string, i: number) => {
        const match = sec.match(/^## (.+)/);
        if (!match) {
          // Первый блок без заголовка — обычный абзац
          return (
            <div key={i} className="prose prose-slate dark:prose-invert max-w-none text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-4">
              <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkMath]} rehypePlugins={[rehypeKatex]}>{sec.trim()}</ReactMarkdown>
            </div>
          );
        }
        const title = match[1].trim();
        const body = sec.replace(/^## .+\n?/, '').trim();
        const icon =
          title.includes('📌') ? '📌' :
          title.includes('🔑') ? '🔑' :
          title.includes('📖') ? '📖' :
          title.includes('💡') ? '💡' :
          title.includes('Вывод') ? '🏁' :
          title.includes('Ключев') ? '🔑' :
          title.includes('Структур') ? '🗂️' :
          '📄';
        const cleanTitle = title.replace(/[📌🔑📖📚💡]/g, '').trim();

        return (
          <SectionBlock key={i} icon={icon} title={cleanTitle}>
            <div className="prose prose-slate dark:prose-invert prose-sm max-w-none">
              <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkMath]} rehypePlugins={[rehypeKatex]}>{body}</ReactMarkdown>
            </div>
          </SectionBlock>
        );
      })}

      {/* Словарь терминов */}
      {terms.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-6 pt-5 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
            <span className="text-lg">📚</span>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Словарь терминов</h3>
            <span className="ml-1 text-xs bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
              {terms.length}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            {terms.map((t: any, i: number) => (
              <div key={i} className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/40 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/60 rounded-xl p-3">
                <p className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 text-sm mb-0.5">{t.term}</p>
                <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-xs leading-relaxed">{t.definition}</p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/FlashcardsView.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { FlashcardsInfo, Flashcard } from '../types/index';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Eye, EyeOff, GraduationCap, ArrowLeft, Check, X, Copy } from 'lucide-react';

export default function FlashcardsView({ data }: { data: FlashcardsInfo }) {
  const cards = data?.flashcards;

  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
  const [studyMode, setStudyMode] = useState(false);
  const [studyIdx, setStudyIdx] = useState(0);
  const [studyRevealed, setStudyRevealed] = useState(false);
  const [studyResults, setStudyResults] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
  const [studyDone, setStudyDone] = useState(false);
  const [copiedIdx, setCopiedIdx] = useState(-1);

  const toggle = (i: number) => setRevealed({ ...revealed, [i]: !revealed[i] });
  const revealAll = () => {
    if (!cards || !Array.isArray(cards)) return;
    const r: Record<number, boolean> = {};
    cards.forEach((_: Flashcard, i: number) => { r[i] = true; });
    setRevealed(r);
  };
  const hideAll = () => setRevealed({});

  const startStudy = () => {
    setStudyMode(true);
    setStudyIdx(0);
    setStudyRevealed(false);
    setStudyResults({});
    setStudyDone(false);
  };

  const exitStudy = () => setStudyMode(false);

  const markCard = (known: boolean) => {
    if (!cards) return;
    const next = { ...studyResults, [studyIdx]: known };
    if (studyIdx + 1 < cards.length) {
      setStudyResults(next);
      setStudyIdx(studyIdx + 1);
      setStudyRevealed(false);
    } else {
      setStudyResults(next);
      setStudyDone(true);
    }
  };

  if (!cards || !Array.isArray(cards)) return <p className="text-slate-400">Нет данных</p>;

  // Study mode
  if (studyMode) {
    const knownCount = Object.values(studyResults).filter(Boolean).length;

    if (studyDone) {
      return (
        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          className="card p-8 text-center">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/20 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 border border-emerald-100 dark:border-emerald-900/30">
            <GraduationCap className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-500 dark:text-emerald-400" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2">
            Результат: {knownCount} из {cards.length}
          </h3>
          <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-6">
            {knownCount === cards.length ? 'Отлично! Вы знаете все карточки!' :
             knownCount >= cards.length / 2 ? 'Неплохо, но есть что подтянуть.' :
             'Стоит повторить материал ещё раз.'}
          </p>
          <div className="flex justify-center gap-3">
            <button onClick={startStudy}
              className="px-5 py-2 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
              Пройти снова
            </button>
            <button onClick={exitStudy}
              className="px-5 py-2 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
              К списку
            </button>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      );
    }

    const card = cards[studyIdx];
    return (
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <button onClick={exitStudy}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-slate-200 transition">
            <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />К списку
          </button>
          <span className="text-sm text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-505">{studyIdx + 1} / {cards.length}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="card p-8 min-h-[260px] flex flex-col justify-center">
          <p className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 text-center mb-6">{card.question}</p>
          <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
            {studyRevealed ? (
              <motion.p key="answer" initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-center border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 pt-4">
                {card.answer}
              </motion.p>
            ) : (
              <motion.button key="reveal" initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                onClick={() => setStudyRevealed(true)}
                className="mx-auto px-5 py-2 text-sm text-brand-600 dark:text-indigo-400 border border-brand-200 dark:border-indigo-900/40 rounded-lg hover:bg-brand-50 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950/30 transition">
                Показать ответ
              </motion.button>
            )}
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>

        {studyRevealed && (
          <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            className="flex gap-3 mt-4">
            <button onClick={() => markCard(true)}
              className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/20 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-medium hover:bg-emerald-100 dark:hover:bg-emerald-950/40 transition">
              <Check className="w-5 h-5" />Знаю
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => markCard(false)}
              className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/20 border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-900/30 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 font-medium hover:bg-rose-100 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/40 transition">
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />Ещё раз
            </button>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </motion.div>
    );
  }

  // Normal mode
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
        <button onClick={revealAll} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 transition border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700">
          <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Показать все
        </button>
        <button onClick={hideAll} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 transition border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700">
          <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Скрыть все
        </button>
        <button onClick={startStudy}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-brand-600 dark:text-indigo-400 bg-brand-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/20 hover:bg-brand-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950/40 transition border border-brand-200 dark:border-indigo-900/30">
          <GraduationCap className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Начать обучение
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {cards.map((c: Flashcard, i: number) => (
          <motion.div key={i} initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} transition={{ delay: i * 0.05 }}
            className="card p-4 cursor-pointer hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-700 transition group"
            onClick={() => toggle(i)}>
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
              <p className="font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2">Q{i+1}: {c.question}</p>
              <button onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); navigator.clipboard.writeText(`Q: ${c.question}\nA: ${c.answer}`); setCopiedIdx(i); setTimeout(() => setCopiedIdx(-1), 1500); }}
                className="shrink-0 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition p-1 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-500 dark:hover:text-slate-400">
                {copiedIdx === i ? <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-500" /> : <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
              </button>
            </div>
            {revealed[i] ? (
              <p className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-sm border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/80 pt-2">{c.answer}</p>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 text-sm border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/80 pt-2 italic">Нажмите, чтобы увидеть ответ</p>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/QuizView.tsx
================================================================================
import { QuizQuestion, QuizInfo } from '../types';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { CheckCircle2, XCircle, RotateCcw, Maximize2, Minimize2 } from 'lucide-react';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';

export default function QuizView({ data }: { data: any }) {
  const quiz = data?.quiz;

  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  // Fullscreen mode state (separate from normal)
  const [fsOpen, setFsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [fsIdx, setFsIdx] = useState(0);
  const [fsAnswers, setFsAnswers] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [fsSubmitted, setFsSubmitted] = useState(false);

  const handleSelect = (qi: number, option: string) => {
    if (submitted) return;
    setAnswers({ ...answers, [qi]: option });
  };

  const handleSubmit = () => {
    setSubmitted(true);
    const score = quiz.filter((q: QuizQuestion, i: number) => answers[i]?.startsWith(q.correct)).length;
    if (score === quiz.length) {
      confetti({ particleCount: 120, spread: 80, origin: { y: 0.6 }, colors: ['#4F46E5', '#10b981', '#f59e0b'] });
    }
  };
  const handleRetry = () => { setAnswers({}); setSubmitted(false); };

  if (!quiz || !Array.isArray(quiz)) {
    return (
      <div className="card p-6 bg-slate-50 border-slate-200">
        <p className="text-slate-500 mb-2 font-medium">Тест не удалось сгенерировать корректно</p>
        {data?.error && <p className="text-rose-500 text-xs mb-3">Ошибка: {data.error}</p>}
        {data?.raw && (
          <details className="text-xs">
            <summary className="text-indigo-600 cursor-pointer hover:underline mb-2">Показать сырой ответ AI</summary>
            <pre className="p-3 bg-slate-900 text-slate-300 rounded overflow-x-auto whitespace-pre-wrap max-h-64">
              {data.raw}
            </pre>
          </details>
        )}
        {!data?.raw && !data?.error && <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm">Нет данных для отображения</p>}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const correct = quiz.filter((q: QuizQuestion, i: number) => answers[i]?.startsWith(q.correct)).length;
  const total = quiz.length;
  const pct = Math.round((correct / total) * 100);

  const gradeInfo = pct >= 90 ? { label: 'Отлично', color: 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400', bg: 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/20 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-900/30' }
    : pct >= 70 ? { label: 'Хорошо', color: 'text-brand-600 dark:text-indigo-400', bg: 'bg-brand-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/20 border-brand-200 dark:border-indigo-900/30' }
    : pct >= 50 ? { label: 'Удовлетворительно', color: 'text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400', bg: 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/20 border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-900/30' }
    : { label: 'Нужно подтянуть', color: 'text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400', bg: 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/20 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-900/30' };

  // Fullscreen mode
  if (fsOpen && quiz) {
    const fsQ = quiz[fsIdx];
    const fsTotal = quiz.length;
    const fsCorrect = quiz.filter((q: QuizQuestion, i: number) => fsAnswers[i]?.startsWith(q.correct)).length;

    const fsSelect = (opt: string) => {
      if (fsSubmitted) return;
      setFsAnswers({ ...fsAnswers, [fsIdx]: opt });
    };

    const fsNext = () => {
      if (fsIdx + 1 < fsTotal) setFsIdx(fsIdx + 1);
      else setFsSubmitted(true);
    };

    const fsExit = () => { setFsOpen(false); setFsIdx(0); setFsAnswers({}); setFsSubmitted(false); };

    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900 overflow-y-auto">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-8 py-4 border-b border-slate-700">
          <span className="text-white font-semibold text-lg">StudyForge — Тест для аудитории</span>
          <button onClick={fsExit} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-white/70 hover:text-white transition text-sm">
            <Minimize2 className="w-4 h-4" />Выйти
          </button>
        </div>

        {!fsSubmitted ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[calc(100vh-64px)] px-8 py-12">
            <p className="text-slate-400 text-2xl mb-6">Вопрос {fsIdx + 1} / {fsTotal}</p>
            <p className="text-white text-3xl font-bold text-center max-w-3xl mx-auto mb-12">{fsQ.question}</p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 max-w-3xl w-full">
              {fsQ.options?.map((opt: string, j: number) => {
                const selected = fsAnswers[fsIdx] === opt;
                return (
                  <div key={j} onClick={() => fsSelect(opt)}
                    className={'rounded-2xl p-6 text-xl cursor-pointer transition border ' +
                      (selected ? 'bg-brand-600 border-brand-500 text-white' : 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-white hover:bg-slate-700 hover:border-brand-500')}>
                    {opt}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            {fsAnswers[fsIdx] && (
              <motion.button initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                onClick={fsNext}
                className="mt-10 px-8 py-3 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 text-white rounded-xl text-lg font-medium transition">
                {fsIdx + 1 < fsTotal ? 'Следующий вопрос →' : 'Завершить тест'}
              </motion.button>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center px-8 py-12">
            <p className="text-4xl font-bold text-white mb-2">Правильных ответов: {fsCorrect} из {fsTotal}</p>
            <p className="text-slate-400 text-lg mb-10">{Math.round((fsCorrect / fsTotal) * 100)}%</p>
            <div className="max-w-3xl w-full space-y-4">
              {quiz.map((q: QuizQuestion, i: number) => {
                const isCorrect = fsAnswers[i]?.startsWith(q.correct);
                return (
                  <div key={i} className={'rounded-xl p-5 border ' + (isCorrect ? 'bg-emerald-900/30 border-emerald-700' : 'bg-rose-900/20 border-rose-800')}>
                    <p className="text-white font-medium mb-2">{i + 1}. {q.question}</p>
                    <p className={isCorrect ? 'text-emerald-400 text-sm' : 'text-rose-400 text-sm'}>
                      {isCorrect ? '✓ Верно' : `✗ Правильный ответ: ${q.correct}) ${q.options?.find((o: string) => o.startsWith(q.correct))?.replace(/^[A-D]\)\s*/, '') || q.correct}`}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <button onClick={fsExit}
              className="mt-8 px-6 py-2.5 bg-white text-slate-900 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-slate-100 transition">
              Закрыть
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="relative">
      {!submitted && (
        <button onClick={() => setFsOpen(true)}
          className="absolute top-0 right-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-brand-600 dark:text-indigo-400 border border-brand-200 dark:border-indigo-900/30 hover:bg-brand-50 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950/20 transition">
          <Maximize2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Показать аудитории
        </button>
      )}

      {submitted && (
        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          className={'card p-5 mb-6 flex items-center justify-between border ' + gradeInfo.bg}>
          <div>
            <p className={'font-bold text-lg ' + gradeInfo.color}>{correct} из {total} — {gradeInfo.label}</p>
            <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-sm">{pct}% правильных ответов</p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={handleRetry}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700">
            <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Пройти снова
          </button>
        </motion.div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-4">
        {quiz.map((q: QuizQuestion, i: number) => {
          const selected = answers[i];
          const isCorrect = selected?.startsWith(q.correct);
          const isWrong = submitted && selected && !isCorrect;

          return (
            <motion.div key={i} initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: i * 0.05 }}
              className="card p-5">
              <p className="font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-3">{i+1}. {q.question}</p>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {q.options?.map((opt: string, j: number) => {
                  const isSelected = selected === opt;
                  const isCorrectOpt = submitted && opt.startsWith(q.correct);
                  const isWrongOpt = submitted && isSelected && !isCorrect;

                  let cls = 'p-3 rounded-lg text-sm cursor-pointer transition border ';
                  if (!submitted) {
                    cls += isSelected ? 'bg-brand-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/30 border-brand-300 dark:border-indigo-800 text-slate-900 dark:text-indigo-200' : 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/50 border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/60 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-700';
                  } else if (isCorrectOpt) {
                    cls += 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 border-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300';
                  } else if (isWrongOpt) {
                    cls += 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/40 border-rose-300 dark:border-rose-900/50 text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-300 line-through';
                  } else {
                    cls += 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/50 border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/60 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-550';
                  }

                  return (
                    <div key={j} className={'flex items-center gap-2 ' + cls}
                      onClick={() => handleSelect(i, opt)}>
                      {isCorrectOpt && <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500 shrink-0" />}
                      {isWrongOpt && <XCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-500 shrink-0" />}
                      <span>{opt}</span>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              {submitted && q.explanation && (
                <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 italic border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/80 pt-3">💡 {q.explanation}</p>
              )}
            </motion.div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {!submitted && (
        <div className="mt-6 text-center">
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-400 mb-2">
            Отвечено {Object.keys(answers).length} из {total} вопросов
          </p>
          {Object.keys(answers).length === total && (
            <button onClick={handleSubmit}
              className="px-8 py-2.5 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 text-white rounded-lg font-medium transition">
              Проверить ответы
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/HistoryPanel.tsx
================================================================================
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FileText, X, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { HistoryItem } from '../types';

interface Props {
  items: HistoryItem[];
  onRestore: (item: HistoryItem) => void;
  onRemove: (id: string) => void;
  onClear: () => void;
}

export default function HistoryPanel({ items, onRestore, onRemove, onClear }: Props) {
  if (items.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.35 }}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
        <span className="font-medium text-slate-800">История анализов</span>
        <button onClick={onClear}
          className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-500 transition">
          <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3" />Очистить
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        {items.map(item => (
          <div key={item.id} className="card p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
            <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-brand-500 shrink-0" />
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 truncate">{item.fileName}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{item.date} · {item.textLength.toLocaleString()} символов · {item.duration} сек</p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => onRestore(item)}
              className="px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium text-brand-600 border border-brand-200 hover:bg-brand-50 transition">
              Открыть
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => onRemove(item.id)}
              className="p-1 text-slate-300 hover:text-rose-500 transition">
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx
================================================================================
import { AnalysisSettings } from '../types';

interface Props {
  settings: AnalysisSettings;
  onChange: (s: AnalysisSettings) => void;
}

const detailLabels: Record<string, string> = { brief: 'Краткий', medium: 'Средний', detailed: 'Подробный' };
const diffLabels: Record<string, string> = { easy: 'Лёгкий', medium: 'Средний', hard: 'Сложный', mixed: 'Смешанный' };
const typeLabels: Record<string, string> = { multiple: 'Выбор ответа', truefalse: 'Верно / Неверно' };

function SegmentedControl({ options, value, onChange }: { options: { key: string; label: string }[]; value: string; onChange: (v: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900/50 rounded-lg p-1 gap-1">
      {options.map(o => (
        <button key={o.key} onClick={() => onChange(o.key)}
          className={'flex-1 min-w-[80px] sm:min-w-0 px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-sm transition ' + (value === o.key ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 font-medium shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-slate-200')}>
          {o.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function SettingsPanel({ settings, onChange }: Props) {
  const set = (patch: Partial<AnalysisSettings>) => onChange({ ...settings, ...patch });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2 block">Детализация конспекта</label>
        <SegmentedControl
          options={Object.entries(detailLabels).map(([k, v]) => ({ key: k, label: v }))}
          value={settings.summaryDetail}
          onChange={v => set({ summaryDetail: v as any })}
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2 block">Количество карточек: {settings.flashcardCount}</label>
        <input type="range" min={3} max={12} value={settings.flashcardCount}
          onChange={e => set({ flashcardCount: +e.target.value })}
          className="w-full accent-brand-600" />
        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1"><span>3</span><span>12</span></div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2 block">Количество вопросов: {settings.quizCount}</label>
        <input type="range" min={3} max={10} value={settings.quizCount}
          onChange={e => set({ quizCount: +e.target.value })}
          className="w-full accent-brand-600" />
        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1"><span>3</span><span>10</span></div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2 block">Сложность теста</label>
        <SegmentedControl
          options={Object.entries(diffLabels).map(([k, v]) => ({ key: k, label: v }))}
          value={settings.quizDifficulty}
          onChange={v => set({ quizDifficulty: v as any })}
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2 block">Тип вопросов</label>
        <SegmentedControl
          options={Object.entries(typeLabels).map(([k, v]) => ({ key: k, label: v }))}
          value={settings.quizType}
          onChange={v => set({ quizType: v as any })}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/ExpressPrepView.tsx
================================================================================
import { SummaryInfo } from '../types';
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkMath from 'remark-math';
import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex';
import 'katex/dist/katex.min.css';
import { Play, Pause, Square, Headphones, FileText, Sparkles, Volume2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { updateQuestProgress } from '../api/quests';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { useConfirm } from '../contexts/ConfirmContext';
import { playTTS, stopTTS, pauseTTS, resumeTTS, getTTSPlaybackRate, setTTSPlaybackRate } from '../utils/tts';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { checkAILimit, incrementAIUsage } from '../api/limits';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import { Share2 } from 'lucide-react';

interface PodcastLine {
  speaker: 'Макс' | 'Софья' | string;
  text: string;
}

export default function ExpressPrepView({ 
  summaryJson, 
  materialId, 
  originalText, 
  onUpdate 
}: { 
  summaryJson: any; 
  materialId?: string; 
  originalText?: string; 
  onUpdate?: (updatedFields: any) => void; 
}) {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { alert } = useConfirm();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  // Вытаскиваем данные из составного JSON
  const cheatsheet = summaryJson?.cheatsheet || '';
  const podcastLines: PodcastLine[] = summaryJson?.podcast || [];

  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'cheatsheet' | 'podcast'>(
    summaryJson?._startTab === 'podcast' ? 'podcast' : 'cheatsheet'
  );
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [isPaused, setIsPaused] = useState(false);
  const [currentSpeakingIndex, setCurrentSpeakingIndex] = useState(-1);
  const currentIndexRef = useRef(0);
  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false);

  const handleCreateCheatsheet = async () => {
    if (!materialId) return;
    setIsGenerating(true);

    const context = (originalText || summaryJson?.content || '').slice(0, 15000);
    if (!context.trim()) {
      await alert({ title: 'Ошибка', message: 'Недостаточно контента для создания шпаргалки.', type: 'danger' });
      setIsGenerating(false);
      return;
    }

    if (user?.id) {
      const limitCheck = await checkAILimit(user.id, 5);
      if (!limitCheck.allowed) {
        setIsGenerating(false);
        toast.error('Не хватает энергии ИИ (нужно 5). Перейдите на PRO-тариф для безлимитного доступа!');
        navigate('/pricing');
        return;
      }
      await incrementAIUsage(user.id, 5);
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/quick-prep', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ context }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
      const d = await res.json();

      if (!d.cheatsheet) throw new Error();

      // Сохраняем в Supabase
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('materials')
        .update({
          summary_json: {
            ...summaryJson,
            cheatsheet: d.cheatsheet
          }
        })
        .eq('id', materialId);

      if (error) throw error;

      // Обновляем состояние родительского компонента
      if (onUpdate) {
        onUpdate({
          summary_json: {
            ...summaryJson,
            cheatsheet: d.cheatsheet
          }
        });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      await alert({ title: 'Ошибка', message: 'Не удалось сгенерировать шпаргалку. Попробуйте еще раз.', type: 'danger' });
    } finally {
      setIsGenerating(false);
    }
  };

  // Очистка при размонтировании
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      stopTTS();
    };
  }, []);

  // Синхронизация паузы убрана, так как теперь используется надежный backend TTS (edge-tts/audio element), 
  // который не прерывается при потере фокуса, в отличие от старого window.speechSynthesis.

  const stopPodcast = () => {
    stopTTS();
    setIsPlaying(false);
    setIsPaused(false);
    setCurrentSpeakingIndex(-1);
    currentIndexRef.current = 0;
  };

  const playPodcast = async () => {
    if (!window.speechSynthesis) {
      await alert({ title: 'Ошибка', message: 'Синтез речи не поддерживается вашим браузером.', type: 'danger' });
      return;
    }

    if (isPlaying && !isPaused) {
      // Пауза
      pauseTTS();
      setIsPaused(true);
      return;
    }

    if (isPlaying && isPaused) {
      // Возобновление
      resumeTTS();
      setIsPaused(false);
      return;
    }

    // Запуск с начала или с текущего индекса
    stopTTS();
    setIsPaused(false);
    
    const speakNext = () => {
      if (currentIndexRef.current >= podcastLines.length) {
        setIsPlaying(false);
        setIsPaused(false);
        setCurrentSpeakingIndex(-1);
        currentIndexRef.current = 0;
        return;
      }

      const line = podcastLines[currentIndexRef.current];
      setCurrentSpeakingIndex(currentIndexRef.current);

      const role = line.speaker === 'Макс' ? 'male' : 'female';

      playTTS(
        line.text,
        role,
        () => {
          currentIndexRef.current++;
          speakNext();
        },
        (e) => {
          console.error('Speech synthesis error:', e);
          setIsPlaying(false);
          setIsPaused(false);
          setCurrentSpeakingIndex(-1);
        }
      );
    };

    setIsPlaying(true);
    speakNext();
    if (user?.id) updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'podcast', 1);
  };

  const handleShare = async () => {
    if (!materialId) return;
    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('materials')
        .update({ is_public: true })
        .eq('id', materialId);
      
      if (error) throw error;
      
      const shareUrl = `${window.location.origin}/shared/${materialId}`;
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl);
      toast.success('Ссылка скопирована! Теперь вы можете отправить её друзьям.');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      toast.error('Не удалось создать ссылку');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <button
          onClick={handleShare}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 font-bold rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/50 transition"
        >
          <Share2 className="w-4 h-4" />
          Поделиться с друзьями
        </button>
      </div>
      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {/* ВКЛАДКА 1: Шпаргалка */}
        {tab === 'cheatsheet' && (
          <motion.div
            key="cheatsheet"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
            className="space-y-4"
          >
            {!cheatsheet ? (
              <div className="card p-8 text-center space-y-5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl shadow-sm">
                <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-inner">
                  <FileText className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-500 dark:text-amber-400" />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 text-base">Шпаргалка еще не создана</h3>
                  <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-sm max-w-md mx-auto">
                    Выжмите самое главное из этой лекции! ИИ мгновенно подготовит краткую экспресс-шпаргалку для быстрого повторения перед парой.
                  </p>
                </div>
                {materialId ? (
                  <button
                    onClick={handleCreateCheatsheet}
                    disabled={isGenerating}
                    className="px-6 py-3 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-black transition flex items-center gap-2 mx-auto shadow-lg shadow-rose-600/25 active:translate-y-0 hover:-translate-y-0.5 duration-200"
                  >
                    {isGenerating ? (
                      <>
                        <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
                        Анализируем лекцию...
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-300 animate-pulse" />
                        Создать шпаргалку за 5 минут
                      </>
                    )}
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <p className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 italic text-xs">
                    Перезапустите анализ файла, чтобы сгенерировать дополнительные материалы.
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="bg-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-800/80 text-white rounded-2xl p-6 md:p-8 shadow-xl border border-slate-200/10 dark:border-slate-800 relative overflow-hidden">
                <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-48 h-48 bg-white/5 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
                <p className="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-indigo-300 dark:text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-4 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  ⚡ Экспресс-шпаргалка за 5 минут
                </p>
                <div className="prose prose-invert max-w-none text-sm text-slate-300 leading-relaxed cheatsheet-style">
                  <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkMath]} rehypePlugins={[rehypeKatex]}>{cheatsheet}</ReactMarkdown>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ВКЛАДКА 2: Аудио-подкаст */}
        {tab === 'podcast' && (
          <motion.div
            key="podcast"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
            className="space-y-6"
          >
            {podcastLines.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="card p-6 text-center text-slate-400 italic text-sm">
                Для этой лекции аудио-подкаст еще не создан. Перезапустите анализ файла.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                {/* Аудио-плеер */}
                <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-600 to-indigo-800 text-white rounded-3xl p-5 md:p-6 shadow-xl shadow-indigo-200/50 dark:shadow-none border border-indigo-500/20 dark:border-indigo-500/10 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-5 relative overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-64 h-64 bg-white/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none -mt-10 -mr-10" />
                  
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4 relative z-10 w-full md:w-auto">
                    <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-md rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 border border-white/20 shadow-inner">
                      <Headphones className="w-7 h-7 text-white" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-left flex-1">
                      <h4 className="font-extrabold text-sm sm:text-base text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        AI-Подкаст: {summaryJson?.title || 'Разбор лекции'}
                        <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-300" />
                      </h4>
                      <p className="text-xs text-indigo-200 mt-0.5">Слушайте простое объяснение темы</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 relative z-10 w-full md:w-auto">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => {
                          const current = getTTSPlaybackRate();
                          const next = current === 1.0 ? 1.25 : current === 1.25 ? 1.5 : current === 1.5 ? 2.0 : 1.0;
                          setTTSPlaybackRate(next);
                          // Forcing re-render to update the button text
                          setTab(tab); 
                        }}
                        className="px-3 py-3.5 bg-indigo-900/50 hover:bg-indigo-900 text-indigo-200 hover:text-white rounded-xl transition border border-indigo-400/30 font-bold"
                        title="Скорость"
                      >
                        {getTTSPlaybackRate()}x
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={playPodcast}
                        className="flex-1 md:flex-none justify-center px-6 py-3.5 bg-white hover:bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 font-black rounded-xl text-sm transition flex items-center gap-2 shadow-lg hover:-translate-y-0.5"
                      >
                        {isPlaying && !isPaused ? <Pause className="w-5 h-5 fill-current" /> : <Play className="w-5 h-5 fill-current" />}
                        {isPlaying && !isPaused ? 'Пауза' : currentSpeakingIndex !== -1 ? 'Продолжить' : 'Слушать'}
                      </button>
                      {currentSpeakingIndex !== -1 && (
                        <button
                          onClick={stopPodcast}
                          className="p-3.5 bg-indigo-900/50 hover:bg-indigo-900 text-indigo-200 hover:text-white rounded-xl transition border border-indigo-400/30"
                          title="Остановить"
                        >
                          <Square className="w-5 h-5 fill-current" />
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* Бегущий диалог подкаста */}
                <div className="space-y-4 max-h-[500px] overflow-y-auto pr-2 custom-scrollbar">
                  {podcastLines.map((line, idx) => {
                    const isSpeaking = currentSpeakingIndex === idx;
                    const isMax = line.speaker === 'Макс';

                    return (
                      <div
                        key={idx}
                        className={`flex ${isMax ? 'justify-start' : 'justify-end'} transition-all duration-300 mb-2`}
                      >
                        <div
                          className={`relative max-w-[85%] sm:max-w-[70%] px-5 py-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm transition-all duration-300 ${
                            isMax
                              ? `bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 rounded-bl-sm ${
                                  isSpeaking
                                    ? 'border-indigo-500 dark:border-indigo-500 ring-4 ring-indigo-500/20 shadow-md'
                                    : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800/80'
                                }`
                              : `bg-indigo-600 dark:bg-indigo-700 border text-white rounded-br-sm ${
                                  isSpeaking
                                    ? 'border-indigo-400 dark:border-indigo-400 ring-4 ring-indigo-500/30 shadow-md'
                                    : 'border-indigo-500 dark:border-indigo-600'
                                }`
                          }`}
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5 opacity-90">
                            <span className={`text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider ${isMax ? 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400' : 'text-indigo-200'}`}>
                              {line.speaker}
                            </span>
                            {isSpeaking && (
                              <Volume2 className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isMax ? 'text-indigo-500 dark:text-indigo-400' : 'text-white'} animate-pulse`} />
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <p className={`text-[13px] sm:text-sm leading-relaxed font-medium ${isMax ? 'text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200' : 'text-white'}`}>
                            {line.text}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/AchievementToast.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Trophy, Star, Flame, Zap, BookOpen, Award, Brain, CheckCircle2, X } from 'lucide-react';

export interface Achievement {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  icon: string;
  xp: number;
}

interface Props {
  achievement: Achievement | null;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const ICON_MAP: Record<string, React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  trophy: Trophy,
  star: Star,
  flame: Flame,
  zap: Zap,
  book: BookOpen,
  award: Award,
  brain: Brain,
  check: CheckCircle2,
};

export default function AchievementToast({ achievement, onClose }: Props) {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!achievement) return;
    setVisible(true);
    const t = setTimeout(() => { setVisible(false); setTimeout(onClose, 400); }, 4500);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [achievement]);

  if (!achievement) return null;

  const Icon = ICON_MAP[achievement.icon] ?? Trophy;

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      {visible && (
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 80, scale: 0.9 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0, scale: 1 }}
          exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 80, scale: 0.9 }}
          transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 300, damping: 25 }}
          className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex items-center gap-4 bg-slate-900 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-2xl shadow-amber-500/10 max-w-xs"
        >
          {/* Иконка достижения */}
          <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-400 to-amber-600 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-lg">
            <Icon className="w-6 h-6 text-white" />
          </div>

          {/* Текст */}
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5 flex items-center gap-1">
              <Trophy className="w-3 h-3" /> Достижение разблокировано!
            </p>
            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{achievement.title}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 truncate">{achievement.description}</p>
            <div className="mt-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-indigo-500/20 border border-indigo-500/30 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5">
              <Zap className="w-3 h-3 text-indigo-400" />
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-indigo-300">+{achievement.xp} XP</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Кнопка закрыть */}
          <button
            onClick={() => { setVisible(false); setTimeout(onClose, 400); }}
            className="text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-400 transition shrink-0"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

          {/* Прогресс-бар автозакрытия */}
          <motion.div
            className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-0.5 bg-amber-400 rounded-full"
            initial={{ scaleX: 1, originX: 0 }}
            animate={{ scaleX: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 4.5, ease: 'linear' }}
          />
        </motion.div>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/ConfettiCelebration.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';

interface Props {
  trigger: boolean;
  type?: 'quest' | 'exam5' | 'achievement' | 'level';
}

export default function ConfettiCelebration({ trigger, type = 'quest' }: Props) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!trigger) return;

    const defaults = { startVelocity: 30, spread: 360, ticks: 60, zIndex: 9999 };

    if (type === 'level') {
      // Мощный залп для повышения уровня
      const duration = 3000;
      const end = Date.now() + duration;
      const frame = () => {
        confetti({ ...defaults, particleCount: 7, origin: { x: Math.random(), y: Math.random() * 0.6 } });
        if (Date.now() < end) requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      };
      frame();
    } else if (type === 'exam5') {
      // Двойной залп по бокам для пятёрки
      confetti({ particleCount: 100, angle: 60, spread: 55, origin: { x: 0 }, colors: ['#4f46e5', '#7c3aed', '#06b6d4'] });
      confetti({ particleCount: 100, angle: 120, spread: 55, origin: { x: 1 }, colors: ['#4f46e5', '#7c3aed', '#06b6d4'] });
    } else if (type === 'achievement') {
      confetti({ particleCount: 80, spread: 80, origin: { y: 0.6 }, colors: ['#f59e0b', '#fbbf24', '#fcd34d'] });
    } else {
      // Обычный квест — лёгкий
      confetti({ particleCount: 60, spread: 70, origin: { y: 0.6 }, colors: ['#4f46e5', '#10b981', '#f59e0b'] });
    }
  }, [trigger, type]);

  return null;
}

FILE: frontend/src/pages/Landing.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';

export default function Landing() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  useEffect(() => { if (user?.id) navigate('/dashboard'); }, [user?.id]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 font-sans selection:bg-indigo-500/30 overflow-x-hidden relative">
      {/* Grid Pattern Background */}
      <div 
        className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none opacity-[0.4] dark:opacity-[0.1]"
        style={{
          backgroundImage: `radial-gradient(currentColor 1.5px, transparent 1.5px)`,
          backgroundSize: '24px 24px',
          maskImage: 'linear-gradient(to bottom, white 50%, transparent)',
          WebkitMaskImage: 'linear-gradient(to bottom, white 50%, transparent)'
        }}
      />

      <Navbar />
      <Hero />
      <Stats />
      <ProductSimulation />
      <Features />
      <EducatorsSection />
      <CtaSection />
      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}

function Navbar() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();
  const isDarkActive = theme === 'dark' || (theme === 'system' && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches);
  return (
    <>
      <nav className="fixed top-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-between px-6 md:px-12 py-4 bg-white/70 dark:bg-slate-950/70 backdrop-blur-xl border-b border-slate-200/50 dark:border-slate-800/50">
        {/* Logo */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 cursor-pointer z-50" onClick={() => navigate('/')}>
          <div className="w-9 h-9 bg-indigo-600 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-indigo-500/20">
            <GraduationCap className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
          </div>
          <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-xl tracking-tight">StudyForge</span>
        </div>

        {/* Desktop Nav Links */}
        <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-8 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
          <a href="#features" className="hover:text-indigo-600 dark:hover:text-indigo-400 transition">Возможности</a>
          <a href="#how-it-works" className="hover:text-indigo-600 dark:hover:text-indigo-400 transition">Как это работает</a>
          <a href="#educators" className="hover:text-indigo-600 dark:hover:text-indigo-400 transition">Преподавателям</a>
        </div>

        {/* Desktop Actions */}
        <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
          <button onClick={() => setTheme(isDarkActive ? 'light' : 'dark')} className="text-slate-500 hover:text-indigo-500 transition mr-2" title="Переключить тему">
            {isDarkActive ? <Sun className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Moon className="w-5 h-5" />}
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => navigate('/login')}
            className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-white transition px-3 py-2">
            Войти
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => navigate('/register')}
            className="text-sm font-bold px-5 py-2.5 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-xl transition shadow-lg shadow-indigo-500/20 hover:shadow-indigo-500/30 hover:-translate-y-0.5 active:translate-y-0 duration-200">
            Начать бесплатно
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Mobile Menu Toggle */}
        <div className="md:hidden flex items-center gap-4 z-50">
          <button onClick={() => setTheme(isDarkActive ? 'light' : 'dark')} className="text-slate-500 hover:text-indigo-500 transition" title="Переключить тему">
            {isDarkActive ? <Sun className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Moon className="w-5 h-5" />}
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(!isMenuOpen)} 
            className="p-2 -mr-2 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-white focus:outline-none"
          >
            {isMenuOpen ? <X className="w-6 h-6" /> : <Menu className="w-6 h-6" />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </nav>

      {/* Mobile Menu Overlay */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {isMenuOpen && (
          <motion.div 
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -20 }}
            className="fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-white/95 dark:bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-8 md:hidden px-6 pt-16"
          >
            <a href="#features" onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(false)} className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100">Возможности</a>
            <a href="#how-it-works" onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(false)} className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100">Как это работает</a>
            <a href="#educators" onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(false)} className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100">Преподавателям</a>
            <div className="flex flex-col w-full max-w-xs gap-4 mt-4">
              <button onClick={() => { setIsMenuOpen(false); navigate('/login'); }}
                className="w-full py-4 text-center font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 transition">
                Войти
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => { setIsMenuOpen(false); navigate('/register'); }}
                className="w-full py-4 text-center font-bold text-white bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 rounded-xl transition shadow-lg shadow-indigo-500/20">
                Начать бесплатно
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </>
  );
}

function Hero() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [rotateX, setRotateX] = useState(0);
  const [rotateY, setRotateY] = useState(0);
  const [isHovered, setIsHovered] = useState(false);

  const handleMouseMove = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const card = e.currentTarget;
    const box = card.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = e.clientX - box.left - box.width / 2;
    const y = e.clientY - box.top - box.height / 2;
    // Максимальный угол наклона - 12 градусов
    setRotateX(-y / (box.height / 24));
    setRotateY(x / (box.width / 24));
  };

  const handleMouseLeave = () => {
    setIsHovered(false);
    setRotateX(0);
    setRotateY(0);
  };

  return (
    <section className="pt-32 pb-24 px-6 relative max-w-7xl mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-12 items-center">
      
      {/* Левая текстовая колонка */}
      <motion.div 
        initial="hidden"
        animate="visible"
        variants={{
          hidden: { opacity: 0 },
          visible: { opacity: 1, transition: { staggerChildren: 0.2 } }
        }}
        className="lg:col-span-6 text-left space-y-6"
      >
        {/* Академический бейдж */}
        <motion.div 
          variants={{
            hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 20 },
            visible: { opacity: 1, y: 0, transition: { duration: 0.6, ease: "easeOut" } }
          }}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 border border-indigo-100 dark:border-indigo-900/30 rounded-full text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide transition-colors duration-300"
        >
          <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>Доказательная педагогика и ИИ</span>
        </motion.div>

        <motion.h1 
          variants={{
            hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 20 },
            visible: { opacity: 1, y: 0, transition: { duration: 0.6, ease: "easeOut" } }
          }}
          className="text-4xl md:text-6xl font-black tracking-tight leading-[1.1] text-slate-900 dark:text-white transition-colors duration-300"
        >
          Осваивайте лекции <br className="hidden md:block" />
          <span className="text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">в 4 раза быстрее</span>
        </motion.h1>

        <motion.p 
          variants={{
            hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 20 },
            visible: { opacity: 1, y: 0, transition: { duration: 0.6, ease: "easeOut" } }
          }}
          className="text-lg text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed max-w-xl transition-colors duration-300"
        >
          Автоматизируйте рутину конспектирования. StudyForge анализирует материалы, создаёт интерактивные конспекты, карточки для интервального повторения по методу Лейтнера и проводит устные экзамены.
        </motion.p>

        {/* Кнопки действий */}
        <motion.div 
          variants={{
            hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 20 },
            visible: { opacity: 1, y: 0, transition: { duration: 0.6, ease: "easeOut" } }
          }}
          className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-wrap pt-2"
        >
          <button onClick={() => navigate('/register')}
            className="group relative flex items-center gap-2 px-8 py-4 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-650 dark:hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-bold text-base rounded-xl transition-all shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 dark:shadow-indigo-900/10 hover:shadow-indigo-300 dark:hover:shadow-indigo-850/20 hover:-translate-y-1 active:translate-y-0 duration-300 overflow-hidden">
            <span className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full -mt-1 rounded-lg opacity-30 bg-gradient-to-b from-transparent via-transparent to-black" />
            <span className="relative">Начать бесплатно</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform relative" />
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => {
              const el = document.getElementById('how-it-works');
              if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
            }}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-8 py-4 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 font-bold text-base rounded-xl transition-all shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:-translate-y-1 active:translate-y-0 duration-300 transition-colors">
            <Zap className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400" />
            Смотреть демо
          </button>
        </motion.div>

        {/* Дополнительные преимущества */}
        <motion.div 
          variants={{
            hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 20 },
            visible: { opacity: 1, y: 0, transition: { duration: 0.6, ease: "easeOut" } }
          }}
          className="flex items-center gap-6 pt-4 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400"
        >
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500" /> Без привязки карт</span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500" /> Сделано для вузов</span>
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Правая колонка с интерактивной 3D сценой */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-6 flex items-center justify-center relative mt-12 lg:mt-0">
        {/* Размытый фон сцены с пульсацией */}
        <motion.div 
          animate={{ scale: [1, 1.1, 1], opacity: [0.3, 0.5, 0.3] }}
          transition={{ duration: 6, repeat: Infinity, ease: "easeInOut" }}
          className="absolute w-[450px] h-[450px] bg-gradient-to-tr from-indigo-300/40 to-violet-300/40 rounded-full blur-[100px] pointer-events-none" 
        />

        {/* 3D Перспектива */}
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.8, y: 50 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.8, type: "spring", bounce: 0.4, delay: 0.2 }}
          className="relative w-full max-w-lg aspect-[4/3] cursor-pointer origin-center z-10"
          style={{ perspective: 1200 }}
          onMouseMove={handleMouseMove}
          onMouseEnter={() => setIsHovered(true)}
          onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
        >
          <motion.div
            className="w-full h-full relative"
            animate={{
              rotateX: isHovered ? rotateX : 0,
              rotateY: isHovered ? rotateY : 0,
              scale: isHovered ? 1.05 : 1
            }}
            transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 100, damping: 20 }}
            style={{ transformStyle: "preserve-3d" }}
          >
            {/* Нижний уровень: Тень с пульсацией */}
            <motion.div 
              animate={{ opacity: isHovered ? 0.8 : 0.4, scale: isHovered ? 1.05 : 1 }}
              className="absolute inset-4 bg-indigo-600/15 rounded-3xl blur-2xl transition-all duration-300"
              style={{ transform: "translateZ(-60px)" }}

              style={{ transform: "translateZ(0px)" }}
            >
              {/* Хедер дашборда */}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 pb-3 transition-colors">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-rose-400 shadow-sm" />
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-amber-400 shadow-sm" />
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 shadow-sm" />

                  <div className="h-10 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/50 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl flex items-center px-4 justify-between transition-colors">
