import { Material, StudySession, Flashcard } from '../types';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { BookOpen, Brain, GraduationCap, Flame, ChevronRight, FileText, Zap, Calendar, Award, Gamepad2, Heart, Trophy, Sparkles, BarChart2, Clock, Bot, Layers, Users, MessageSquare, CheckCircle2, Backpack } from 'lucide-react';
import { format, subDays } from 'date-fns';
import { ru } from 'date-fns/locale';
import { BarChart, Bar, XAxis, YAxis, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer } from 'recharts';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';
import { getDueCards } from '../api/flashcards';
import { getQuestsWithProgress, getUserXP, calcLevel } from '../api/quests';
import { getProfile } from '../api/social';
import { useTheme } from '../contexts/ThemeProvider';

function StatCard({ icon: Icon, label, value, color }: { icon: React.ElementType; label: string; value: number; color: string }) {
  const bgMap: Record<string, string> = { 
    indigo: 'bg-indigo-50/80 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 border-indigo-100 dark:border-indigo-900/50', 
    emerald: 'bg-emerald-50/80 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 border-emerald-100 dark:border-emerald-900/50', 
    amber: 'bg-amber-50/80 dark:bg-amber-950/40 border-amber-100 dark:border-amber-900/50', 
    rose: 'bg-rose-50/80 dark:bg-rose-950/40 border-rose-100 dark:border-rose-900/50' 
  };
  const textMap: Record<string, string> = { 
    indigo: 'text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400', 
    emerald: 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400', 
    amber: 'text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400', 
    rose: 'text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400' 
  };
  return (
    <div className="bg-white/60 dark:bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-md border border-slate-200/60 dark:border-slate-800/60 rounded-2xl p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center gap-3 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
      <div className={`w-10 h-10 ${bgMap[color]} border rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0`}>
        <Icon className={`w-5 h-5 ${textMap[color]}`} />
      </div>
      <div>
        <p className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">{value}</p>
        <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">{label}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Dashboard() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [dueCards, setDueCards] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [subjects, setSubjects] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [mood, setMood] = useState<any>(null);
  const [quests, setQuests] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [xp, setXp] = useState(0);
  const [levelInfo, setLevelInfo] = useState(calcLevel(0));
  const [nextExam, setNextExam] = useState<any>(null);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<any>(null);

  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const isDark = theme === 'dark' || (theme === 'system' && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches);

  const handleToggleRole = async () => {
    if (!user || !profile) return;
    const newRole = profile.role === 'teacher' ? 'student' : 'teacher';
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .update({ role: newRole })
      .eq('id', user.id);
    if (!error) {
      setProfile({ ...profile, role: newRole });
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event('profile_updated'));
      // Если переключаемся на преподавателя, перенаправляем на /teacher
      if (newRole === 'teacher') {
        navigate('/teacher');
      }
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    Promise.all([
      getMaterials(user.id),
      getDueCards(user.id),
      supabase.from('subjects').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id),
      supabase.from('study_sessions').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).order('date', { ascending: false }).limit(30),
      supabase.from('mood_log').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).order('logged_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1).maybeSingle(),
      getQuestsWithProgress(user.id),
      getUserXP(user.id),
      getProfile(user.id),
    ]).then(([mats, cards, subRes, sessRes, moodRes, questsData, xpVal, prof]) => {
      setMaterials(mats);
      setDueCards(cards);
      const fetchedSubjects = subRes.data || [];
      setSubjects(fetchedSubjects);
      setSessions(sessRes.data || []);
      setMood(moodRes.data);
      setQuests(questsData);
      const lvl = calcLevel(xpVal);
      setXp(xpVal);
      setLevelInfo(lvl);
      setProfile(prof);
      // Ближайший экзамен
      if (fetchedSubjects.length > 0) {
        const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
        const upcoming = fetchedSubjects
          .filter(s => s.exam_date && s.exam_date >= today)
          .sort((a, b) => a.exam_date.localeCompare(b.exam_date));
        if (upcoming.length > 0) setNextExam(upcoming[0]);
      }
    }).catch(console.error).finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [user?.id]);

  const streak = (() => {
    const dates = new Set(sessions.map((s: StudySession) => s.date));
    let count = 0;
    let d = new Date();
    while (dates.has(format(d, 'yyyy-MM-dd'))) {
      count++;
      d = subDays(d, 1);
    }
    return count;
  })();

  const chartData = (() => {
    const map: Record<string, number> = {};
    sessions.forEach((s: StudySession) => { map[s.date] = s.cards_reviewed || 0; });
    const days = [];
    for (let i = 13; i >= 0; i--) {
      const d = subDays(new Date(), i);
      const key = format(d, 'yyyy-MM-dd');
      days.push({ day: format(d, 'dd.MM'), cards_reviewed: map[key] || 0 });
    }
    return days;
  })();

  if (loading) return (
    <div className="p-6 flex items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh]">
      <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
    </div>
  );

  const name = user?.user_metadata?.full_name || 'Студент';

  const getRecommendedTool = () => {
    if (!mood) return null;
    const mode = mood.recommended_mode.toLowerCase();
    if (mode.includes('глубокое погружение')) return 'exam';
    if (mode.includes('активное обучение')) return 'play';
    if (mode.includes('повторение')) return 'review';
    if (mode.includes('лёгкий просмотр')) return 'chat';
    if (mode.includes('анти-стресс')) return 'courses';
    return null;
  };

  const recTool = getRecommendedTool();

  return (
    <div className="p-3 md:p-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto">
      {/* Role switching banner */}
      {profile && profile.role !== 'teacher' ? (
        <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-50 to-indigo-100/50 dark:from-slate-900 dark:to-indigo-950/20 border border-indigo-100 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 shadow-sm tour-step-role-toggle">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-indigo-600 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-white shrink-0 shadow-md">
              <GraduationCap className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h4 className="font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm">Вы вошли как Студент 🎒</h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-xl mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
                Хотите создавать свои классы, приглашать студентов по коду приглашения и ставить им дедлайны? Переключитесь в режим преподавателя!
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={handleToggleRole}
            className="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md shadow-indigo-200 dark:shadow-none transition shrink-0"
          >
            Стать Преподавателем <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 ml-1 inline text-indigo-500" />
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : profile && (
        <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-50 to-emerald-100/50 dark:from-slate-900 dark:to-emerald-950/20 border border-emerald-100 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 shadow-sm tour-step-role-toggle">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-emerald-600 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-white shrink-0 shadow-md">
              <Users className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h4 className="font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm flex items-center gap-1.5">Вы вошли как Преподаватель <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-500" /></h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-xl mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
                Вам доступно управление группами и назначение заданий. Чтобы проходить курсы, играть в миссии и сдавать экзамены, переключитесь обратно в режим студента.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={handleToggleRole}
            className="px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md shadow-emerald-200 dark:shadow-none transition shrink-0"
          >
            Режим Студента 🎒
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Greeting + XP */}
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-6 flex-wrap gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-0.5">Привет, {name}! 👋</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 capitalize">{format(new Date(), 'EEEE, d MMMM', { locale: ru })}</p>
        </div>
        {/* XP / Level mini-badge */}
        <motion.button
          onClick={() => navigate('/analytics')}
          whileHover={{ scale: 1.02 }}
          className="flex w-full md:w-auto items-center gap-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-purple-600 text-white px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl shadow-xl shadow-indigo-200 dark:shadow-none hover:shadow-indigo-300 transition-shadow tour-step-desktop-xp tour-step-mobile-xp"
        >
          <div className="flex-1 text-left md:text-right">
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-200 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1.5">Уровень {levelInfo.level} · {levelInfo.name}</p>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="flex-1 md:w-32 h-2 bg-black/20 rounded-full overflow-hidden backdrop-blur-sm shadow-inner">
                <motion.div
                  className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-white/80 to-white rounded-full shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(255,255,255,0.8)]"
                  initial={{ width: 0 }}
                  animate={{ width: `${levelInfo.progress}%` }}
                  transition={{ duration: 1, ease: 'easeOut' }}
                />
              </div>
              <span className="text-sm font-black whitespace-nowrap">{xp} XP</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <BarChart2 className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-100 shrink-0 hidden sm:block" />
        </motion.button>
      </div>

      {/* Виджет ближайшего экзамена */}
      {nextExam && (() => {
        const daysLeft = Math.ceil((new Date(nextExam.exam_date).getTime() - new Date().getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
        const urgency = daysLeft <= 3 ? 'rose' : daysLeft <= 7 ? 'amber' : 'indigo';
        const urgencyBg = urgency === 'rose' 
          ? 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/20 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-900/40' 
          : urgency === 'amber' 
            ? 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/20 border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-900/40' 
            : 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/20 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-900/40';
        const urgencyText = urgency === 'rose' 
          ? 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400' 
          : urgency === 'amber' 
            ? 'text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400' 
            : 'text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400';
        return (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            onClick={() => navigate('/courses')}
            className={`mb-6 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-4 ${urgencyBg} hover:shadow-md transition`}
          >
            <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center font-black ${urgency === 'rose' ? 'bg-rose-500' : urgency === 'amber' ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-indigo-500'} text-white shrink-0`}>
              <span className="text-lg leading-none">{daysLeft}</span>
              <span className="text-[8px] font-bold uppercase">дн.</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <p className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5 ${urgencyText}`}>
                {daysLeft <= 3 ? <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Flame className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Срочно!</span> : daysLeft <= 7 ? <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Скоро экзамен</span> : <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Ближайший экзамен</span>}
              </p>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">{nextExam.name}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{new Date(nextExam.exam_date).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long' })}</p>
            </div>
            <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600" />
          </motion.div>
        );
      })()}

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 md:gap-4">
        <StatCard icon={BookOpen} label="Материалов" value={materials.length} color="indigo" />
        <StatCard icon={Brain} label="Карточек сегодня" value={dueCards.length} color="emerald" />
        <StatCard icon={GraduationCap} label="Предметов" value={subjects.length} color="amber" />
        {/* Стрик с анимацией огня */}
        <motion.div
          className="card p-4 flex items-center gap-3 cursor-default"
          whileHover={{ scale: 1.02 }}
        >
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center ${streak >= 3 ? 'bg-rose-500' : 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/30'}`}>
            <Flame className={`w-5 h-5 ${streak >= 3 ? 'text-white' : 'text-rose-400 dark:text-rose-500'} ${streak >= 3 ? 'animate-pulse' : ''}`} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">{streak}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center gap-1">{streak >= 3 ? <><Flame className="w-3 h-3 text-orange-500" /> Дней подряд!</> : 'Дней подряд'}</p>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </div>

      {/* Mood Recommendation */}
      {mood && (
        <div
          onClick={() => navigate('/mood')}
          className="mt-6 p-4 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-indigo-100 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl shadow-sm cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition flex items-center gap-4"
        >
          <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Heart className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-500 dark:text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">Твое состояние & Режим дня</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium leading-relaxed">{mood.recommended_mode}</p>
          </div>
          <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-650" />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Быстрый повтор за 5 минут */}
      {materials.length > 0 && (
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          onClick={() => navigate('/quick-prep')}
          className="mt-3 p-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-50 to-orange-50 dark:from-slate-900 dark:to-orange-950/20 border border-amber-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition flex items-center gap-4"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-amber-400 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Clock className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">⚡ Экспресс-режим</p>
            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">Повторить материал за 5 минут</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Шпаргалка + 2 разогревочных вопроса</p>
          </div>
          <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-650" />
        </motion.div>
      )}

      {/* Two columns */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4 md:gap-6 mt-4 md:mt-6">
        {/* Left Col: Quests */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-1 space-y-6">
          {materials.length === 0 && (
            <div className="card p-5 border-l-4 border-l-emerald-500 bg-emerald-50/50 dark:bg-emerald-950/20">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <h2 className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Bot className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-500" />
                  Первые шаги
                </h2>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-bold">1</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">Загрузи первый материал</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">+50 XP</p>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')} className="px-3 py-1 bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold hover:bg-emerald-600 transition">Вперед!</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="card p-5 border-l-4 border-l-indigo-500">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
              <h2 className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Trophy className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-500" />
                Квесты дня
              </h2>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {quests.filter(q => q.type === 'daily').map(q => (

                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${q.is_completed ? 'text-emerald-600 line-through dark:text-emerald-400/80' : 'text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200'}`}>
                        {q.title}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-normal mt-0.5 leading-snug">
                        {q.description}

                    <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-indigo-500 shrink-0">+{q.xp_reward} XP</span>
                  </div>

                    <div className="flex-1 h-1.5 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div 
                        className={`h-full transition-all duration-500 ${q.is_completed ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-indigo-500'}`}
                        style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (q.current_count / q.target_count) * 100)}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 shrink-0">{q.current_count}/{q.target_count}</span>

            </div>

          <div className="card p-5 border-l-4 border-l-purple-500">

                Еженедельные
              </h2>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {quests.filter(q => q.type === 'weekly').map(q => (
                <div key={q.id} className="space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${q.is_completed ? 'text-emerald-600 line-through dark:text-emerald-400/80' : 'text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200'}`}>
                        {q.title}
                      </p>

                        {q.description}
                      </p>

                    <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-purple-500 shrink-0">+{q.xp_reward} XP</span>
                  </div>

                    <div className="flex-1 h-1.5 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div 
                        className={`h-full transition-all duration-500 ${q.is_completed ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-purple-500'}`}

                    </div>

                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Col: Due and Recent */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:gap-6">
            {/* Due cards */}
            <div className="card p-5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">На повторение сегодня</h2>
                <button onClick={() => navigate('/review')} className="text-sm text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700 font-medium">Начать →</button>
              </div>
              {dueCards.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-emerald-600 text-sm flex items-center gap-1.5 justify-center">Все карточки повторены! <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" /></p>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {dueCards.slice(0, 3).map((c: any) => (
                    <div key={c.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                      <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">{c.question}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                  {dueCards.length > 3 && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">+ ещё {dueCards.length - 3}</p>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Recent materials */}
            <div className="card p-5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Последние материалы</h2>
                <button onClick={() => navigate('/library')} className="text-sm text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700 font-medium">Все →</button>
              </div>
              {materials.length === 0 ? (
                <button onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')} className="text-sm text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700">Загрузи первую лекцию →</button>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {materials.slice(0, 3).map((m: Material) => (
                    <div
                      key={m.id}
                      onClick={() => navigate('/library/' + m.id)}
                      className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 cursor-pointer hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-200 transition"
                    >
                      <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate flex-1">{m.title}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 shrink-0">{format(new Date(m.created_at), 'dd.MM.yy')}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Activity chart */}
          <div className="card p-4 md:p-5 overflow-x-auto custom-scrollbar">
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-4">Активность за 2 недели</h2>
            <div style={{ height: 200, minWidth: 500 }}>
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <BarChart data={chartData}>
                  <XAxis dataKey="day" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} stroke={isDark ? '#475569' : '#94a3b8'} />
                  <YAxis allowDecimals={false} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} stroke={isDark ? '#475569' : '#94a3b8'} />
                  <Tooltip
                    contentStyle={{ 
                      borderRadius: 8, 
                      border: isDark ? '1px solid #334155' : '1px solid #e2e8f0', 
                      backgroundColor: isDark ? '#1e293b' : '#ffffff',
                      color: isDark ? '#f1f5f9' : '#0f172a',
                      fontSize: 13 
                    }}
                    itemStyle={{ color: isDark ? '#f1f5f9' : '#0f172a' }}
                    labelStyle={{ color: isDark ? '#94a3b8' : '#64748b' }}
                    labelFormatter={(l: string) => l}
                    formatter={(v: number) => [v, 'Карточек']}
                  />
                  <Bar dataKey="cards_reviewed" fill={isDark ? '#6366f1' : '#4F46E5'} radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
                </BarChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Quick actions */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-3 md:gap-4 mt-4 md:mt-6">
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')}
          className="card p-5 text-center hover:shadow-md transition group tour-step-mobile-upload"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 group-hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:group-hover:bg-indigo-900/50 transition">
            <Zap className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">Загрузить лекцию</p>
        </button>
        
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/review')}
          className={`card p-5 text-center transition group relative overflow-hidden ${
            recTool === 'review' ? 'ring-2 ring-emerald-500 dark:ring-emerald-600 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100 dark:shadow-emerald-950/30' : 'hover:shadow-md'
          }`}
        >
          {recTool === 'review' && (
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-1">
              <span className="relative flex h-2 w-2">
                <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-emerald-400 opacity-75"></span>
                <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-emerald-500"></span>
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 transition ${
            recTool === 'review' ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' : 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 group-hover:bg-emerald-100 dark:group-hover:bg-emerald-900/50'
          }`}>
            <Brain className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <p className={`text-sm font-medium ${recTool === 'review' ? 'text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-100 font-bold' : 'text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'}`}>
            Повторить карточки
          </p>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/play')}
          className={`card p-5 text-center transition group relative overflow-hidden ${
            recTool === 'play' ? 'ring-2 ring-emerald-500 dark:ring-emerald-600 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100 dark:shadow-emerald-950/30' : 'hover:shadow-md'
          }`}
        >
          {recTool === 'play' && (
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-1">
              <span className="relative flex h-2 w-2">
                <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-emerald-400 opacity-75"></span>
                <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-emerald-500"></span>
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 transition ${
            recTool === 'play' ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' : 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 group-hover:bg-emerald-100 dark:group-hover:bg-emerald-900/50'
          }`}>
            <Gamepad2 className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <p className={`text-sm font-medium ${recTool === 'play' ? 'text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-100 font-bold' : 'text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'}`}>
            Микро-миссии
          </p>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/chat')}
          className={`card p-5 text-center transition group relative overflow-hidden tour-step-mobile-chat ${
            recTool === 'chat' ? 'ring-2 ring-indigo-500 dark:ring-indigo-600 shadow-lg shadow-indigo-100 dark:shadow-indigo-950/30' : 'hover:shadow-md'
          }`}
        >
          {recTool === 'chat' && (
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-1">
              <span className="relative flex h-2 w-2">
                <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-indigo-400 opacity-75"></span>
                <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-indigo-500"></span>
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 transition ${
            recTool === 'chat' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 group-hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:group-hover:bg-indigo-900/50'
          }`}>
            <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <p className={`text-sm font-medium ${recTool === 'chat' ? 'text-indigo-900 dark:text-indigo-100 font-bold' : 'text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'}`}>
            Чат-помощник
          </p>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/exam')}
          className={`card p-5 text-center transition group relative overflow-hidden tour-step-mobile-exam ${
            recTool === 'exam' ? 'ring-2 ring-rose-500 dark:ring-rose-600 shadow-lg shadow-rose-100 dark:shadow-rose-950/30' : 'hover:shadow-md'
          }`}
        >
          {recTool === 'exam' && (
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-1">
              <span className="relative flex h-2 w-2">
                <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-rose-400 opacity-75"></span>
                <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-rose-500"></span>
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 transition ${
            recTool === 'exam' ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white' : 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/40 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 group-hover:bg-rose-100 dark:group-hover:bg-rose-900/50'
          }`}>
            <Award className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <p className={`text-sm font-medium ${recTool === 'exam' ? 'text-rose-900 dark:text-rose-100 font-bold' : 'text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'}`}>
            Устный экзамен
          </p>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/courses')}
          className={`card p-5 text-center transition group relative overflow-hidden ${
            recTool === 'courses' ? 'ring-2 ring-amber-500 dark:ring-amber-600 shadow-lg shadow-amber-100 dark:shadow-amber-950/30' : 'hover:shadow-md'
          }`}

            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-1">

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Analyze.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import UploadZone from '../components/UploadZone';
import { Mic, Zap, BookOpen, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';
import AgentCards from '../components/AgentCards';
import ResultsPanel from '../components/ResultsPanel';
import { AnalysisState, AgentStatus, DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from '../types';
import { DEMO_TEXT } from '../demo';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { syncFlashcards } from '../api/flashcards';
import { updateQuestProgress, recordStudyActivity } from '../api/quests';
import { hasBadWords } from '../utils/moderation';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import { checkAILimit, incrementAIUsage } from '../api/limits';

const initAgents: AgentStatus[] = [
  { id: 'summarizer', label: 'Конспект', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'flashcards', label: 'Карточки', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'quiz', label: 'Тест', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'cheatsheet', label: 'Шпаргалка', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'podcast', label: 'Подкаст', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'missions', label: 'Миссии', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
];

interface Toast {
  id: number;
  text: string;
}

export default function Analyze() {
  const [state, setState] = useState<AnalysisState>({
    phase: 'upload', agents: initAgents, results: null, settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  });
  const [isDemo, setIsDemo] = useState(false);
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<Toast[]>([]);
  const toastId = useRef(0);
  const analysisStartRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const sourceFileNameRef = useRef<string>('');
  const textLengthRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const addToast = (text: string) => {
    const id = ++toastId.current;
    setToasts(prev => [...prev, { id, text }]);
    setTimeout(() => setToasts(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== id)), 3000);
  };

  const handleUpload = async (file: File | null, youtubeUrl?: string) => {
    analysisStartRef.current = Date.now();
    sourceFileNameRef.current = file ? file.name : (youtubeUrl ? 'YouTube Video' : 'Unknown');
    setState({ phase: 'extracting', agents: initAgents.map(a => ({ ...a, status: 'idle' as const })), results: null, settings: state.settings, sourceFileName: sourceFileNameRef.current });
    
    const fd = new FormData();
    if (file) fd.append('file', file);
    if (youtubeUrl) fd.append('youtube_url', youtubeUrl);
    
    fd.append('summary_detail', state.settings.summaryDetail);
    fd.append('flashcard_count', String(state.settings.flashcardCount));
    fd.append('quiz_count', String(state.settings.quizCount));
    fd.append('quiz_difficulty', state.settings.quizDifficulty);
    fd.append('quiz_type', state.settings.quizType);
    
    // Определяем эндпоинт в зависимости от типа
    const isMedia = youtubeUrl || (file && (file.type.startsWith('video/') || file.type.startsWith('audio/') || file.type.startsWith('image/')));
    const endpoint = isMedia ? '/api/analyze/media' : '/api/analyze';
    
    // ПРОВЕРКА ЛИМИТОВ ИИ
    if (user?.id) {
      const limitCheck = await checkAILimit(user.id, 20);
      if (!limitCheck.allowed) {
        setState(p => ({ ...p, phase: 'upload' }));
        toast.error('Не хватает энергии ИИ (нужно 20). Перейдите на PRO-тариф для безлимитного доступа!');
        navigate('/pricing');
        return;
      }
      await incrementAIUsage(user.id, 20);
    }

    try {
      const abortController = new AbortController();
      (window as any)._analyzeAbort = abortController;
      const res = await fetch(endpoint, { method: 'POST', body: fd, signal: abortController.signal });
      if (!res.ok) { const e = await res.json(); throw new Error(e.detail || 'Ошибка сервера'); }
      const reader = res.body?.getReader();
      if (!reader) throw new Error('No body');
      const dec = new TextDecoder(); let buf = '';
      while (true) {
        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        buf += dec.decode(value, { stream: true });
        const lines = buf.split('\n'); buf = lines.pop() || '';
        for (const l of lines) {
          if (l.startsWith('data: ')) {
            try { handleSSE(JSON.parse(l.slice(6))); } catch {}
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err: Error | any) { 
      if (err.name === 'AbortError') return;
      setState(p => ({ ...p, phase: 'error', error: err.message })); 
    }
  };

  const agentDoneLabels: Record<string, string> = {
    summarizer: 'Конспект готов',
    flashcards: 'Карточки готовы',
    quiz: 'Тест готов',
    cheatsheet: 'Шпаргалка готова',
    podcast: 'Подкаст готов',
    missions: 'Квесты готовы',
  };

  const handleSSE = (d: Record<string, any>) => {
    if (d.type === 'text_extracted') { 
      if (hasBadWords(d.text)) {
        if ((window as any)._analyzeAbort) {
          (window as any)._analyzeAbort.abort();
        }
        setState(p => ({ ...p, phase: 'error', error: 'Текст содержит недопустимые материалы (мат или неприемлемый контент).' }));
        return;
      }
      setState(p => ({ ...p, phase: 'analyzing', textLength: d.length })); 
      textLengthRef.current = d.length;
      (window as any)._lastExtractedText = d.text; // Сохраняем текст из события
    }
    else if (d.type === 'status') {
      // No toast needed, handled in main UI
    }
    else if (d.type === 'agent_start') setState(p => ({ ...p, agents: p.agents.map(a => a.id === d.agent ? { ...a, status: 'running' } : a) }));
    else if (d.type === 'agent_done') {
      // No toast needed, visualized directly in agent card
      setState(p => ({ ...p, agents: p.agents.map(a => a.id === d.agent ? { ...a, status: 'done', progress: d.progress / d.total } : a) }));
    }
    else if (d.type === 'results') {
      const results = {
        summarizer: d.summarizer,
        flashcards: d.flashcards,
        quiz: d.quiz,
        cheatsheet: d.cheatsheet,
        podcast: d.podcast,
        missions: d.missions
      };
      const dur = analysisStartRef.current
        ? Math.round((Date.now() - analysisStartRef.current) / 1000)
        : 0;
      setState(p => ({ ...p, phase: 'done', results, analysisDuration: dur || undefined }));

      if (user) {
        const title = sourceFileNameRef.current
          ? sourceFileNameRef.current.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')
          : 'Без названия';

        (async () => {
          const { data: inserted, error } = await supabase
            .from('materials')
            .insert({
              user_id: user.id,
              title,
              subject: results.summarizer?.subject || '',
              original_text: textLengthRef.current > 0 ? (window as any)._lastExtractedText || '' : '',
              summary_json: {
                content: results.summarizer?.content || '',
                terms: results.summarizer?.terms || [],
                cheatsheet: results.cheatsheet?.content || '',
                podcast: results.podcast?.podcast || []
              },
              missions_json: results.missions,
              flashcards_json: results.flashcards,
              quiz_json: results.quiz,
            })
            .select('id')
            .single();

          if (error) {
            console.error('Supabase insert error:', error);
            return;
          }

          if (inserted?.id) {
            setState(p => ({ ...p, savedMaterialId: inserted.id }));
          }

          const cards = results.flashcards?.flashcards || [];
          if (cards.length > 0 && inserted?.id) {
            await syncFlashcards(user.id, inserted.id, cards);
          }
          await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'analyze', 1);
          await recordStudyActivity(user.id, 0, 1);
        })();
      }
    }
    else if (d.type === 'error') setState(p => ({ ...p, phase: 'error', error: d.message }));
  };

  const reset = () => { setState({ phase: 'upload', agents: initAgents, results: null, settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS }); setIsDemo(false); };

  const handleDemo = () => {
    setIsDemo(true);
    const file = new File([DEMO_TEXT], 'demo_lecture.txt', { type: 'text/plain' });
    handleUpload(file);
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {state.phase === 'upload' && (
          <motion.div key="upload" initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -20 }}>
            <UploadZone settings={state.settings} onSettingsChange={s => setState(p => ({ ...p, settings: s }))} onUpload={handleUpload} onDemo={handleDemo} />
          </motion.div>
        )}
        {(state.phase === 'extracting' || state.phase === 'analyzing') && (
          <motion.div key="analyzing" initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }}>
            <AgentCards agents={state.agents} phase={state.phase} isDemo={isDemo} />
          </motion.div>
        )}
        {state.phase === 'done' && state.results && (
          <motion.div key="results" initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}>
            <ResultsPanel 
              results={state.results} 
              onReset={reset} 
              textLength={state.textLength} 
              analysisDuration={state.analysisDuration} 
              sourceFileName={state.sourceFileName} 
              materialId={state.savedMaterialId} 
            />
            
            {state.savedMaterialId && state.results.podcast && (
              <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.3 }} className="mt-6 bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 to-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 text-white shadow-xl flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 border border-slate-700">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-rose-500/20 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 border border-rose-500/30">
                    <Mic className="w-6 h-6 text-rose-500" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-center sm:text-left">
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-1 flex items-center justify-center sm:justify-start gap-2">AI-Подкаст сгенерирован! <Zap className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400" /></h3>
                    <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm">Слушайте разбор этой темы в аудиоформате.</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => navigate(`/library/${state.savedMaterialId}?mode=express&tab=podcast`)}
                  className="px-6 py-3 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-sm transition w-full sm:w-auto shrink-0 shadow-lg shadow-rose-900/50"
                >
                  Слушать подкаст
                </button>
              </motion.div>
            )}

            <div className="mt-6 text-center">
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate('/library')}
                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition shadow-md flex items-center gap-2 mx-auto"
              >
                <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" /> Перейти в библиотеку
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
        {state.phase === 'error' && (
          <motion.div key="error" initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} className="text-center py-16">
            {state.error?.includes('429') || state.error?.includes('API_LIMIT_REACHED') ? (
              <>
                <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/30 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-500" />
                </div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2">Нейросеть перегружена</h2>
                <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-6 text-sm max-w-sm mx-auto leading-relaxed">
                  Сейчас слишком много студентов анализируют материалы одновременно, и мы достигли лимита запросов к Google AI. Пожалуйста, сделайте паузу и попробуйте через 5 минут!
                </p>
                <button onClick={reset} className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-amber-500 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-sm hover:bg-amber-600 transition shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20">
                  Попробовать снова
                </button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <AlertTriangle className="w-10 h-10 text-rose-500 mx-auto mb-3" />
                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-1">Ошибка</h2>
                <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-4 text-sm">{state.error}</p>
                <button onClick={reset} className="px-5 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-indigo-700 transition">Попробовать снова</button>
              </>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      {/* Toasts */}
      <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <AnimatePresence>
          {toasts.map(t => (
            <motion.div key={t.id}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 40, scale: 0.95 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0, scale: 1 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 40, scale: 0.95 }}
              className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 shadow-lg rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 font-medium">
              {t.text}
            </motion.div>
          ))}
        </AnimatePresence>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Library.tsx
================================================================================
import { Material } from '../types';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Search, BookOpen, Trash2, Plus, Headphones, Globe, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { ru } from 'date-fns/locale';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { useConfirm } from '../contexts/ConfirmContext';
import { getMaterials, deleteMaterial, updateMaterialVisibility } from '../api/materials';
import { getAssignedMaterials, joinGroup } from '../api/groups';
import { CustomSelect } from '../components/CustomSelect';

export default function Library() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { confirm } = useConfirm();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [subjectFilter, setSubjectFilter] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [showJoinModal, setShowJoinModal] = useState(false);
  const [inviteCode, setInviteCode] = useState('');
  const [joinError, setJoinError] = useState('');

  const loadData = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    
    const cached = localStorage.getItem(`library_cache_${user.id}`);
    if (cached) {
      try {
        setMaterials(JSON.parse(cached));
        setLoading(false);
      } catch (e) {}
    } else {
      setLoading(true);
    }
    
    try {
      const [myMats, assignedMats] = await Promise.all([
        getMaterials(user.id),
        getAssignedMaterials(user.id)
      ]);
      // Remove duplicates by ID (if teacher assigned my own material)
      const all = [...myMats, ...assignedMats];
      const unique = Array.from(new Map(all.map(m => [m.id, m])).values());
      setMaterials(unique);
      localStorage.setItem(`library_cache_${user.id}`, JSON.stringify(unique));
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [user?.id]);

  const subjects = [...new Set(materials.map(m => m.subject).filter(Boolean))];

  const filtered = materials.filter(m => {
    const matchSearch = m.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
    const matchSubject = !subjectFilter || m.subject === subjectFilter;
    return matchSearch && matchSubject;
  });

  const handleDelete = async (e: React.MouseEvent, id: string) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (!(await confirm({ title: 'Удаление', message: 'Удалить этот материал навсегда?', type: 'danger' }))) return;
    await deleteMaterial(id);
    setMaterials(prev => prev.filter(m => m.id !== id));
  };

  const handleTogglePublic = async (e: React.MouseEvent, material: any) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const newStatus = !material.is_public;
    await updateMaterialVisibility(material.id, newStatus);
    setMaterials(prev => prev.map(m => m.id === material.id ? { ...m, is_public: newStatus } : m));
  };

  const handleJoinGroup = async () => {
    if (!user || !inviteCode.trim()) return;
    setJoinError('');
    const ok = await joinGroup(user.id, inviteCode.trim());
    if (ok) {
      setShowJoinModal(false);
      setInviteCode('');
      loadData(); // Reload materials to get assignments
    } else {
      setJoinError('Неверный код или вы уже состоите в группе.');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Библиотека знаний</h1>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowJoinModal(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold transition"
          >
            Ввести код класса
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            Новый анализ →
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {showJoinModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm shadow-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-xl text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2">Присоединиться к группе</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-sm mb-4">Введите инвайт-код, который дал вам преподаватель.</p>
            <input 
              autoFocus
              value={inviteCode} 
              onChange={e => setInviteCode(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
              placeholder="A7B-9X2" 
              className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl mb-2 font-mono text-center text-xl uppercase tracking-widest focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100" 
            />
            {joinError && <p className="text-rose-500 text-sm mb-4 text-center">{joinError}</p>}
            <div className="flex gap-2 mt-4">
              <button onClick={() => setShowJoinModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-bold rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700">Отмена</button>
              <button onClick={handleJoinGroup} disabled={!inviteCode.trim()} className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold rounded-xl hover:bg-indigo-700 disabled:opacity-50">Вступить</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 mb-6 relative z-20">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            value={search}
            onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Поиск по названию..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
          />
        </div>
        {subjects.length > 0 && (
          <CustomSelect
            value={subjectFilter}
            onChange={val => setSubjectFilter(val)}
            options={[
              { value: '', label: 'Все предметы' },
              ...subjects.map(s => ({ value: s, label: s }))
            ]}
            className="sm:w-64 px-4 py-2.5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 shadow-sm"
          />
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Loading */}
      {loading && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map(i => (
            <div key={i} className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 animate-pulse">
              <div className="flex gap-2 mb-3">
                <div className="h-4 w-24 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded"></div>
                <div className="h-4 w-16 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded"></div>
              </div>
              <div className="h-6 w-3/4 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded mb-4"></div>
              <div className="flex gap-3 mb-6">
                <div className="h-4 w-10 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded"></div>
                <div className="h-4 w-10 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded"></div>
                <div className="h-4 w-16 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded ml-auto"></div>
              </div>
              <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/60 flex justify-between">
                <div className="h-3 w-20 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded"></div>
                <div className="h-3 w-16 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded"></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Empty */}
      {!loading && materials.length === 0 && (
        <div className="text-center py-20">
          <BookOpen className="w-16 h-16 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-700 mx-auto mb-4" />
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">Библиотека пуста</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 text-sm mb-6">Загрузите первую лекцию, чтобы начать</p>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')}
            className="px-5 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition"
          >
            Загрузить первый материал
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Grid */}
      {!loading && filtered.length > 0 && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {filtered.map(m => {
            const fcCount = m.flashcards_json?.flashcards?.length || 0;
            const qCount = m.quiz_json?.quiz?.length || 0;
            const hasPodcast = m.summary_json?.podcast?.length > 0;
            return (
              <div
                key={m.id}
                onClick={() => navigate('/library/' + m.id)}
                className="card p-5 cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition group"
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-2">
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mb-1.5">
                      {(m as any).is_assigned && (
                        <span className="inline-block px-2 py-0.5 bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-[10px] font-bold rounded uppercase tracking-wider">
                          От преподавателя
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {m.subject && (
                        <span className="inline-block px-2 py-0.5 bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 text-[10px] font-bold rounded uppercase tracking-wider truncate max-w-[150px]">
                          {m.subject}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {!(m as any).is_assigned && (
                        <button
                          onClick={e => handleTogglePublic(e, m)}
                          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold rounded uppercase tracking-wider transition ${
                            m.is_public
                              ? 'bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/30 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/40'
                              : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 hover:bg-indigo-50 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950/30 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-400 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:border-indigo-200 dark:hover:border-indigo-800/40'
                          }`}
                          title={m.is_public ? 'Опубликовано в Study Network. Нажмите, чтобы сделать приватным.' : 'Опубликовать в Study Network'}
                        >
                          {m.is_public ? (
                            <><Globe className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" /> В сети</>
                          ) : (
                            <><Lock className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500" /> Поделиться</>
                          )}
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 leading-snug line-clamp-2 break-words">{m.title}</h3>
                  </div>
                  {!(m as any).is_assigned && (
                    <button
                      onClick={e => handleDelete(e, m.id)}
                      className="shrink-0 p-1.5 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 hover:text-rose-500 dark:hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/30 rounded-lg transition opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-3">
                  <span>📝</span>
                  <span>🃏 {fcCount}</span>
                  <span>❓ {qCount}</span>
                  {hasPodcast && (
                    <span className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 font-bold bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                      <Headphones className="w-3 h-3" /> Подкаст
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-auto pt-2 border-t border-slate-50 dark:border-slate-800/60">
                  <div className="flex flex-col">
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
                      {format(new Date(m.created_at), 'dd.MM.yyyy', { locale: ru })}
                    </p>
                    {(m as any).assigned_deadline && (
                      <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-rose-500 mt-0.5">
                        Дедлайн: {format(new Date((m as any).assigned_deadline), 'dd.MM, HH:mm', { locale: ru })}
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  {hasPodcast && (
                    <button
                      onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); navigate(`/library/${m.id}?mode=express&tab=podcast`); }}
                      className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 hover:text-indigo-800 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 font-bold transition opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
                    >
                      <Headphones className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Слушать →
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* No results for filter */}
      {!loading && materials.length > 0 && filtered.length === 0 && (
        <p className="text-center text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 py-10">Ничего не найдено</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/MaterialDetail.tsx
================================================================================
import { Material } from '../types';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Edit3, Check, X, Globe, Lock, Headphones, Zap, BookOpen, Users } from 'lucide-react';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { ru } from 'date-fns/locale';
import ResultsPanel from '../components/ResultsPanel';
import ExpressPrepView from '../components/ExpressPrepView';
import { getMaterialById, updateMaterialSubject, updateMaterialVisibility } from '../api/materials';

export default function MaterialDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const [material, setMaterial] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [editingSubject, setEditingSubject] = useState(false);
  const [subjectInput, setSubjectInput] = useState('');
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'full' | 'express' | 'podcast'>(  
    searchParams.get('mode') === 'express' 
      ? (searchParams.get('tab') === 'podcast' ? 'podcast' : 'express')
      : 'full'
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!id) return;
    getMaterialById(id)
      .then(data => {
        setMaterial(data);
        setSubjectInput(data.subject || '');
      })
      .catch(console.error)
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [id]);

  const saveSubject = async () => {
    if (!id) return;
    await updateMaterialSubject(id, subjectInput);
    setMaterial((prev: Material) => ({ ...prev, subject: subjectInput }));
    setEditingSubject(false);
  };

  const togglePublic = async () => {
    if (!id || !material) return;
    const newStatus = !material.is_public;
    await updateMaterialVisibility(id, newStatus);
    setMaterial((prev: Material) => ({ ...prev, is_public: newStatus }));
  };

  if (loading) return (
    <div className="p-6 flex items-center justify-center">
      <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
    </div>
  );

  if (!material) return (
    <div className="p-6 text-center">
      <p className="text-slate-400">Материал не найден</p>
      <button onClick={() => navigate('/library')} className="mt-4 text-indigo-600 text-sm hover:underline">← Библиотека</button>
    </div>
  );

  const hasFlashcards = material.flashcards_json?.flashcards?.length > 0;
  const hasPodcast = material.summary_json?.podcast?.length > 0;

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
      {/* Back */}
      <button
        onClick={() => navigate('/library')}
        className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-slate-200 text-sm mb-4 transition"
      >
        <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
        Библиотека
      </button>

      {/* Title & Subject */}
      <div className="mb-2">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 break-words">{material.title}</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-2">
        {editingSubject ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <input
              value={subjectInput}
              onChange={e => setSubjectInput(e.target.value)}
              className="px-3 py-1 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500"
              placeholder="Предмет"
              autoFocus
            />
            <button onClick={saveSubject} className="text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-700"><Check className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
            <button onClick={() => { setEditingSubject(false); setSubjectInput(material.subject || ''); }} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <button
            onClick={() => setEditingSubject(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm"
          >
            {material.subject ? (
              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 rounded-md">{material.subject}</span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1"><Edit3 className="w-3 h-3" />Добавить предмет</span>
            )}
          </button>
        )}

        <div className="w-px h-4 bg-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-700 hidden sm:block" />

        <button 
          onClick={togglePublic}
          className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md transition ${
            material.is_public 
              ? 'bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/30 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 hover:bg-emerald-200 dark:hover:bg-emerald-950/50' 
              : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
          }`}
        >
          {material.is_public ? (
            <><Globe className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> В Маркетплейсе</>
          ) : (
            <><Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Только для меня</>
          )}
        </button>

        <button 
          onClick={() => navigate(`/co-study/${id}`)}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/30 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/50 rounded-md transition"
        >
          <Users className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Совместная комната
        </button>
      </div>

      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 mb-6">
        {format(new Date(material.created_at), 'dd.MM.yyyy', { locale: ru })}
      </p>

      {/* Режимы обучения */}
      <div className={`grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 bg-slate-100/80 dark:bg-slate-900/80 p-1.5 rounded-2xl w-full mb-6 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 sm:grid-cols-2 ${hasPodcast && hasFlashcards ? 'md:grid-cols-4' : hasPodcast || hasFlashcards ? 'md:grid-cols-3' : ''}`}>
        <button
          onClick={() => setMode('full')}
          className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 sm:py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition ${
            mode === 'full'
              ? 'bg-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-800 text-white shadow-md'
              : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-200 hover:bg-white/50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/50'
          }`}
        >
          <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5 sm:w-4 sm:h-4" /> Полное обучение
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => setMode('express')}
          className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 sm:py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition ${
            mode === 'express'
              ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white shadow-md'
              : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 dark:hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-white/50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/50'
          }`}
        >
          <Zap className="w-5 h-5 sm:w-4 sm:h-4" /> За 5 минут
        </button>
        {hasPodcast && (
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('podcast')}
            className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 sm:py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition ${
              mode === 'podcast'
                ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-md'
                : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-indigo-600 dark:hover:text-indigo-400 hover:bg-white/50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/50'
            }`}
          >
            <Headphones className="w-5 h-5 sm:w-4 sm:h-4" /> AI-Подкаст
          </button>
        )}
        {hasFlashcards && (
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate(`/shorts/${id}`)}
            className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 sm:py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-white bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-fuchsia-600 hover:from-violet-500 hover:to-fuchsia-500 shadow-lg shadow-fuchsia-500/20 transition-transform hover:-translate-y-0.5 sm:col-span-full md:col-auto"
          >
            Учебный TikTok 📱
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Контент в зависимости от выбранного режима */}
      {mode === 'full' ? (
        <ResultsPanel
          results={{
            summarizer: material.summary_json,
            flashcards: material.flashcards_json,
            quiz: material.quiz_json,
          }}
          onReset={() => navigate('/library')}
          textLength={material.original_text?.length || material.summary_json?.content?.length || 0}
          materialId={material.id}
        />
      ) : mode === 'podcast' ? (
        <ExpressPrepView 
          key="podcast" 
          materialId={material.id}
          originalText={material.original_text || ''}
          summaryJson={{ ...material.summary_json, _startTab: 'podcast' }} 
          onUpdate={(updatedFields) => {
            setMaterial((prev: any) => {
              if (!prev) return prev;
              return {
                ...prev,
                ...updatedFields,
                summary_json: {
                  ...prev.summary_json,
                  ...(updatedFields.summary_json || {})
                }
              };
            });
          }}
        />
      ) : (
        <ExpressPrepView 
          key="express" 
          materialId={material.id}
          originalText={material.original_text || ''}
          summaryJson={material.summary_json} 
          onUpdate={(updatedFields) => {
            setMaterial((prev: any) => {
              if (!prev) return prev;
              return {
                ...prev,
                ...updatedFields,
                summary_json: {
                  ...prev.summary_json,
                  ...(updatedFields.summary_json || {})
                }
              };
            });
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* Review CTA */}
      {hasFlashcards && mode === 'full' && (
        <div className="mt-6 text-center">
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/review')}
            className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition"
          >
            🧠 Начать повторение карточек
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Review.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { CheckCircle2, Zap } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { useSound } from '../hooks/useSound';
import { getDueCards, updateCardProgress } from '../api/flashcards';
import { updateQuestProgress, recordStudyActivity } from '../api/quests';

function CircularProgress({ value, size = 100, strokeWidth = 8, isDark = false }: { value: number; size?: number; strokeWidth?: number; isDark?: boolean }) {
  const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const offset = circumference - (value / 100) * circumference;
  const color = value >= 80 ? '#10b981' : value >= 50 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
  return (
    <svg width={size} height={size} className="transform -rotate-90">
      <circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke={isDark ? '#334155' : '#e2e8f0'} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />
      <circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        strokeLinecap="round" strokeDasharray={circumference} strokeDashoffset={offset} />
    </svg>
  );
}

export default function Review() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { isDark } = (window as any).__useTheme?.() ?? { isDark: document.documentElement.classList.contains('dark') };
  const { playFlipSound, playSuccessSound } = useSound();
  const [cards, setCards] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [idx, setIdx] = useState(0);
  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState(false);
  const [sessionStats, setSessionStats] = useState({ correct: 0, wrong: 0 });
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const [processing, setProcessing] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const loadCards = () => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    setLoading(true);
    getDueCards(user.id)
      .then(data => { setCards(data); setIdx(0); setRevealed(false); setSessionStats({ correct: 0, wrong: 0 }); setDone(false); })
      .catch(console.error)
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  };

  useEffect(() => { loadCards(); }, [user?.id]);

  const handleReveal = () => {
    if (!revealed) {
      playFlipSound();
      setRevealed(true);
    }
  };

  const handleAnswer = async (quality: number) => {
    if (processing || !cards[idx]) return;
    setProcessing(true);
    try {
      await updateCardProgress(cards[idx].id, quality);
      if (user?.id) {
        await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'review', 1);
        await recordStudyActivity(user.id, 1, 0);
      }
      if (quality >= 3) setSessionStats(p => ({ ...p, correct: p.correct + 1 }));
      else setSessionStats(p => ({ ...p, wrong: p.wrong + 1 }));

      if (idx + 1 >= cards.length) {
        playSuccessSound();
        setDone(true);
      }
      else { setIdx(p => p + 1); setRevealed(false); }
    } finally {
      setProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 flex items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh]">
      <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
    </div>
  );

  // No cards due
  if (cards.length === 0) return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 text-center py-10 md:py-20">
      <CheckCircle2 className="w-16 h-16 text-emerald-500 mx-auto mb-4" />
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2">Отличная работа!</h2>
      <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-6">На сегодня карточек нет. Приходите завтра!</p>
      <div className="flex justify-center gap-3 flex-wrap">
        <button onClick={() => navigate('/library')} className="px-5 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
          В библиотеку
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')} className="px-5 py-2 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
          Новый анализ
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  // Session done
  if (done) {
    const total = sessionStats.correct + sessionStats.wrong;
    const pct = total > 0 ? Math.round((sessionStats.correct / total) * 100) : 0;
    const darkMode = document.documentElement.classList.contains('dark');
    return (
      <div className="p-4 md:p-6 text-center py-10 md:py-16">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-6">Сессия завершена! 🎉</h2>
        <div className="relative inline-block mb-4">
          <CircularProgress value={pct} size={120} strokeWidth={10} isDark={darkMode} />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
            <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100">{pct}%</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Правильно: <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">{sessionStats.correct}</span> из {total}</p>
        <p className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 text-sm mb-8">Карточки будут показаны снова по расписанию spaced repetition</p>
        <div className="flex justify-center gap-3 flex-wrap">
          <button onClick={loadCards} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
            <Zap className="w-4 h-4" />Повторить ещё
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')} className="px-5 py-2 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
            На дашборд
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const card = cards[idx];

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
      {/* Progress bar */}
      <div className="mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2">
          <span>{idx + 1} / {cards.length} карточек</span>
          <span>{sessionStats.correct} ✓ · {sessionStats.wrong} ✗</span>
        </div>
        <div className="h-2 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
          <div className="h-full bg-indigo-500 rounded-full transition-all" style={{ width: `${((idx) / cards.length) * 100}%` }} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Card */}
      <motion.div
        key={idx}
        initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 30 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
        className="card min-h-48 p-6 md:p-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] transition-transform"
        onClick={handleReveal}
      >
        <span className="text-xs uppercase text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 tracking-wider mb-3">Вопрос</span>
