        <p className="text-lg md:text-xl font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 text-center mb-6">{card.question}</p>

        {revealed && (
          <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} className="w-full border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 pt-6">
            <span className="text-xs uppercase text-emerald-500 tracking-wider mb-2 block text-center">Ответ</span>
            <p className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-center">{card.answer}</p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {!revealed && (
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-600 mt-4">Нажмите, чтобы открыть ответ</p>
        )}
      </motion.div>

      {/* Actions */}
      <div className="mt-6">
        {!revealed ? (
          <button
            onClick={handleReveal}
            className="w-full h-14 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 active:bg-indigo-800 text-white rounded-xl font-semibold transition text-base"
          >
            Показать ответ
          </button>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 md:gap-3">
            <button
              onClick={() => handleAnswer(1)}
              className="h-14 md:h-12 bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/40 hover:bg-rose-100 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/60 border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-900/50 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 rounded-xl font-medium transition text-sm"
            >
              Не знал 😕
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => handleAnswer(3)}
              className="h-14 md:h-12 bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 hover:bg-amber-100 dark:hover:bg-amber-950/60 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-900/50 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 rounded-xl font-medium transition text-sm"
            >
              Затруднялся 🤔
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => handleAnswer(5)}
              className="h-14 md:h-12 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 hover:bg-emerald-100 dark:hover:bg-emerald-950/60 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-900/50 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 rounded-xl font-medium transition text-sm"
            >
              Знал! 😎
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Planner.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Calendar, Trash2, Sparkles, CheckSquare, Square, Info, X, Clock, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { format, differenceInDays } from 'date-fns';
import { ru } from 'date-fns/locale';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { useConfirm } from '../contexts/ConfirmContext';
import { checkAILimit, incrementAIUsage } from '../api/limits';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';

export default function Planner() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { alert } = useConfirm();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [subjects, setSubjects] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedMaterials, setSelectedMaterials] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedSubjectIds, setSelectedSubjectIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [goals, setGoals] = useState('');
  const [plan, setPlan] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [hours, setHours] = useState(2);
  const [newName, setNewName] = useState('');
  const [newDate, setNewDate] = useState('');
  const [newColor, setNewColor] = useState('#4F46E5');
  const [historyPlans, setHistoryPlans] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedSubject, setSelectedSubject] = useState<string>('');
  const [selectedDay, setSelectedDay] = useState<any>(null);
  const [dateError, setDateError] = useState('');

  const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

  const loadData = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    const { data: sub } = await supabase.from('subjects').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).order('exam_date');
    const { data: mat } = await supabase.from('materials').select('id, title, summary_json').eq('user_id', user.id);
    const { data: plans } = await supabase.from('study_plans').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    
    const subList = sub || [];
    const plansList = plans || [];
    setSubjects(subList);
    setMaterials(mat || []);
    setHistoryPlans(plansList);
    
    if (subList.length === 0) {
      setPlan([]);
      setHistoryPlans([]);
      setSelectedSubjectIds([]);
      setSelectedSubject('');
      return;
    }

    // НЕ выбираем автоматически — пользователь должен сам отметить нужные
    // Только если ровно 1 предмет и ничего не выбрано — выбираем его
    if (subList.length === 1 && selectedSubjectIds.length === 0) {
      setSelectedSubjectIds([subList[0].id]);
    }

    if (plansList.length > 0 && plan.length === 0) {
      setPlan(plansList[0].plan_json);
      setGoals(plansList[0].notes || '');
    } else if (plansList.length === 0) {
      setPlan([]);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => { loadData(); }, [user?.id]);

  const addSubject = async () => {
    if (!newName || !newDate) return;
    if (newDate < todayStr) {
      setDateError('Дата экзамена не может быть в прошлом');
      return;
    }
    setDateError('');
    const { data, error } = await supabase.from('subjects').insert({ user_id: user!.id, name: newName, exam_date: newDate, color: newColor }).select().single();
    if (!error && data) {
      setSelectedSubjectIds(prev => [...prev, data.id]);
    }
    setNewName('');
    setNewDate('');
    loadData();
  };

  const deleteSubject = async (id: string) => {
    const subToDelete = subjects.find(s => s.id === id);
    await supabase.from('subjects').delete().eq('id', id);
    
    if (subToDelete?.name === selectedSubject) {
      setSelectedSubject('');
    }
    
    setSelectedSubjectIds(prev => prev.filter(sid => sid !== id));
    loadData();
  };

  const handleGeneratePlan = async () => {
    if (selectedSubjectIds.length === 0) {
      await alert({ title: 'Выберите предмет', message: 'Выберите хотя бы один предмет для планирования', type: 'warning' });
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);

    if (user?.id) {
      const limitCheck = await checkAILimit(user.id, 5);
      if (!limitCheck.allowed) {
        await alert({ title: 'Энергия ИИ исчерпана', message: 'Не хватает энергии ИИ (нужно 5). Перейдите на PRO-тариф!', type: 'danger' });
        navigate('/pricing');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      await incrementAIUsage(user.id, 5);
    }

    try {
      const selectedContent = materials
        .filter(m => selectedMaterials.includes(m.id))
        .map(m => `Документ "${m.title}": ${m.summary_json?.content || ''}`)
        .join('\n\n');

      const selectedSubs = subjects.filter(s => selectedSubjectIds.includes(s.id));

      // Получаем актуальное настроение пользователя
      const { data: moodData } = await supabase
        .from('mood_log')
        .select('mood, energy_level, notes')
        .eq('user_id', user!.id)
        .order('logged_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(1)
        .maybeSingle();

      let moodInfo = "";
      if (moodData) {
          moodInfo = `\nСОСТОЯНИЕ СТУДЕНТА: настроение ${moodData.mood}, энергия ${moodData.energy_level}/5. `;
          if (moodData.energy_level <= 2) moodInfo += "Студент очень устал, планируй максимально легкие и короткие задачи.";
          else if (moodData.energy_level >= 4) moodInfo += "Студент полон сил, можно планировать интенсивную работу.";
      }

      const res = await fetch('/api/plan', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          subjects: selectedSubs.map(s => ({ name: s.name, exam_date: s.exam_date, color: s.color })),
          available_hours_per_day: hours,
          context_materials: selectedContent,
          user_goals: goals + moodInfo
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      const generatedPlan = data.plan || [];
      setPlan(generatedPlan);

      if (user && generatedPlan.length > 0) {
        const subNames = selectedSubs.map(s => s.name).join(', ');
        await supabase.from('study_plans').insert({
          user_id: user.id,
          title: `${subNames.length > 20 ? subNames.slice(0, 20) + '...' : subNames} (${format(new Date(), 'dd.MM')})`,
          plan_json: generatedPlan,
          notes: goals
        });
        loadData(); // Reload history
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error generating/saving plan:', err);
      setPlan([]);
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const toggleMaterial = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedMaterials(prev => 
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter(x => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
    );
  };

  const toggleSubjectSelect = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedSubjectIds(prev => 
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter(x => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
    );
  };

  const getColorForSubject = (name: string) => {
    const s = subjects.find((s: { name: string; color: string }) => s.name === name);
    return s?.color || '#4F46E5';
  };

  // Filter plan by selected subject and ensure subject still exists
  const filteredPlan = plan.map(day => ({
    ...day,
    tasks: day.tasks?.filter((t: { subject: string }) => {
      const exists = subjects.some(s => s.name === t.subject);
      if (!exists) return false;
      return selectedSubject ? t.subject === selectedSubject : true;
    }) || []
  })).filter(day => day.tasks.length > 0);

  const subjectTaskCounts: Record<string, number> = {};
  plan.forEach(day => {
    day.tasks?.forEach((t: { subject: string; hours?: number }) => {
      subjectTaskCounts[t.subject] = (subjectTaskCounts[t.subject] || 0) + 1;
    });
  });

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6">
        <Calendar className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600" />
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Персональный планировщик</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-4 md:gap-6">
        <div className="lg:col-span-3 space-y-6">
          <div className="card p-4 md:p-5">
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" /> Экзамены
            </h2>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 mb-3">Отметьте галочкой предметы для плана</p>
            <div className="space-y-3 mb-4">
              {subjects.map(s => {
                const taskCount = subjectTaskCounts[s.name] || 0;
                const isSelected = selectedSubject === s.name;
                const isPlanned = selectedSubjectIds.includes(s.id);
                return (
                  <div key={s.id} className="group relative">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setSelectedSubject(isSelected ? '' : s.name)}
                      className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between text-sm p-2 rounded-lg transition border ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-900/50 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 font-bold'
                          : 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border-transparent hover:border-slate-200 dark:hover:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-hidden">
                        <div 
                          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSubjectSelect(s.id); }}
                          className={`w-4 h-4 rounded border flex items-center justify-center transition ${
                            isPlanned ? 'bg-indigo-600 border-indigo-600 text-white' : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-600'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {isPlanned && <CheckSquare className="w-3 h-3" />}
                        </div>
                        <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0" style={{ backgroundColor: s.color }} />
                        <span className="font-medium truncate">{s.name}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        {taskCount > 0 && (
                          <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 rounded text-[10px]">{taskCount}</span>
                        )}
                        <button 
                          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); deleteSubject(s.id); }} 
                          className="text-slate-300 hover:text-rose-500 transition opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <input value={newName} onChange={e => setNewName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Название предмета" className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500" />
              <div>
                <input 
                  type="date" 
                  value={newDate} 
                  min={todayStr}
                  onChange={e => { setNewDate(e.target.value); setDateError(''); }} 
                  className={`w-full px-3 py-1.5 border rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 ${dateError ? 'border-rose-300 dark:border-rose-700' : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700'}`} 
                />
                {dateError && <p className="text-rose-500 text-[10px] mt-1">{dateError}</p>}
              </div>
              <button onClick={addSubject} className="w-full py-2 bg-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium hover:bg-slate-900 dark:hover:bg-slate-600 transition">Добавить предмет</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="card p-4 md:p-5">
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" /> Часы в день
            </h2>
            <input type="number" min={1} max={12} value={hours} onChange={e => setHours(Number(e.target.value))} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100" />
          </div>

          {historyPlans.length > 0 && (
            <div className="card p-4 md:p-5">
              <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" /> История планов
              </h2>
              <div className="space-y-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto pr-1 custom-scrollbar">
                {historyPlans.map(p => (
                  <div key={p.id} className="group relative">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => { setPlan(p.plan_json); setGoals(p.notes || ''); }}
                      className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs transition border ${
                        plan === p.plan_json 
                        ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-900/50 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 font-bold' 
                        : 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border-transparent text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 hover:border-slate-200 dark:hover:border-slate-700'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {p.title}
                    </button>
                    <button 
                      onClick={async () => {
                        await supabase.from('study_plans').delete().eq('id', p.id);
                        loadData();
                      }}
                      className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-300 hover:text-rose-500 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-9 space-y-6">
          <div className="card p-4 md:p-6">
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-500" /> На чем основывать план?
            </h2>
            
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-4">Выберите материалы из библиотеки, которые нужно изучить:</p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3 mb-6 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto pr-2 custom-scrollbar">
              {materials.map(m => (
                <div 
                  key={m.id} 
                  onClick={() => toggleMaterial(m.id)}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border cursor-pointer transition ${
                    selectedMaterials.includes(m.id) 
                    ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-900/50 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300' 
                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800/50 border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-600'
                  }`}
                >
                  {selectedMaterials.includes(m.id) ? <CheckSquare className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" /> : <Square className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />}
                  <span className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{m.title}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
              {materials.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 col-span-2 italic">В библиотеке пока пусто</p>}
            </div>

            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Info className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" /> Ваши цели и пожелания
            </h2>
            <textarea 
              value={goals}
              onChange={e => setGoals(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Например: 'Я плохо знаю тему массивов, удели ей больше времени' или 'Мне нужно выучить только теорию для зачета'"
              className="w-full h-24 px-4 py-3 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition resize-none mb-6 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
            />

            <button
              onClick={handleGeneratePlan}
              disabled={loading || selectedSubjectIds.length === 0}
              className="w-full py-3 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-xl font-semibold transition disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              {loading ? <div className="w-5 h-5 border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin" /> : <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5" />}
              {loading ? 'Создаем персональный план...' : selectedSubjectIds.length === 0 ? 'Выберите предметы для плана' : `Сформировать план (${selectedSubjectIds.length} предм.)`}
            </button>
          </div>

          {plan.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <h2 className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 text-lg">Ваш план подготовки</h2>
                {selectedSubject && (
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => setSelectedSubject('')}
                    className="text-xs text-indigo-600 hover:underline"
                  >
                    Показать все предметы
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                {filteredPlan.map((day: { date: string; tasks: any[] }, i: number) => {
                  const totalHours = day.tasks?.reduce((sum: number, t: { hours?: number }) => sum + (t.hours || 0), 0) || 0;
                  return (
                    <motion.div 
                      key={i} 
                      layoutId={`day-${day.date}`}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedDay(day)}
                      className="card p-4 md:p-5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-l-4 border-l-indigo-500 flex flex-col cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition-shadow group"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 shrink-0">
                        <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 capitalize text-sm group-hover:text-indigo-600 dark:group-hover:text-indigo-400 transition-colors">
                          {format(new Date(day.date + 'T12:00:00'), 'EEEE, d MMMM', { locale: ru })}
                        </h3>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full">
                          <Clock className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" />
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 whitespace-nowrap">{totalHours}ч</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="space-y-3 flex-1">
                        {day.tasks?.map((task: { subject: string; task: string; hours?: number }, j: number) => (
                          <div key={j} className="flex items-start gap-2.5 text-xs">
                            <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full mt-1.5 shrink-0" style={{ backgroundColor: getColorForSubject(task.subject) }} />
                            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                              <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 truncate block">{task.subject}</span>
                              <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed line-clamp-3">{task.task}</p>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </motion.div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Modal for Day Details */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {selectedDay && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[110] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
            <motion.div 
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
              onClick={() => setSelectedDay(null)}
              className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-sm"
            />
            <motion.div 
              initial={{ scale: 0.9, opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ scale: 0.9, opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-[calc(100%-32px)] md:w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-hidden relative z-10 flex flex-col"
            >
              <div className="p-6 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 sticky top-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Calendar className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 capitalize">
                      {format(new Date(selectedDay.date + 'T12:00:00'), 'EEEE, d MMMM', { locale: ru })}
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Подробный план на день</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button 
                  onClick={() => setSelectedDay(null)}
                  className="p-2 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 rounded-full transition text-slate-400"
                >
                  <X className="w-6 h-6" />
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-6">
                {selectedDay.tasks?.map((task: { subject: string; task: string; hours?: number }, idx: number) => (
                  <div key={idx} className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 rounded-2xl p-4 md:p-5 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: getColorForSubject(task.subject) }} />
                        <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 text-lg break-words line-clamp-2">{task.subject}</h3>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                          <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
                          <span className="text-sm font-bold">{task.hours}ч</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex gap-4">
                      <div className="flex-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
                          <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
                          <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Задача на день</span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 shadow-sm">
                          {task.task}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 text-center">
                <button 
                  onClick={() => setSelectedDay(null)}
                  className="px-6 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-semibold hover:bg-indigo-700 transition shadow-lg shadow-indigo-100"
                >
                  Понятно
                </button>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          </div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Chat.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { MessageSquare, Send, Trash2, Maximize2, Minimize2 } from 'lucide-react';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkMath from 'remark-math';
import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex';
import 'katex/dist/katex.min.css';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { CustomSelect } from '../components/CustomSelect';
import { useConfirm } from '../contexts/ConfirmContext';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';
import { updateQuestProgress } from '../api/quests';
import { checkAILimit, incrementAIUsage } from '../api/limits';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

export default function Chat() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { confirm } = useConfirm();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState('');
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<{ role: string; content: string }[]>([]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [fetchingHistory, setFetchingHistory] = useState(false);
  const [isFullScreen, setIsFullScreen] = useState(false);
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const prevMsgCount = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    getMaterials(user.id).then(setMaterials).catch(console.error);
  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [user?.id]);

  // Загрузка истории при выборе материала
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedId || !user) {
      setMessages([]);
      return;
    }

    const loadHistory = async () => {
      setFetchingHistory(true);
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('chat_messages')
        .select('role, content')
        .eq('material_id', selectedId)
        .eq('user_id', user.id)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: true });
      
      if (!error && data) {
        setMessages(data);
        prevMsgCount.current = data.length;
      }
      setFetchingHistory(false);
    };

    loadHistory();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [selectedId, user?.id]);

  // Auto-scroll only when a NEW message is added (count increases)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (messages.length > prevMsgCount.current) {
      messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    } else if (messages.length > 0 && prevMsgCount.current === 0) {
      // First load of history — instant scroll
      messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'auto' });
    }
    prevMsgCount.current = messages.length;
  }, [messages]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isFullScreen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      return () => { document.body.style.overflow = ''; };
    }
  }, [isFullScreen]);

  const selectedMaterial = materials.find(m => m.id === selectedId);
  
  // Контекст: берем либо оригинал, либо конспект (что есть)
  const getContext = () => {
    if (!selectedMaterial) return '';
    const original = selectedMaterial.original_text || '';
    const summary = typeof selectedMaterial.summary_json === 'string' 
      ? selectedMaterial.summary_json 
      : (selectedMaterial.summary_json?.content || '');
    
    // Если есть оригинал — берем его (он полнее), иначе конспект
    return (original.length > 50 ? original : summary).slice(0, 30000);
  };

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!input.trim() || !selectedId || !user || loading) return;
    
    // Проверка лимитов (1 токен за сообщение)
    const limitCheck = await checkAILimit(user.id, 1);
    if (!limitCheck.allowed) {
      toast.error('Не хватает энергии ИИ (нужно 1). Перейдите на PRO-тариф!');
      navigate('/pricing');
      return;
    }
    await incrementAIUsage(user.id, 1);
    
    const userMsg = { role: 'user', content: input };
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, userMsg]);
    setInput('');
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      // 1. Сохраняем сообщение пользователя в БД
      await supabase.from('chat_messages').insert({
        user_id: user.id,
        material_id: selectedId,
        role: 'user',
        content: userMsg.content
      });

      await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'chat', 1);

      // Получаем актуальное настроение пользователя
      const { data: moodData } = await supabase
        .from('mood_log')
        .select('mood, energy_level, notes')
        .eq('user_id', user.id)
        .order('logged_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(1)
        .maybeSingle();

      // 2. Запрос к AI
      const res = await fetch('/api/chat', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          message: userMsg.content, 
          context: getContext(), 
          history: messages.slice(-6), // Отправляем последние 6 сообщений для памяти
          extra_params: moodData ? { mood: moodData } : {}
        }),
      });
      
      const data = await res.json();
      const assistantMsg = { role: 'assistant', content: data.reply };
      
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, assistantMsg]);

      // 3. Сохраняем ответ AI в БД
      await supabase.from('chat_messages').insert({
        user_id: user.id,
        material_id: selectedId,
        role: 'assistant',
        content: assistantMsg.content
      });

    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'assistant', content: 'Ошибка при обращении к AI. Попробуйте снова.' }]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleClearHistory = async () => {
    if (!selectedId || !user) return;
    const ok = await confirm({ title: 'Очистить историю', message: 'Вы уверены, что хотите очистить историю чата?', type: 'danger' });
    if (!ok) return;
    await supabase.from('chat_messages').delete().eq('material_id', selectedId).eq('user_id', user.id);
    setMessages([]);
  };

  const chatContent = (
    <div className={`flex flex-col transition-all duration-300 ${
      isFullScreen 
        ? 'fixed inset-0 z-[200] bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 p-4 md:p-8 h-screen w-screen' 
        : 'h-full p-4 md:p-6 bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-transparent'
    }`}>
      {/* Header */}
      <div className={`flex items-center justify-between mb-4 ${isFullScreen ? 'max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full' : ''}`}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-950/50 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 truncate">AI-чат</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate">Задавайте вопросы по вашим материалам</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
          <button 
            onClick={() => setIsFullScreen(!isFullScreen)}
            className="p-2 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 hover:text-indigo-600 dark:hover:text-indigo-400 transition rounded-lg hover:bg-indigo-50 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950/30"
            title={isFullScreen ? "Уменьшить" : "На весь экран"}
          >
            {isFullScreen ? <Minimize2 className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Maximize2 className="w-5 h-5" />}
          </button>
          
          {messages.length > 0 && (
            <button onClick={handleClearHistory} className="p-2 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 hover:text-rose-500 dark:hover:text-rose-400 transition rounded-lg hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/30" title="Очистить чат">
              <Trash2 className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Material select */}
      <div className={`mb-4 relative z-20 ${isFullScreen ? 'max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full' : ''}`}>
        <CustomSelect
          value={selectedId}
          onChange={val => setSelectedId(val)}
          options={[
            { value: '', label: 'Выберите материал...' },
            ...materials.map(m => ({ value: m.id, label: m.title }))
          ]}
          className="md:w-72 px-4 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 shadow-sm text-sm"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Chat area */}
      <div className={`flex-1 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col relative ${isFullScreen ? 'max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full h-full' : ''}`}>
        {!selectedId ? (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-8">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
              <MessageSquare className="w-8 h-8 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 font-semibold mb-1">Чат не выбран</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-sm max-w-xs">Выберите лекцию из списка выше, чтобы начать обсуждение с AI</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4 custom-scrollbar">
              {fetchingHistory ? (
                <div className="flex justify-center py-8">
                  <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
                </div>
              ) : messages.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center py-12">
                  <div className="inline-block px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 rounded-full text-xs font-medium mb-3">
                    Конспект загружен в память
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 text-sm">
                    Спросите что-нибудь о «{selectedMaterial?.title}»
                  </p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                messages.map((msg, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className={`flex ${msg.role === 'user' ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}>
                    <div
                      className={`max-w-[85%] md:max-w-[75%] lg:max-w-[65%] p-3.5 text-sm rounded-2xl shadow-sm ${
                        msg.role === 'user'
                          ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-tr-none'
                          : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 rounded-tl-none'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className={`markdown-content ${msg.role === 'user' ? 'white-text' : ''}`}>
                        <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkMath]} rehypePlugins={[rehypeKatex]}>{msg.content}</ReactMarkdown>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
              {loading && (
                <div className="flex justify-start">
                  <div className="bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-none p-4 flex gap-1.5 shadow-sm">
                    <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-slate-400 dark:bg-slate-500 rounded-full animate-bounce" style={{ animationDelay: '0ms' }} />
                    <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-slate-400 dark:bg-slate-500 rounded-full animate-bounce" style={{ animationDelay: '150ms' }} />
                    <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-slate-400 dark:bg-slate-500 rounded-full animate-bounce" style={{ animationDelay: '300ms' }} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
              <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
            </div>

            {/* Input */}
            <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700">
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <input
                  value={input}
                  onChange={e => setInput(e.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); handleSend(); } }}
                  placeholder="Напишите вопрос по материалу... (1 ⚡ ИИ)"
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition shadow-sm bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
                  disabled={loading}
                />
                <button
                  onClick={handleSend}
                  disabled={loading || !input.trim()}
                  className="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white p-3 rounded-xl transition disabled:opacity-50 shadow-md shadow-indigo-200 flex-shrink-0"
                >
                  <Send className="w-5 h-5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return isFullScreen ? createPortal(chatContent, document.body) : chatContent;
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Missions.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Gamepad2, BookOpen, AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, ChevronRight, ChevronDown, HelpCircle, Trophy, Sparkles, X, ArrowRight, Lock, Unlock } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkMath from 'remark-math';
import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex';
import 'katex/dist/katex.min.css';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { useConfirm } from '../contexts/ConfirmContext';
import { CustomSelect } from '../components/CustomSelect';
import { getMaterials, updateMaterialMissions } from '../api/materials';
import { updateQuestProgress, checkAndAwardAchievements, recordStudyActivity } from '../api/quests';
import ConfettiCelebration from '../components/ConfettiCelebration';
import AchievementToast from '../components/AchievementToast';
import type { Achievement } from '../components/AchievementToast';
import { checkAILimit, incrementAIUsage } from '../api/limits';

interface Mission {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  explanation: string;
  question: string;
  options: string[];
  correct: string;
  hint: string;
}

export default function Missions() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { confirm, alert } = useConfirm();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const queryMaterialId = searchParams.get('materialId');

  // Восстановление состояния из sessionStorage
  const savedStateStr = sessionStorage.getItem('studyforge_missions_state');
  const savedState = savedStateStr ? JSON.parse(savedStateStr) : null;

  // Состояние материалов
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedMaterialId, setSelectedMaterialId] = useState<string>(queryMaterialId || savedState?.selectedMaterialId || '');
  
  // Состояние игры
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<'select' | 'loading' | 'game' | 'completed'>(
    queryMaterialId
      ? 'loading'
      : (savedState?.phase === 'loading' ? 'select' : (savedState?.phase || 'select'))
  );
  const [missions, setMissions] = useState<Mission[]>(savedState?.missions || []);
  const [activeStep, setActiveStep] = useState<number>(savedState?.activeStep !== undefined ? savedState.activeStep : 0); // индекс текущей миссии (0, 1, 2)
  const [selectedOption, setSelectedOption] = useState<string | null>(savedState?.selectedOption || null);
  const [isCorrect, setIsCorrect] = useState<boolean | null>(savedState?.isCorrect !== undefined ? savedState.isCorrect : null);
  const [showHint, setShowHint] = useState<boolean>(savedState?.showHint || false);
  const [shake, setShake] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [showConfetti, setShowConfetti] = useState(false);
  const [achievement, setAchievement] = useState<Achievement | null>(null);
  const [isDropdownOpen, setIsDropdownOpen] = useState(false);

  // Сохранение состояния в sessionStorage
  useEffect(() => {
    sessionStorage.setItem('studyforge_missions_state', JSON.stringify({
      selectedMaterialId,
      phase,
      missions,
      activeStep,
      selectedOption,
      isCorrect,
      showHint
    }));
  }, [selectedMaterialId, phase, missions, activeStep, selectedOption, isCorrect, showHint]);

  // Загрузка материалов
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    getMaterials(user.id)
      .then((data) => {
        setMaterials(data);
        
        // Если передан queryMaterialId и мы еще не начали играть, автоматически запускаем
        if (queryMaterialId && (phase === 'select' || phase === 'loading')) {
          const found = data.find(m => m.id === queryMaterialId);
          if (found) {
            (async () => {
              setPhase('loading');
              setSelectedOption(null);
              setIsCorrect(null);
              setShowHint(false);
              setActiveStep(0);

              // 1. Проверяем, есть ли уже сгенерированные миссии в БД
              const foundAny = found as any;
              if (foundAny.missions_json && foundAny.missions_json.missions && foundAny.missions_json.missions.length > 0) {
                setMissions(foundAny.missions_json.missions);
                setPhase('game');
                return;
              }

              // 2. Если нет (старый материал), генерируем на лету
              const summary = typeof found.summary_json === 'string'
                ? found.summary_json
                : (found.summary_json?.content || '');
              const context = (found.original_text || summary || '').slice(0, 15000);

              if (user?.id) {
                const limitCheck = await checkAILimit(user.id, 2);
                if (!limitCheck.allowed) {
                  setPhase('select');
                  await alert({ title: 'Энергия ИИ исчерпана', message: 'Не хватает энергии ИИ (нужно 2). Перейдите на PRO-тариф!', type: 'danger' });
                  navigate('/pricing');
                  return;
                }
                await incrementAIUsage(user.id, 2);
              }

              try {
                const res = await fetch('/api/missions/generate', {
                  method: 'POST',
                  headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                  body: JSON.stringify({ context })
                });

                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Ошибка генерации игры');
                const gData = await res.json();
                
                if (gData.missions && gData.missions.length > 0) {
                  setMissions(gData.missions);
                  setPhase('game');
                  updateMaterialMissions(found.id, gData.missions);
                } else {
                  throw new Error('Пустой список миссий');
                }
              } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
                setPhase('select');
              }
            })();
          }
        }
      })
      .catch(console.error);
  }, [user?.id, queryMaterialId]);

  const selectedMaterial = materials.find(m => m.id === selectedMaterialId);

  // Старт игры: запрос генерации квестов
  const startGame = async () => {
    if (!selectedMaterial) return;
    setPhase('loading');
    setSelectedOption(null);
    setIsCorrect(null);
    setShowHint(false);
    setActiveStep(0);

    // 1. Проверяем, есть ли уже сгенерированные миссии в БД
    if (selectedMaterial.missions_json && selectedMaterial.missions_json.missions && selectedMaterial.missions_json.missions.length > 0) {
      setMissions(selectedMaterial.missions_json.missions);
      setPhase('game');
      return;
    }

    // 2. Если нет (старый материал), генерируем на лету
    const summary = typeof selectedMaterial.summary_json === 'string'
      ? selectedMaterial.summary_json
      : (selectedMaterial.summary_json?.content || '');
    const context = (selectedMaterial.original_text || summary || '').slice(0, 15000);

    if (user?.id) {
      const limitCheck = await checkAILimit(user.id, 2);
      if (!limitCheck.allowed) {
        setPhase('select');
        await alert({ title: 'Энергия ИИ исчерпана', message: 'Не хватает энергии ИИ (нужно 2). Перейдите на PRO-тариф!', type: 'danger' });
        navigate('/pricing');
        return;
      }
      await incrementAIUsage(user.id, 2);
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/missions/generate', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ context })
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Ошибка генерации игры');
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (data.missions && data.missions.length > 0) {
        setMissions(data.missions);
        setPhase('game');
        updateMaterialMissions(selectedMaterial.id, data.missions);
      } else {
        throw new Error('Пустой список миссий');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      await alert({ title: 'Ошибка', message: 'Не удалось сформировать игру по материалу. Попробуйте еще раз.', type: 'danger' });
      setPhase('select');
    }
  };

  // Проверка ответа студента
  const handleOptionClick = (option: string) => {
    if (isCorrect) return; // уже ответил верно

    setSelectedOption(option);
    const correctLetter = missions[activeStep].correct; // 'A', 'B' или 'C'
    const isCorrectAnswer = option.startsWith(correctLetter);

    if (isCorrectAnswer) {
      setIsCorrect(true);
      setShowHint(false);
    } else {
      setIsCorrect(false);
      setShowHint(true);
      setShake(true);
      setTimeout(() => setShake(false), 500); // анимация тряски
    }
  };

  // Переход к следующей миссии
  const handleNextMission = async () => {
    setSelectedOption(null);
    setIsCorrect(null);
    setShowHint(false);

    if (activeStep + 1 >= missions.length) {
      // Все миссии пройдены!
      setShowConfetti(true);
      setPhase('completed');
      setTimeout(() => setShowConfetti(false), 100);
      
      // Запись сессии в Supabase для начисления XP и активности
      if (user) {
        await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'quiz', 1);
        await recordStudyActivity(user.id, 3, 0);
        // Проверяем достижения
        const newAchs = await checkAndAwardAchievements(user.id);
        if (newAchs.length > 0) {
          setAchievement({ id: newAchs[0].id, title: newAchs[0].title, description: newAchs[0].description, icon: newAchs[0].icon, xp: newAchs[0].xp });
        }
      }
    } else {
      setActiveStep(prev => prev + 1);
    }
  };

  const currentMission = missions[activeStep];

  return (
    <div className="p-3 md:p-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto min-h-[80vh] flex flex-col justify-center">
      <ConfettiCelebration trigger={showConfetti} type="quest" />
      <AchievementToast achievement={achievement} onClose={() => setAchievement(null)} />
      {/* Шапка */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6 justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
            <Gamepad2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Интерактивные миссии</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Геймификация сложных лекций в порции квестов</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {phase !== 'select' && (
          <button
            onClick={async () => {
              if (await confirm({ title: 'Выход', message: 'Вы уверены, что хотите выйти из игры? Весь прогресс будет потерян.', type: 'warning' })) {
                setPhase('select');
                setMissions([]);
                setActiveStep(0);
                setSelectedOption(null);
                setIsCorrect(null);
                setShowHint(false);
              }
            }}
            className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/30 hover:text-rose-600 dark:hover:text-rose-400 rounded-lg text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 transition"
          >
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Выйти из игры
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {/* ФАЗА 1: Выбор материала */}
        {phase === 'select' && (
          <motion.div
            key="select"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -15 }}
            className="card p-4 md:p-8 text-center relative z-20"
          >
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <Gamepad2 className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-500" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2">Микро-квесты по лекциям</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto mb-6">
              AI-Методист разобьет сложную тему на 3 простые миссии. Изучите порции теории на простом языке и разблокируйте все уровни игры!
            </p>

            <div className="max-w-md mx-auto space-y-4">
              <div className="text-left relative">
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-2">Выберите лекцию из библиотеки:</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <CustomSelect
                    value={selectedMaterialId}
                    onChange={val => setSelectedMaterialId(val)}
                    options={[
                      { value: '', label: 'Выберите лекцию для игры' },
                      ...materials.map(m => ({ value: m.id, label: m.title }))
                    ]}
                    className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 shadow-sm"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                disabled={!selectedMaterialId}
                onClick={startGame}
                className="w-full py-3.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100"
              >
                <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5" /> Начать игру
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА ЗАГРУЗКИ / ГЕНЕРАЦИИ ИГРЫ */}
        {phase === 'loading' && (
          <motion.div
            key="loading"
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center"
          >
            <div className="relative mb-6">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-emerald-100 dark:border-emerald-950/50 border-t-emerald-600 rounded-full animate-spin" />
              <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Gamepad2 className="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-500 animate-pulse" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 text-base mb-1">ИИ-Методист готовит сценарий квестов...</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 max-w-xs leading-relaxed">
              Сложная научная лекция переводится на игровой язык с метафорами и аналогиями
            </p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА 2: Игровой процесс */}
        {phase === 'game' && currentMission && (
          <motion.div
            key="game"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.98 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-6"
          >
            {/* Визуальная шкала миссий */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800/60 p-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 gap-1">
              {missions.map((m, idx) => {
                const isActive = activeStep === idx;
                const isPassed = activeStep > idx;
                const isLocked = activeStep < idx;

                return (
                  <div key={m.id} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 relative">
                    <div className={`flex items-center gap-1 md:gap-1.5 px-2 md:px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition shadow-sm ${
                      isActive 
                        ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' 
                        : isPassed 
                        ? 'bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400' 
                        : 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500'
                    }`}>
                      {isPassed ? <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : isLocked ? <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Unlock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                      <span className="hidden sm:inline">Миссия {idx + 1}</span>
                      <span className="sm:hidden">{idx + 1}</span>
                    </div>
                    {idx < missions.length - 1 && (
                      <div className="hidden sm:block absolute right-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 translate-x-1/2 z-10">
                        <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600" />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {/* Игровой блок теории (простым языком) */}
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-900 to-slate-900 text-white rounded-2xl p-4 md:p-6 shadow-xl relative border border-emerald-900/50 overflow-hidden">
              <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-32 h-32 bg-emerald-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
              
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black tracking-wider uppercase">Квест {activeStep + 1}: {currentMission.title}</span>
              </div>
              
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-white leading-snug">
                  📖 Теория на пальцах
                </h3>
                <div className="text-slate-300 text-sm leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line markdown-content white-text">
                  <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkMath]} rehypePlugins={[rehypeKatex]}>{currentMission.explanation}</ReactMarkdown>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Проверочный вопрос */}
            <motion.div 
              animate={shake ? { x: [-10, 10, -10, 10, 0] } : {}}
              transition={{ duration: 0.4 }}
              className="card p-4 md:p-5 space-y-4"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                <HelpCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-500 shrink-0" />
                <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 text-sm uppercase tracking-wider">Испытание для разблокировки следующего уровня:</h4>
              </div>

              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 leading-relaxed mb-4 markdown-content">
                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkMath]} rehypePlugins={[rehypeKatex]}>{currentMission.question}</ReactMarkdown>
              </div>

              {/* Кнопки ответов */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3">
                {currentMission.options.map((opt) => {
                  const isThisSelected = selectedOption === opt;
                  const isCorrectAnswer = opt.startsWith(currentMission.correct);

                  let btnStyle = "bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800";
                  if (selectedOption) {
                    if (isThisSelected) {
                      btnStyle = isCorrectAnswer 
                        ? "bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 border-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-700 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300 ring-2 ring-emerald-500/20 font-bold" 
                        : "bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/40 border-rose-300 dark:border-rose-700 text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-300 ring-2 ring-rose-500/20 font-bold";
                    } else if (isCorrectAnswer && !isCorrect) {
                      btnStyle = "bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/30 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 opacity-60";
                    } else {
                      btnStyle = "bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border-transparent text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 opacity-40 cursor-default";
                    }
                  }

                  return (
                    <button
                      key={opt}
                      onClick={() => handleOptionClick(opt)}
                      disabled={isCorrect === true}
                      className={`w-full p-3 md:p-4 rounded-xl border text-left text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-200 flex justify-between items-center ${btnStyle}`}
                    >
                      <span>{opt}</span>
                      {selectedOption && isThisSelected && (
                        isCorrectAnswer 
                          ? <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-500 shrink-0" /> 
                          : <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-500 shrink-0" />
                      )}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {/* Вывод подсказки при ошибке */}
              <AnimatePresence>
                {showHint && currentMission.hint && (
                  <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                    exit={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                    style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}
                    className="bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/30 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-900/50 rounded-xl"
                  >
                    <div className="p-3.5 flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300">
                      <AlertCircle className="w-4.5 h-4.5 shrink-0 text-amber-500 mt-0.5" />
                      <div>
                        <span className="font-bold block mb-0.5">💡 Подсказка AI-Методиста:</span>
                        <div className="mb-2">{currentMission.hint}</div>
                        <div className="inline-flex font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px] text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-900/60 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md animate-pulse">
                          👉 Попробуйте выбрать другой вариант
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </motion.div>
                )}
              </AnimatePresence>

              {/* Кнопка далее */}
              {isCorrect && (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  className="pt-2"
                >
                  <button
                    onClick={handleNextMission}
                    className="w-full py-3.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100 dark:shadow-emerald-950/20"
                  >
                    {activeStep + 1 >= missions.length ? 'Завершить обучение 🎉' : 'Пройти к миссии ' + (activeStep + 2) + ' →'}
                  </button>
                </motion.div>
              )}
            </motion.div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА 3: Завершение и награда */}
        {phase === 'completed' && (
          <motion.div
            key="completed"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="card p-5 md:p-10 text-center bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50 to-teal-50 dark:from-emerald-950/20 dark:to-teal-950/20 border-emerald-100 dark:border-emerald-900/30 shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden"
          >
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-64 h-64 bg-emerald-500/5 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
            <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-64 h-64 bg-teal-500/5 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6 text-white shadow-xl relative overflow-hidden">
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-white/10 rounded-full scale-90 border border-dashed border-white/50 animate-spin-slow" />
              <Trophy className="w-10 h-10 animate-pulse" />
            </div>

            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300 mb-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
              Тема полностью освоена!
              <Sparkles className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-500 animate-bounce" />
            </h2>
            <p className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 text-sm max-w-md mx-auto mb-8">
              Вы успешно прошли все 3 микро-миссии по лекции «{selectedMaterial?.title}»! Вы поняли суть темы без лишней академической шелухи.
            </p>

            {/* Бейдж награды XP */}
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white font-extrabold px-6 py-3 rounded-2xl shadow-md mb-8 ring-4 ring-emerald-600/10">
              <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-300" />
              <span>НАГРАДА: +50 XP</span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 justify-center max-w-md mx-auto">
              <button
                onClick={() => setPhase('select')}
                className="flex-1 py-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100 dark:shadow-emerald-950/20"
              >
                Выбрать другую лекцию
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')}
                className="px-6 py-3 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
              >
                Вернуться на дашборд
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/ProfessorExam.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Award, BookOpen, Mic, MicOff, Send, MessageSquare, AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, HelpCircle, ArrowRight, RefreshCw, X, Volume2, Sparkles, Play, Pause, Square, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkMath from 'remark-math';
import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex';
import { CustomSelect } from '../components/CustomSelect';
import 'katex/dist/katex.min.css';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { useConfirm } from '../contexts/ConfirmContext';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';
import { updateQuestProgress, checkAndAwardAchievements, recordStudyActivity } from '../api/quests';
import ConfettiCelebration from '../components/ConfettiCelebration';
import AchievementToast from '../components/AchievementToast';
import type { Achievement } from '../components/AchievementToast';
import { playTTS, pauseTTS, resumeTTS, stopTTS } from '../utils/tts';
import { checkAILimit, incrementAIUsage } from '../api/limits';

// Web Speech API
const SpeechRecognition = (window as any).SpeechRecognition || (window as any).webkitSpeechRecognition;

interface EvaluationResult {
  score: number;
  grade: string;
  verdict: string;
  correct_points: string[];
  errors: string[];
  missing_points: string[];
}

export default function ProfessorExam() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { confirm, alert } = useConfirm();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  // Восстановление состояния из sessionStorage
  const savedStateStr = sessionStorage.getItem('studyforge_professor_exam_state');
  const savedState = savedStateStr ? JSON.parse(savedStateStr) : null;

  // Состояние материалов
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedMaterialId, setSelectedMaterialId] = useState<string>(savedState?.selectedMaterialId || '');
  const [isDropdownOpen, setIsDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  
  // Состояние экзамена
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<'select' | 'question' | 'evaluation' | 'loading'>(
    savedState?.phase === 'loading' ? 'select' : (savedState?.phase || 'select')
  );
  const [currentQuestion, setCurrentQuestion] = useState<string>(savedState?.currentQuestion || '');
  const [answerText, setAnswerText] = useState<string>(savedState?.answerText || '');
  const [interimText, setInterimText] = useState<string>('');
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<{ question: string; answer: string; grade: string }[]>(savedState?.history || []);
  const [evaluation, setEvaluation] = useState<EvaluationResult | null>(savedState?.evaluation || null);
  const [examHistory, setExamHistory] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [showHistory, setShowHistory] = useState<boolean>(false);
  
  // Голосовой ввод
  const [isListening, setIsListening] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const recognitionRef = useRef<any>(null);
  // Режим экзамена
  const [socraticMode, setSocraticMode] = useState<boolean>(savedState?.socraticMode || false);
  const [todayMood, setTodayMood] = useState<any>(null);

  // Конфетти и достижения
  const [showConfetti, setShowConfetti] = useState(false);
  const [achievement, setAchievement] = useState<Achievement | null>(null);

  // Состояние TTS
  const [ttsState, setTtsState] = useState<'playing' | 'paused' | 'stopped'>('stopped');

  // Сохранение состояния в sessionStorage
  useEffect(() => {
    sessionStorage.setItem('studyforge_professor_exam_state', JSON.stringify({
      selectedMaterialId,
      phase,
      currentQuestion,
      answerText,
      history,
      evaluation,
      socraticMode
    }));
  }, [selectedMaterialId, phase, currentQuestion, answerText, history, evaluation, socraticMode]);

  // Автоозвучка при появлении вопроса
  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase === 'question' && currentQuestion) {
      setTtsState('playing');
      playTTS(
        currentQuestion, 
        'professor', 
        () => setTtsState('stopped'),
        () => setTtsState('stopped')
      );
    } else {
      stopTTS();
      setTtsState('stopped');
    }

    return () => {
      stopTTS();
    };
  }, [phase, currentQuestion]);

  // Загрузка материалов и истории экзаменов
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    const cacheMats = localStorage.getItem(`prof_materials_${user.id}`);
    if (cacheMats) { try { setMaterials(JSON.parse(cacheMats)); } catch(e){} }
    
    getMaterials(user.id).then(data => {
      setMaterials(data);
      localStorage.setItem(`prof_materials_${user.id}`, JSON.stringify(data));
    }).catch(console.error);
    
    fetchExamHistory();
    fetchTodayMood();
  }, [user?.id]);

  const fetchTodayMood = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('mood_log')
      .select('mood, energy_level')
      .eq('user_id', user.id)
      .eq('logged_at', today)
      .limit(1)
      .maybeSingle();
    setTodayMood(data);
  };

  const fetchExamHistory = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    const cacheKey = `prof_history_${user.id}`;
    const cached = localStorage.getItem(cacheKey);
    if (cached) { try { setExamHistory(JSON.parse(cached)); } catch(e){} }
    
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('exam_sessions')
      .select('*')
      .eq('user_id', user.id)
      .eq('status', 'completed')
      .order('completed_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(10);
      
    if (data) {
      setExamHistory(data);
      localStorage.setItem(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(data));
    }
  };

  // Настройка голосового распознавания
  const initRecognition = () => {
    if (recognitionRef.current) return recognitionRef.current;
    
    if (!SpeechRecognition) return null;

    const rec = new SpeechRecognition();
    rec.continuous = true;
    rec.lang = 'ru-RU';
    rec.interimResults = true;

    rec.onstart = () => {
      setIsListening(true);
      console.log('Speech recognition started');
    };
    
    rec.onend = () => {
      setIsListening(false);
      setInterimText('');
      console.log('Speech recognition ended');
    };

    rec.onresult = (event: any) => {
      let finalTranscript = '';
      let currentInterim = '';
      for (let i = event.resultIndex; i < event.results.length; ++i) {
        if (event.results[i].isFinal) {
          finalTranscript += event.results[i][0].transcript;
        } else {
          currentInterim += event.results[i][0].transcript;
        }
      }
      if (finalTranscript) {
        setAnswerText(prev => prev ? prev + ' ' + finalTranscript : finalTranscript);
      }
      setInterimText(currentInterim);
    };

    rec.onerror = async (e: any) => {
      console.error('Speech recognition error:', e.error);
      setIsListening(false);
      
      if (e.error === 'not-allowed') {
        await alert({ title: 'Ошибка доступа', message: 'Доступ к микрофону заблокирован. Разрешите его в настройках браузера (значок замка в адресной строке).', type: 'danger' });
      } else if (e.error === 'network') {
        await alert({ title: 'Ошибка сети', message: 'Ошибка сети. Проверьте интернет-соединение. Голосовой ввод Google требует стабильной сети.', type: 'danger' });
      } else if (e.error === 'no-speech') {
        // Просто игнорируем, если не услышал
        console.log('No speech detected');
      } else {
        console.warn('Recognition error:', e.error);
      }
    };

    recognitionRef.current = rec;
    return rec;
  };

  const toggleListening = async () => {
    const rec = initRecognition();
    
    if (!rec) {
      await alert({ title: 'Не поддерживается', message: 'Голосовой ввод не поддерживается вашим браузером. Пожалуйста, используйте Google Chrome, Edge или Safari.', type: 'danger' });
      return;
    }

    if (isListening) {
      try {
        rec.stop();
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Stop error:', e);
      }
    } else {
      try {
        rec.start();
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Start error:', err);
        // Если уже запущено, просто обновляем стейт
        if (err.name === 'InvalidStateError') {
          setIsListening(true);
        }
      }
    }
  };

  // Очистка при размонтировании
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (recognitionRef.current) {
        try {
          recognitionRef.current.stop();
        } catch (e) {}
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const selectedMaterial = materials.find(m => m.id === selectedMaterialId);

  // Начать экзамен / Запросить вопрос
  const startExamOrNextQuestion = async (clearHistory = false) => {
    if (!selectedMaterial) return;
    setPhase('loading');
    setAnswerText('');
    
    let currentHistory = history;
    if (clearHistory) {
      currentHistory = [];
      setHistory([]);
      setEvaluation(null);
    }

    // Собираем историю для контекста
    const historyText = currentHistory
      .map(h => `В: ${h.question}\nО: ${h.answer}\nОценка: ${h.grade}`)
      .join('\n\n');

    // Получаем конспект/оригинал лекции
    const summary = typeof selectedMaterial.summary_json === 'string'
      ? selectedMaterial.summary_json
      : (selectedMaterial.summary_json?.content || '');
    const context = (selectedMaterial.original_text || summary || '').slice(0, 20000);
    // Получаем актуальное настроение пользователя
    const { data: moodData } = await supabase
      .from('mood_log')
      .select('mood, energy_level, notes')
      .eq('user_id', user!.id)
      .order('logged_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(1)
      .maybeSingle();

    // Загрузка недавних экзаменов и ошибок для адаптивности сложности
    const [examHistoryRes, mistakesRes] = await Promise.all([
      supabase.from('exam_sessions').select('score, grade').eq('user_id', user!.id).eq('status', 'completed').order('completed_at', { ascending: false }).limit(5),
      supabase.from('mistake_diary').select('topic').eq('user_id', user!.id).eq('is_resolved', false).limit(5)
    ]);

    const examHistory = examHistoryRes.data || [];
    const recentMistakes = (mistakesRes.data || []).map(m => m.topic).filter(Boolean);

    if (user?.id) {
      const limitCheck = await checkAILimit(user.id, 1);
      if (!limitCheck.allowed) {
        setPhase(clearHistory ? 'select' : 'evaluation');
        await alert({ title: 'Энергия ИИ исчерпана', message: 'Не хватает энергии ИИ (нужно 1). Перейдите на PRO-тариф!', type: 'danger' });
        navigate('/pricing');
        return;
      }
      await incrementAIUsage(user.id, 1);
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/professor/question', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          context,
          history_text: historyText,
          mood: moodData,
          socratic_mode: socraticMode,
          exam_history: examHistory,
          recent_mistakes: recentMistakes,
        })
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Ошибка связи с сервером');
      const data = await res.json();
      setCurrentQuestion(data.question);
      setPhase('question');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      await alert({ title: 'Ошибка', message: 'Не удалось задать вопрос. Попробуйте еще раз.', type: 'danger' });
      setPhase(clearHistory ? 'select' : 'evaluation');
    }
  };

  // Отправить ответ на оценку
  const submitAnswer = async () => {
    if (!answerText.trim() || !selectedMaterial) return;
    setPhase('loading');
