    const summary = typeof selectedMaterial.summary_json === 'string'
      ? selectedMaterial.summary_json
      : (selectedMaterial.summary_json?.content || '');
    const context = (selectedMaterial.original_text || summary || '').slice(0, 20000);

    if (user?.id) {
      const limitCheck = await checkAILimit(user.id, 1);
      if (!limitCheck.allowed) {
        setPhase('question');
        await alert({ title: 'Энергия ИИ исчерпана', message: 'Не хватает энергии ИИ (нужно 1). Перейдите на PRO-тариф!', type: 'danger' });
        navigate('/pricing');
        return;
      }
      await incrementAIUsage(user.id, 1);
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/professor/evaluate', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          context,
          question: currentQuestion,
          student_answer: answerText
        })
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Ошибка оценки');
      const evalData: EvaluationResult = await res.json();
      
      setEvaluation(evalData);
      
      // Конфетти при пятёрке!
      if (evalData.grade === '5') {
        setShowConfetti(true);
        setTimeout(() => setShowConfetti(false), 100);
      }
      
      // Добавляем в локальную историю
      setHistory(prev => [
        ...prev,
        { question: currentQuestion, answer: answerText, grade: evalData.grade }
      ]);

      // Запись сессии в Supabase для статистики
      if (user) {
        // Создаем сессию экзамена если ее еще нет
        const { data: sessionData } = await supabase.from('exam_sessions').insert({
          user_id: user.id,
          material_id: selectedMaterial?.id ?? null,
          subject: selectedMaterial?.subject ?? '',
          score: evalData.score,
          questions_json: [{ question: currentQuestion, answer: answerText, evaluation: evalData }],
          total_questions: 1,
          status: 'completed',
          completed_at: new Date().toISOString()
        }).select().single();

        if (sessionData) {
          fetchExamHistory();
          await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'exam', 1);
          // Проверяем достижения
          const newAchs = await checkAndAwardAchievements(user.id);
          if (newAchs.length > 0) {
            setAchievement({ id: newAchs[0].id, title: newAchs[0].title, description: newAchs[0].description, icon: newAchs[0].icon, xp: newAchs[0].xp });
          }
        }

        await recordStudyActivity(user.id, 1, 0);

        // Автоматически сохраняем ошибки в дневник ошибок
        const mistakesToSave = [
          ...evalData.errors.map((err: string) => ({
            user_id: user.id,
            material_id: selectedMaterial?.id ?? null,
            question: currentQuestion,
            correct_answer: err,
            student_answer: answerText,
            topic: selectedMaterial?.subject ?? '',
            is_resolved: false,
          })),
          ...evalData.missing_points.map((miss: string) => ({
            user_id: user.id,
            material_id: selectedMaterial?.id ?? null,
            question: currentQuestion,
            correct_answer: miss,
            student_answer: '(не упомянуто)',
            topic: selectedMaterial?.subject ?? '',
            is_resolved: false,
          })),
        ];

        if (mistakesToSave.length > 0) {
          await supabase.from('mistake_diary').insert(mistakesToSave);
        }
      }

      setPhase('evaluation');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      await alert({ title: 'Ошибка', message: 'Ошибка при оценке ответа. Пожалуйста, попробуйте отправить заново.', type: 'danger' });
      setPhase('question');
    }
  };

  // Цвета для академической оценки
  const getGradeColors = (grade: string) => {
    switch (grade) {
      case '5': return {
        bg: 'bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50/90 to-teal-50/40 border-emerald-200/60 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100/10 backdrop-blur-md',
        text: 'text-emerald-950',
        textColor: 'text-emerald-800',
        sealBg: 'bg-gradient-to-tr from-emerald-500 to-teal-500 shadow-emerald-200 shadow-md border border-emerald-400',
        label: '🏆 Отлично (5)'
      };
      case '4': return {
        bg: 'bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-50/90 to-blue-50/40 border-indigo-200/60 shadow-lg shadow-indigo-100/10 backdrop-blur-md',
        text: 'text-indigo-950',
        textColor: 'text-indigo-800',
        sealBg: 'bg-gradient-to-tr from-indigo-500 to-blue-500 shadow-indigo-200 shadow-md border border-indigo-400',
        label: '⭐ Хорошо (4)'
      };
      case '3': return {
        bg: 'bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-50/90 to-orange-50/40 border-amber-200/60 shadow-lg shadow-amber-100/10 backdrop-blur-md',
        text: 'text-amber-950',
        textColor: 'text-amber-800',
        sealBg: 'bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-500 to-orange-500 shadow-amber-200 shadow-md border border-amber-400',
        label: '📝 Удовлетворительно (3)'
      };
      default: return {
        bg: 'bg-gradient-to-br from-rose-50/90 to-red-50/40 border-rose-200/60 shadow-lg shadow-rose-100/10 backdrop-blur-md',
        text: 'text-rose-950',
        textColor: 'text-rose-800',
        sealBg: 'bg-gradient-to-tr from-rose-500 to-red-500 shadow-rose-200 shadow-md border border-rose-400',
        label: '❌ Неудовлетворительно (2)'
      };
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-3 md:p-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto min-h-[80vh] flex flex-col justify-center">
      <ConfettiCelebration trigger={showConfetti} type="exam5" />
      <AchievementToast achievement={achievement} onClose={() => setAchievement(null)} />
      {/* Заголовок */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6 justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-rose-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
            <Award className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">AI-Экзаменатор</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Устная репетиция сдача сессии профессору</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {/* Переключатель режима */}
          {phase === 'select' && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setSocraticMode(p => !p)}
              className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition border ${
                socraticMode
                  ? 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 border-purple-200 dark:bg-purple-950/40 dark:text-purple-300 dark:border-purple-800'
                  : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400 dark:border-slate-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
              }`}
              title="Режим Сократовского диалога: профессор докапывается до сути через уточняющие вопросы"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              {socraticMode ? 'Сократ ВКЛ' : 'Сократ ВЫКЛ'}
            </button>
          )}
          {phase !== 'select' && (
            <button
              onClick={async () => {
                if (await confirm({ title: 'Завершить', message: 'Вы уверены, что хотите завершить экзамен?', type: 'warning' })) {
                  setPhase('select');
                  setCurrentQuestion('');
                  setAnswerText('');
                  setHistory([]);
                  setEvaluation(null);
                }
              }}
              className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 rounded-lg text-slate-600 transition dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/40 dark:hover:text-rose-400 dark:border dark:border-slate-700"
            >
              <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Выйти
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {/* ФАЗА 1: Выбор материала */}
        {phase === 'select' && (
          <motion.div
            key="select"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -15 }}
            className="space-y-6"
          >
            <div className="card p-4 md:p-8 text-center relative z-20">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-rose-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                <Award className="w-8 h-8 text-rose-500" />
              </div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2">Готовы проверить свои знания?</h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-md mx-auto mb-6">
                AI-Профессор задаст вам каверзные вопросы на понимание. Вы можете вводить ответ текстом или наговаривать голосом через микрофон.
              </p>

              <div className="max-w-md mx-auto space-y-4">
                <div className="text-left relative">
                  <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-2">Выберите лекцию из библиотеки:</label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <CustomSelect
                      value={selectedMaterialId}
                      onChange={val => setSelectedMaterialId(val)}
                      placeholder="Выберите материал для сдачи"
                      options={materials.map(m => ({ value: m.id, label: m.title }))}
                      className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-rose-500 shadow-sm"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                {!todayMood && (
                  <div className="bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 border border-indigo-100 dark:border-indigo-800 rounded-xl p-4 text-left shadow-sm">
                    <p className="text-sm font-bold text-indigo-800 dark:text-indigo-300 mb-1">Настроим сложность под вас?</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 mb-3 leading-relaxed">
                      ИИ-Профессор подстроит каверзность вопросов под ваше текущее состояние. 
                    </p>
                    <Link to="/mood" className="text-xs px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-lg font-bold inline-block hover:bg-indigo-700 transition shadow-sm">
                      Указать настрой в Трекере
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                )}
                {todayMood && (
                  <div className="bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 border border-emerald-100 dark:border-emerald-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between text-left shadow-sm">
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300">Состояние учтено</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 mt-0.5">Сложность экзамена будет адаптирована.</p>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-3xl ml-3 filter drop-shadow-sm">
                      {todayMood.mood === 'great' ? '🚀' : todayMood.mood === 'good' ? '😊' : todayMood.mood === 'okay' ? '😐' : todayMood.mood === 'tired' ? '😴' : '😰'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                )}

                <button
                  disabled={!selectedMaterialId}
                  onClick={() => startExamOrNextQuestion(true)}
                  className="w-full py-3.5 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-rose-100"
                >
                  <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5" /> Войти в аудиторию
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* История экзаменов */}
            {examHistory.length > 0 && (
              <div className="card p-4 md:p-6 mt-6">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
                    <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500" />
                    История последних экзаменов
                  </h3>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {examHistory.map(session => (
                    <div key={session.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700">
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-0.5">
                          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 truncate">{session.subject || 'Без предмета'}</span>
                          <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 rounded text-[9px] font-bold">
                            {new Date(session.completed_at).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 truncate">Билет по одной из лекций</p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 ml-4">
                        <div className="text-right">
                          <p className="text-xs font-black text-rose-600">{session.score}%</p>
                          <p className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 uppercase font-bold">Оценка</p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА ЗАГРУЗКИ / «Профессор думает...» */}
        {phase === 'loading' && (
          <motion.div
            key="loading"
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center"
          >
            <div className="relative mb-6">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-rose-100 border-t-rose-600 rounded-full animate-spin" />
              <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Award className="w-6 h-6 text-rose-500 animate-pulse" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 text-base mb-1">Профессор анализирует материал...</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 max-w-xs leading-relaxed">
              Формулируется индивидуальный вопрос для оценки вашего понимания
            </p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА 2: Экзаменационный вопрос */}
        {phase === 'question' && (
          <motion.div
            key="question"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.98 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-6"
          >
            {/* Вопрос Профессора */}
            <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-2xl p-4 md:p-6 shadow-xl relative border border-slate-800 overflow-hidden">
              <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-32 h-32 bg-rose-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
              
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/30 rounded text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase">Билет №{history.length + 1}</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Лекция: {selectedMaterial?.title}</span>
              </div>
              
              <div className="flex gap-4 items-start">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-slate-800 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 border border-slate-700">
                  <HelpCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-400" />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                    <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-300 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">Вопрос профессора:</h3>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-800 rounded-lg p-1 border border-slate-700">
                      {ttsState === 'playing' ? (
                        <button onClick={() => { pauseTTS(); setTtsState('paused'); }} className="p-1.5 hover:bg-slate-700 rounded-md transition text-slate-300" title="Пауза">
                          <Pause className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      ) : (
                        <button onClick={() => { 
                          if (ttsState === 'paused') { resumeTTS(); setTtsState('playing'); }
                          else { playTTS(currentQuestion, 'professor', () => setTtsState('stopped')); setTtsState('playing'); }
                        }} className="p-1.5 hover:bg-slate-700 rounded-md transition text-slate-300" title="Воспроизвести">
                          <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      )}
                      <button onClick={() => { stopTTS(); setTtsState('stopped'); }} className="p-1.5 hover:bg-slate-700 rounded-md transition text-slate-300" title="Остановить">
                        <Square className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-base font-medium text-white leading-relaxed markdown-content white-text professor-question">
                    <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkMath]} rehypePlugins={[rehypeKatex]}>{currentQuestion}</ReactMarkdown>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Ввод ответа студента */}
            <div className="card p-4 md:p-5 space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Ваш устный ответ:</label>
                {SpeechRecognition && (
                  <button
                    onClick={toggleListening}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition ${
                      isListening
                        ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 animate-pulse dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-300 dark:border-rose-800'
                        : 'bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 dark:text-slate-400 dark:border-slate-700'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {isListening ? <MicOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Mic className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-500" />}
                    {isListening ? 'Слушаю вас...' : 'Говорить в микрофон'}
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              <textarea
                value={answerText + (interimText ? (answerText ? ' ' : '') + interimText : '')}
                onChange={e => {
                  setAnswerText(e.target.value);
                  setInterimText('');
                }}
                placeholder="Сформулируйте ваш развернутый ответ на вопрос профессора..."
                className="w-full h-36 px-4 py-3.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-rose-500 transition resize-none bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
              />

              <div className="flex gap-3">
                <button
                  disabled={!answerText.trim()}
                  onClick={submitAnswer}
                  className="flex-1 py-3 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-rose-100"
                >
                  <Send className="w-4 h-4" /> Ответить Профессору
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА 3: Оценка и глубокий разбор пробелов */}
        {phase === 'evaluation' && evaluation && (
          <motion.div
            key="evaluation"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-6"
          >
            {/* Оценка и вердикт */}
            {(() => {
              const colors = getGradeColors(evaluation.grade);
              return (
                <div className={`border rounded-3xl p-5 md:p-7 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center gap-6 md:gap-8 ${colors.bg}`}>
                  {/* Академическая печать оценки */}
                  <div className="shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
                    <div className={`w-24 h-24 rounded-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-white font-black text-4xl shadow-xl ${colors.sealBg} relative overflow-hidden border-4 border-white`}>
                      <div className="absolute inset-1 bg-transparent rounded-full border-2 border-dashed border-white/30 scale-95" />
                      {evaluation.grade}
                    </div>
                     <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 mt-2.5 tracking-wider uppercase bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full shadow-sm">Академическая печать</span>
                  </div>

                  {/* Отзыв и точность */}
                  <div className="flex-1 space-y-4 w-full">
                    <div className="text-center md:text-left">
                       <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black tracking-wider uppercase bg-white/90 dark:bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-200/50 dark:border-slate-700/50 shadow-sm ${colors.textColor}`}>
                        {colors.label}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Отзыв профессора */}
                     <div className="bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-100/80 dark:border-slate-700/50 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-sm relative hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-300">
                       <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                         <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-100 to-slate-200 dark:from-slate-700 dark:to-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-600 shadow-inner shrink-0 text-lg">
                          👨‍🏫
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex-1 space-y-1">
                           <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Отзыв профессора</div>
                           <p className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 italic font-semibold text-sm leading-relaxed relative pl-4 border-l-2 border-rose-400">
                            "{evaluation.verdict}"
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Точность ответа */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-1 flex-wrap md:flex-nowrap">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                         <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Точность ответа:</span>
                         <span className="text-xs font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">{evaluation.score}%</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-[150px] h-2 bg-slate-200/60 dark:bg-slate-700/60 rounded-full overflow-hidden relative">
                        <div 
                          className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 to-rose-600 rounded-full" 
                          style={{ width: `${evaluation.score}%` }} 
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })()}

            {/* Когнитивный разбор: что правильно, ошибки и пробелы */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
              {/* Зеленый блок: Что сказали правильно */}
              <div className="card p-5 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/50 bg-white/70 dark:bg-slate-800/70 backdrop-blur-md shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-300 rounded-2xl flex flex-col h-full">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pb-4 mb-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/50">
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 flex items-center justify-center border border-emerald-100 shrink-0">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4.5 h-4.5 text-emerald-600" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h4 className="font-extrabold text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-100 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">Усвоено верно</h4>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">Сильные стороны ответа</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  {evaluation.correct_points.length === 0 ? (
                    <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-center p-4">
                      <p className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 text-xs italic">Нет полностью верных фактов.</p>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <ul className="space-y-2.5">
                      {evaluation.correct_points.map((p, i) => (
                        <li key={i} className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs leading-relaxed flex items-start gap-2 bg-emerald-50/20 dark:bg-emerald-950/20 border border-emerald-50/40 dark:border-emerald-900/40 p-2.5 rounded-xl hover:bg-emerald-50/40 dark:hover:bg-emerald-950/40 transition-all duration-200">
                          <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full shrink-0 mt-1.5" />
                          <span className="font-medium">{p}</span>
                        </li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Красный блок: Допущенные ошибки */}
              <div className="card p-5 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/50 bg-white/70 dark:bg-slate-800/70 backdrop-blur-md shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-300 rounded-2xl flex flex-col h-full">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pb-4 mb-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/50">
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 flex items-center justify-center border border-rose-100 shrink-0">
                    <AlertCircle className="w-4.5 h-4.5 text-rose-600" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h4 className="font-extrabold text-rose-950 dark:text-rose-100 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">Фактические ошибки</h4>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">Неточности в деталях</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  {evaluation.errors.length === 0 ? (
                    <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-4">
                      <span className="text-2xl mb-1">🎉</span>
                      <p className="text-emerald-700 text-xs font-semibold">Ошибок в ответе не обнаружено!</p>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <ul className="space-y-2.5">
                      {evaluation.errors.map((p, i) => (
                        <li key={i} className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs leading-relaxed flex items-start gap-2 bg-rose-50/20 dark:bg-rose-950/20 border border-rose-50/40 dark:border-rose-900/40 p-2.5 rounded-xl hover:bg-rose-50/40 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/40 transition-all duration-200">
                          <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-rose-500 rounded-full shrink-0 mt-1.5" />
                          <span className="font-medium">{p}</span>
                        </li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Желтый/серый блок: Упущенные знания */}
              <div className="card p-5 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/50 bg-white/70 dark:bg-slate-800/70 backdrop-blur-md shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-300 rounded-2xl flex flex-col h-full">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pb-4 mb-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/50">
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 flex items-center justify-center border border-amber-100 shrink-0">
                    <MessageSquare className="w-4.5 h-4.5 text-amber-600" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h4 className="font-extrabold text-amber-950 dark:text-amber-100 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">Когнитивные пропуски</h4>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">Упущенные важные нюансы</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  {evaluation.missing_points.length === 0 ? (
                    <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-4">
                      <span className="text-2xl mb-1">🎯</span>
                      <p className="text-emerald-700 text-xs font-semibold">Исчерпывающий ответ!</p>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <ul className="space-y-2.5">
                      {evaluation.missing_points.map((p, i) => (
                        <li key={i} className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs leading-relaxed flex items-start gap-2 bg-amber-50/20 dark:bg-amber-950/20 border border-amber-50/40 dark:border-amber-900/40 p-2.5 rounded-xl hover:bg-amber-50/40 dark:hover:bg-amber-950/40 transition-all duration-200">
                          <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-amber-500 rounded-full shrink-0 mt-1.5" />
                          <span className="font-medium">{p}</span>
                        </li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Действия */}
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 pt-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => startExamOrNextQuestion(false)}
                className="flex-1 py-3 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-rose-100 dark:shadow-none"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4" /> Следующий билет
              </button>
              
              <button
                onClick={() => setPhase('select')}
                className="px-6 py-3 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300"
              >
                Завершить экзамен
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/MistakeDiary.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { BookMarked, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, RefreshCw, Trash2, Filter, ChevronDown, Zap, CircleDot } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { useConfirm } from '../contexts/ConfirmContext';
import { useSound } from '../hooks/useSound';
import { addXP } from '../api/quests';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';
import { checkAILimit, incrementAIUsage } from '../api/limits';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';

interface Mistake {
  id: string;
  question: string;
  correct_answer: string;
  student_answer: string;
  topic: string;
  is_resolved: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  material_id: string | null;
}

export default function MistakeDiary() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { confirm, alert } = useConfirm();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [mistakes, setMistakes] = useState<Mistake[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<'all' | 'open' | 'resolved'>('all');
  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const { playFlipSound, playSuccessSound, playErrorSound, playXpSound } = useSound();

  // Состояния для AI-Работы над ошибками
  const [isPracticing, setIsPracticing] = useState(false);
  const [practiceLoading, setPracticeLoading] = useState(false);
  const [practiceQuestions, setPracticeQuestions] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [currentQIndex, setCurrentQIndex] = useState(0);
  const [selectedOption, setSelectedOption] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showExplanation, setShowExplanation] = useState(false);
  const [correctCount, setCorrectCount] = useState(0);
  const [practiceFinished, setPracticeFinished] = useState(false);

  const startPractice = async () => {
    const openMistakes = mistakes.filter(m => !m.is_resolved);
    if (openMistakes.length === 0) return;
    
    // Берём до 5 неисправленных ошибок
    const toPractice = openMistakes.slice(0, 5);
    
    if (user?.id) {
      const limitCheck = await checkAILimit(user.id, 2);
      if (!limitCheck.allowed) {
        await alert({ title: 'Энергия ИИ исчерпана', message: 'Не хватает энергии ИИ (нужно 2). Перейдите на PRO-тариф!', type: 'danger' });
        navigate('/pricing');
        return;
      }
      await incrementAIUsage(user.id, 2);
    }

    setPracticeLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/mistakes/practice', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ mistakes: toPractice }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
      const data = await res.json();
      setPracticeQuestions(data.practice || []);
      setIsPracticing(true);
      setCurrentQIndex(0);
      setSelectedOption(null);
      setShowExplanation(false);
      setCorrectCount(0);
      setPracticeFinished(false);
    } catch (e) {
      await alert({ title: 'Ошибка', message: 'Не удалось запустить работу над ошибками. Попробуйте еще раз.', type: 'danger' });
    } finally {
      setPracticeLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const checkAnswer = (opt: string) => {
    if (selectedOption) return; // Запрещаем повторный выбор
    setSelectedOption(opt);
    setShowExplanation(true);
    const q = practiceQuestions[currentQIndex];
    if (opt.startsWith(q.correct)) {
      playFlipSound(); // Pleasant sound for correct
      setCorrectCount(prev => prev + 1);
    } else {
      playErrorSound();
    }
  };

  const nextQuestion = async () => {
    if (currentQIndex + 1 < practiceQuestions.length) {
      setCurrentQIndex(prev => prev + 1);
      setSelectedOption(null);
      setShowExplanation(false);
    } else {
      setPracticeFinished(true);
      
      const openMistakes = mistakes.filter(m => !m.is_resolved);
      const toPractice = openMistakes.slice(0, 5);
      const successRate = (correctCount + (selectedOption?.startsWith(practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.correct) ? 1 : 0)) / practiceQuestions.length;
      
      // Считаем финальный correctCount с учётом последнего вопроса, если он правильный
      const finalCorrect = correctCount + (selectedOption?.startsWith(practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.correct) ? 1 : 0);
      const finalRate = finalCorrect / practiceQuestions.length;

      if (finalRate >= 0.6) {
        const ids = toPractice.map(m => m.id);
        const resolvedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        
        await supabase
          .from('mistake_diary')
          .update({ is_resolved: true, resolved_at: resolvedAt })
          .in('id', ids);
          
        setMistakes(prev => prev.map(m => ids.includes(m.id) ? { ...m, is_resolved: true } : m));
        
        if (user) {
          await addXP(user.id, 50);
        }
        
        playSuccessSound();
        setTimeout(() => playXpSound(), 800);

        confetti({
          particleCount: 120,
          spread: 70,
          origin: { y: 0.6 }
        });
      }
    }
  };

  const fetchMistakes = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    setLoading(true);
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('mistake_diary')
      .select('*')
      .eq('user_id', user.id)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

    if (!error && data) setMistakes(data);
    setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchMistakes();
  }, [user?.id]);

  const markResolved = async (id: string) => {
    await supabase
      .from('mistake_diary')
      .update({ is_resolved: true, resolved_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('id', id);
    setMistakes(prev => prev.map(m => m.id === id ? { ...m, is_resolved: true } : m));
  };

  const deleteMistake = async (id: string) => {
    await supabase.from('mistake_diary').delete().eq('id', id);
    setMistakes(prev => prev.filter(m => m.id !== id));
  };

  const filtered = mistakes.filter(m => {
    if (filter === 'open') return !m.is_resolved;
    if (filter === 'resolved') return m.is_resolved;
    return true;
  });

  const openCount = mistakes.filter(m => !m.is_resolved).length;
  const resolvedCount = mistakes.filter(m => m.is_resolved).length;

  return (
    <div className="p-3 md:p-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
      {/* ═══ РЕЖИМ ПРАКТИКИ ════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      {isPracticing ? (
        <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto py-8">
          <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
            {!practiceFinished ? (
              <motion.div
                key={`q-${currentQIndex}`}
                initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
                className="space-y-6"
              >
                {/* Хедер прогресса */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg">
                      Вопрос {currentQIndex + 1} из {practiceQuestions.length}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                      Верных ответов: {correctCount}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={async () => {
                      if (await confirm({ title: 'Прервать', message: 'Вы уверены, что хотите прервать работу над ошибками? Прогресс не сохранится.', type: 'warning' })) {
                        setIsPracticing(false);
                      }
                    }}
                    className="text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 transition"
                  >
                    Прервать
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Карточка вопроса */}
                <div className="card p-4 md:p-8 space-y-4 md:space-y-6">
                  <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 leading-relaxed">
                    {practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.question}
                  </h3>

                  {/* Опции */}
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.options.map((opt: string) => {
                      const isSelected = selectedOption === opt;
                      const isCorrect = opt.startsWith(practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.correct);
                      const hasAnswered = selectedOption !== null;

                      let btnStyle = 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200';
                      if (hasAnswered) {
                        if (isCorrect) {
                          btnStyle = 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 border-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-700 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300';
                        } else if (isSelected) {
                          btnStyle = 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/40 border-rose-300 dark:border-rose-700 text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-300';
                        } else {
                          btnStyle = 'border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 opacity-60';
                        }
                      }

                      return (
                        <button
                          key={opt}
                          disabled={hasAnswered}
                          onClick={() => checkAnswer(opt)}
                          className={`w-full p-3 md:p-4 text-left text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl border-2 transition-all duration-200 ${btnStyle} flex justify-between items-center`}
                        >
                          <span>{opt}</span>
                          {hasAnswered && isCorrect && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold bg-emerald-600 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md shrink-0">Верно</span>
                          )}
                          {hasAnswered && isSelected && !isCorrect && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold bg-rose-600 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md shrink-0">Ошибка</span>
                          )}
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>

                  {/* Объяснение */}
                  {showExplanation && (
                    <motion.div
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                      style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}
                      className="p-4 bg-amber-50/50 dark:bg-amber-950/20 border border-amber-100 dark:border-amber-900/50 rounded-xl space-y-1"
                    >
                      <h4 className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400">Разбор и пояснение</h4>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                        {practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.explanation}
                      </p>
                    </motion.div>
                  )}

                  {/* Кнопка далее */}
                  {selectedOption && (
                    <motion.button
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 5 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                      onClick={nextQuestion}
                      className="w-full py-3.5 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-lg shadow-indigo-100"
                    >
                      {currentQIndex + 1 < practiceQuestions.length ? 'Следующий вопрос' : 'Завершить практику'}
                    </motion.button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            ) : (
              <motion.div
                key="finished"
                initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                className="card p-8 md:p-10 text-center space-y-6"
              >
                {/* Иконка */}
                <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-100 to-rose-100">
                  <Zap className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-500 animate-bounce" />
                </div>

                {/* Тексты результатов */}
                {correctCount / practiceQuestions.length >= 0.6 ? (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100">Отличная работа! 🎉</h2>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-md mx-auto">
                      Вы ответили верно на {correctCount} из {practiceQuestions.length} вопросов. AI подтверждает: ваши слабые места усвоены!
                    </p>
                    <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300 text-xs font-bold rounded-full px-4 py-2 mt-2">
                      <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-ping" />
                      Ошибки исправлены &amp; Получено +50 XP
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100">Попробуйте еще раз 💔</h2>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-md mx-auto">
                      Вы ответили верно на {correctCount} из {practiceQuestions.length} вопросов. Нужно хотя бы 2 правильных ответа, чтобы исправить ошибки в дневнике.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Кнопка выхода */}
                <button
                  onClick={() => {
                    setIsPracticing(false);
                    setPracticeFinished(false);
                    fetchMistakes();
                  }}
                  className="px-8 py-3 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white text-xs font-black rounded-xl transition"
                >
                  Вернуться в дневник
                </button>
              </motion.div>
            )}
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* ═══ ОБЫЧНЫЙ РЕЖИМ ДНЕВНИКА ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */
        <>
          {/* Заголовок */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-amber-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
              <BookMarked className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Дневник ошибок</h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Все слабые места после AI-Экзаменатора</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Кнопка работы над ошибками */}
          {openCount > 0 && (
            <button
              onClick={startPractice}
              disabled={practiceLoading}
              className="w-full mb-6 py-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-rose-500 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-rose-600 text-white rounded-2xl font-black text-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-rose-100"
            >
              {practiceLoading ? (
                <>
                  <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
                  AI анализирует ошибки и готовит тест...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Zap className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-200 animate-pulse" />
                  ⚡ Запустить AI-работу над ошибками (+50 XP)
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          )}

          {/* Статистика */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 md:gap-4 mb-6">
            <div className="card p-2 md:p-4 text-center">
              <p className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">{mistakes.length}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] md:text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">Всего ошибок</p>
            </div>
            <div className="card p-2 md:p-4 text-center border-l-4 border-l-rose-400">
              <p className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-rose-600">{openCount}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] md:text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">Внимания</p>
            </div>
            <div className="card p-2 md:p-4 text-center border-l-4 border-l-emerald-400">
              <p className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-emerald-600">{resolvedCount}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] md:text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">Исправлено</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Фильтры */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
            <Filter className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
            {(['all', 'open', 'resolved'] as const).map(f => (
              <button
                key={f}
                onClick={() => setFilter(f)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition ${
                  filter === f
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  {f === 'all' && 'Все'}
                  {f === 'open' && <><CircleDot className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${filter === 'open' ? 'text-rose-300' : 'text-rose-500'}`} /> Открытые</>}
                  {f === 'resolved' && <><CheckCircle2 className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${filter === 'resolved' ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-emerald-500'}`} /> Исправленные</>}
                </span>
              </button>
            ))}
            <button
              onClick={fetchMistakes}
              className="ml-auto p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 transition"
              title="Обновить"
            >
              <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Список ошибок */}
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-20">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
            </div>
          ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="card p-10 text-center">
              <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-emerald-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-7 h-7 text-emerald-500" />
              </div>
              <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">
                {filter === 'open' ? 'Отличная работа!' : 'Пока пусто'}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">
                {filter === 'open'
                  ? 'Все ошибки исправлены. Продолжайте в том же духе!'
                  : 'Пройдите AI-Экзамен, и ваши ошибки появятся здесь.'}
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <AnimatePresence>
                {filtered.map(mistake => (
                  <motion.div
                    key={mistake.id}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    exit={{ opacity: 0, height: 0, margin: 0 }}
                    className={`card overflow-hidden ${
                      mistake.is_resolved ? 'opacity-60' : ''
                    }`}
                  >
                    {/* Заголовок карточки */}
                    <div
                      className="p-4 cursor-pointer flex items-start gap-3"
                      onClick={() => setExpandedId(expandedId === mistake.id ? null : mistake.id)}
                    >
                      <div className={`mt-0.5 shrink-0 w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${
                        mistake.is_resolved ? 'bg-emerald-100' : 'bg-rose-100'
                      }`}>
                        {mistake.is_resolved
                          ? <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                          : <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-600" />
                        }
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 leading-snug line-clamp-2">
                          {mistake.correct_answer}
                        </p>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
                          {mistake.topic && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium">
                              {mistake.topic}
                            </span>
                          )}
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
                            {new Date(mistake.created_at).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 shrink-0 transition-transform ${
                        expandedId === mistake.id ? 'rotate-180' : ''
                      }`} />
                    </div>

                    {/* Раскрытое содержимое */}
                    <AnimatePresence>
                      {expandedId === mistake.id && (
                        <motion.div
                          initial={{ height: 0 }}
                          animate={{ height: 'auto' }}
                          exit={{ height: 0 }}
                          className="overflow-hidden"
                        >
                          <div className="px-4 pb-4 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 pt-3 space-y-3">
                            {/* Контекст (Вопрос) */}
                            <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/50 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/60 rounded-xl">
                              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                                Контекст / Вопрос
                              </p>
                              <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed italic">
                                "{mistake.question}"
                              </p>
                            </div>

                            {/* Ответ студента */}
                            <div className="p-3 bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/30 border border-rose-100 dark:border-rose-900/50 rounded-xl">
                              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-rose-500 dark:text-rose-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                                Мой ответ
                              </p>
                              <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                                {mistake.student_answer || '— (пусто)'}
                              </p>
                            </div>

                            {/* Суть ошибки / Правильный ответ */}
                            <div className="p-3 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/30 border border-emerald-100 dark:border-emerald-900/50 rounded-xl">
                              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                                Суть ошибки / Что нужно было сказать
                              </p>
                              <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                                {mistake.correct_answer}
                              </p>
                            </div>

                            {/* Действия */}
                            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
                              {!mistake.is_resolved && (
                                <button
                                  onClick={() => markResolved(mistake.id)}
                                  className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition"
                                >
                                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                  Отметить как исправленную
                                </button>
                              )}
                              <button
                                onClick={() => deleteMistake(mistake.id)}
                                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/30 hover:text-rose-600 dark:hover:text-rose-400 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition"
                              >
                                <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                Удалить
                              </button>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </motion.div>
                      )}
                    </AnimatePresence>
                  </motion.div>
                ))}
              </AnimatePresence>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/MoodTracker.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Heart, Zap, Brain, Coffee, Frown, Smile, Meh, SmilePlus, CheckCircle2, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { updateQuestProgress } from '../api/quests';

type MoodType = 'great' | 'good' | 'okay' | 'tired' | 'stressed';

interface MoodEntry {
  id: string;
  mood: MoodType;
  energy_level: number;
  notes: string;
  recommended_mode: string;
  logged_at: string;
}

interface MoodConfig {
  label: string;
  emoji: string;
  color: string;
  bg: string;
  border: string;
  icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
}

const MOODS: Record<MoodType, MoodConfig> = {
  great: { label: 'Отлично!', emoji: '🚀', color: 'text-emerald-700', bg: 'bg-emerald-50', border: 'border-emerald-300', icon: SmilePlus },
  good: { label: 'Хорошо', emoji: '😊', color: 'text-indigo-700', bg: 'bg-indigo-50', border: 'border-indigo-300', icon: Smile },
  okay: { label: 'Нормально', emoji: '😐', color: 'text-amber-700', bg: 'bg-amber-50', border: 'border-amber-300', icon: Meh },
  tired: { label: 'Устал(а)', emoji: '😴', color: 'text-slate-700', bg: 'bg-slate-100', border: 'border-slate-300', icon: Coffee },
  stressed: { label: 'Стресс', emoji: '😰', color: 'text-rose-700', bg: 'bg-rose-50', border: 'border-rose-300', icon: Frown },
};

const ENERGY_LABELS: Record<number, string> = {
  1: 'Совсем нет сил',
  2: 'Слабая энергия',
  3: 'Средняя',
  4: 'Бодрый(ая)',
  5: 'Полон(на) сил!'
};

function getRecommendation(mood: MoodType, energy: number): string {
  if (mood === 'great' && energy >= 4) {
    return '🔥 Режим «Глубокое погружение» — идеальный день для изучения сложных тем и AI-Экзаменатора!';
  }
  if ((mood === 'good' || mood === 'great') && energy >= 3) {
    return '📚 Режим «Активное обучение» — хорошо зайдёт изучение новых материалов и интерактивные миссии.';
  }
  if (mood === 'okay' || energy === 3) {
    return '🔄 Режим «Повторение» — самое время для спейсд-репетишн и флэш-карточек. Никаких подвигов!';
  }
  if (mood === 'tired' || energy <= 2) {
    return '🌙 Режим «Лёгкий просмотр» — просто перечитай конспект или послушай AI-чат. Мозг сегодня заслужил отдых.';
  }
  if (mood === 'stressed') {
    return '🧘 Режим «Анти-стресс» — начни с планировщика, разбей задачи на мелкие шаги и сделай только одно дело.';
  }
  return '📖 Выбери любой режим учёбы, который кажется тебе комфортным сегодня.';
}

export default function MoodTracker() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<'check' | 'form' | 'done'>('check');
  const [todayEntry, setTodayEntry] = useState<MoodEntry | null>(null);
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<MoodEntry[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // Form state
  const [selectedMood, setSelectedMood] = useState<MoodType | null>(null);
  const [energy, setEnergy] = useState(3);
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    loadData();
  }, [user?.id]);

  const loadData = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('mood_log')
      .select('*')
      .eq('user_id', user!.id)
      .order('logged_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(14);

    if (data) {
      const todayLog = data.find(d => d.logged_at === today);
      if (todayLog) {
        setTodayEntry(todayLog);
        setPhase('done');
      } else {
        setPhase('form');
      }
      setHistory(data.filter(d => d.logged_at !== today));
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const saveMood = async () => {
    if (!selectedMood || !user) return;
    setSaving(true);

    const rec = getRecommendation(selectedMood, energy);

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('mood_log')
      .insert({
        user_id: user.id,
        mood: selectedMood,
        energy_level: energy,
        notes: notes.trim(),
        recommended_mode: rec,
        logged_at: today,
      })
      .select()
      .single();

    if (!error && data) {
      setTodayEntry(data);
      setPhase('done');
      await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'mood', 1);
    }
    setSaving(false);
  };

  const moodToEmoji = (m: MoodType) => MOODS[m]?.emoji ?? '•';
  const energyToBar = (e: number) => Math.round((e / 5) * 100);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh]">
        <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
      {/* Заголовок */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-pink-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
          <Heart className="w-5 h-5 text-pink-600" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Трекер состояния</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Как ты себя чувствуешь? ИИ подберёт режим учёбы</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {/* ФОРМА ВВОДА НАСТРОЕНИЯ */}
        {phase === 'form' && (
          <motion.div
            key="form"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -15 }}
            className="space-y-5"
          >
            <div className="card p-6">
              <h2 className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-1">Как ты сегодня?</h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 mb-5">Выбери своё состояние прямо сейчас</p>

              <div className="flex sm:grid sm:grid-cols-5 gap-3 mb-8 overflow-x-auto pb-2 snap-x snap-mandatory [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden [-ms-overflow-style:none] [scrollbar-width:none]">
                {(Object.entries(MOODS) as [MoodType, MoodConfig][]).map(([key, cfg]) => {
                  const Icon = cfg.icon;
                  const isSelected = selectedMood === key;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={key}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedMood(key)}
                      className={`flex-none w-[100px] sm:w-auto flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 p-4 rounded-2xl border-2 transition-all snap-center ${
                        isSelected
                          ? `${cfg.bg} ${cfg.border} scale-[1.02] shadow-sm`
                          : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/60 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-600 bg-white/50 dark:bg-slate-800/50'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className="text-3xl mb-1 filter drop-shadow-sm">{cfg.emoji}</span>
                      <span className={`text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wide ${isSelected ? cfg.color : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400'}`}>
                        {cfg.label}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {/* Уровень энергии */}
              <div className="mb-8 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/40 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                  <label className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">
                    Уровень энергии
                  </label>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 px-2 py-1 rounded-md">
                    {ENERGY_LABELS[energy]}
                  </span>
                </div>
                
                <div className="relative pt-2 pb-6">
                  <div className="absolute top-4 left-0 w-full h-1.5 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <div 
                      className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-400 to-indigo-600 transition-all duration-300"
                      style={{ width: `${((energy - 1) / 4) * 100}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <input
                    type="range"
                    min={1}
                    max={5}
                    value={energy}
                    onChange={e => setEnergy(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="absolute top-2 left-0 w-full h-6 opacity-0 cursor-pointer z-10"
                  />
                  <div className="flex justify-between relative z-0 mt-6 px-1">
                    {[1,2,3,4,5].map(n => (
                      <div key={n} className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 -ml-3" style={{ width: '24px' }}>
                        <div className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full border-2 transition-all duration-300 ${energy >= n ? 'bg-indigo-600 border-indigo-600 shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(79,70,229,0.4)]' : 'bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 border-white dark:border-slate-800'}`} />
                        <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold ${energy === n ? 'text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400' : 'text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500'}`}>{n}</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Заметка */}
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-2">
                  Заметка (необязательно)
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={notes}
                  onChange={e => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Что беспокоит? Есть дедлайн? Хочется конкретного?"
                  className="w-full h-20 px-4 py-3 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition resize-none bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder-slate-400"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Превью рекомендации */}
              {selectedMood && (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                  style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}
                  className="mt-4 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/30 border border-indigo-100 dark:border-indigo-900/50 rounded-xl"
                >
                  <div className="p-3.5">
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-500 dark:text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Brain className="w-3 h-3" /> Рекомендация ИИ:
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-sm text-indigo-800 dark:text-indigo-200 font-medium leading-snug">
                      {getRecommendation(selectedMood, energy)}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </motion.div>
              )}

              <button
                disabled={!selectedMood || saving}
                onClick={saveMood}
                className="mt-5 w-full py-3 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-indigo-100"
              >
                <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                {saving ? 'Сохраняем...' : 'Получить план на сегодня'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* РЕЗУЛЬТАТ ДНЯ */}
        {phase === 'done' && todayEntry && (
          <motion.div
            key="done"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-5"
          >
            {/* Сегодняшний результат */}
            <div className={`card p-6 border-l-4 ${MOODS[todayEntry.mood]?.border ?? 'border-l-indigo-400'}`}>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-4">
                <span className="text-4xl">{moodToEmoji(todayEntry.mood)}</span>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Сегодня ты сказал(а)</p>
                  <h2 className={`text-lg font-black ${MOODS[todayEntry.mood]?.color ?? 'text-slate-900'}`}>
                    {MOODS[todayEntry.mood]?.label}
                  </h2>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">Энергия:</span>
                    <div className="w-20 h-1.5 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div
                        className="h-full bg-indigo-500 rounded-full transition-all"
                        style={{ width: `${energyToBar(todayEntry.energy_level)}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-600">{todayEntry.energy_level}/5</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Рекомендация */}
              <div className="p-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-50 to-purple-50 dark:from-indigo-950/30 dark:to-purple-950/30 border border-indigo-100 dark:border-indigo-900/50 rounded-xl">
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-500 dark:text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Brain className="w-3 h-3" /> Рекомендованный режим учёбы:
                </p>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 font-medium leading-relaxed">
                  {todayEntry.recommended_mode}
                </p>
              </div>

              {todayEntry.notes && (
                <div className="mt-3 p-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-xl">
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 italic">«{todayEntry.notes}»</p>
                </div>
              )}

              <button
                onClick={() => { setSelectedMood(null); setEnergy(3); setNotes(''); setPhase('form'); }}
                className="mt-4 text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 flex items-center gap-1 transition"
              >
                Изменить состояние на сегодня
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* История последних 14 дней */}
            {history.length > 0 && (
              <div className="card p-5">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 text-sm mb-4">История настроений (последние 14 дней)</h3>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {history.slice(0, 7).map(entry => (
                    <div key={entry.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 py-2 border-b border-slate-50 dark:border-slate-800 last:border-0">
                      <span className="text-lg w-8 text-center">{moodToEmoji(entry.mood)}</span>
                      <div className="flex-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                            {MOODS[entry.mood]?.label}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
                            {new Date(entry.logged_at).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="w-full h-1 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full mt-1">
                          <div
                            className="h-full bg-indigo-400 rounded-full"
                            style={{ width: `${energyToBar(entry.energy_level)}%` }}
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/pages/Profile.tsx

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { getProfile, updateProfile, uploadAvatar, UserProfile } from '../api/social';
import { getFriends, sendFriendRequest, respondFriendRequest, Friendship } from '../api/chat';
import { UserCircle2, GraduationCap, Trophy, BookOpen, Loader2, UserPlus, Check, Edit3, X, UserCheck, UserX, Bell, BellOff, Camera, Star } from 'lucide-react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { subscribeUserToPush, checkPushSubscription, requestNotificationPermission } from '../utils/push';
import UniversitySelect from '../components/UniversitySelect';
import { getUserXP, calcLevel } from '../api/quests';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import ImageCropper from '../components/ImageCropper';
import { CustomSelect } from '../components/CustomSelect';

export default function Profile() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { id: profileId } = useParams();
  const targetId = profileId || user?.id;
  
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  
  const [friendStatus, setFriendStatus] = useState<'none' | 'pending' | 'accepted'>('none');
  const [incomingRequests, setIncomingRequests] = useState<Friendship[]>([]);
  
  const isMyProfile = user?.id === targetId;

  // Edit State
  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState({ full_name: '', university: '', bio: '', avatar_url: '', role: 'student' });

  // Push notifications state
  const [pushEnabled, setPushEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [pushLoading, setPushLoading] = useState(true);

  // Avatar Upload state
  const [uploadingAvatar, setUploadingAvatar] = useState(false);
  const [cropImageSrc, setCropImageSrc] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // XP / Level state
  const [xp, setXp] = useState(0);
  const [levelInfo, setLevelInfo] = useState({ level: 1, name: 'Новичок', currentXP: 0, nextXP: 100, progress: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!targetId) return;
    
    getProfile(targetId).then(p => {
      setProfile(p);
      if (p) {
        setEditForm({
          full_name: p.full_name || '',
          university: p.university || '',
          bio: p.bio || '',
          avatar_url: p.avatar_url || '',
          role: p.role || 'student'
        });
      }
      setLoading(false);
    });

    getUserXP(targetId).then(v => {
      setXp(v);
      setLevelInfo(calcLevel(v));
    });

    if (user) {
      getFriends(user.id).then(friends => {
        if (!isMyProfile) {
          const friendship = friends.find(f => f.requester_id === targetId || f.addressee_id === targetId);
          if (friendship) setFriendStatus(friendship.status as 'pending' | 'accepted');
        } else {
          // Входящие заявки мне
          const incoming = friends.filter(f => f.addressee_id === user.id && f.status === 'pending');
          setIncomingRequests(incoming);
        }
      });
    }

    checkPushSubscription().then(enabled => {
      setPushEnabled(enabled);
      setPushLoading(false);
    });
  }, [targetId, user?.id, isMyProfile]);

  const handleAddFriend = async () => {
    if (!user || !targetId) return;
    const ok = await sendFriendRequest(user.id, targetId);
    if (ok) setFriendStatus('pending');
  };

  const handleRespondRequest = async (id: string, status: 'accepted' | 'declined') => {
    await respondFriendRequest(id, status);
    setIncomingRequests(prev => prev.filter(req => req.id !== id));
  };

  const handleSaveProfile = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    const ok = await updateProfile(user.id, editForm);
    if (ok) {
      setProfile(prev => prev ? { ...prev, ...editForm } : null);
      setIsEditing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleTogglePush = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    setPushLoading(true);
    try {
      if (!pushEnabled) {
        const granted = await requestNotificationPermission();
        if (!granted) {
          toast.error('Доступ к уведомлениям запрещён в браузере.');
          setPushLoading(false);
          return;
        }
        await subscribeUserToPush(user.id);
        setPushEnabled(true);
      } else {
        toast('Для отключения уведомлений перейдите в настройки браузера.', { icon: 'ℹ️' });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      toast.error('Ошибка настройки уведомлений: ' + (err as Error).message);
    }
    setPushLoading(false);
  };
