  const handleFileChange = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    // Считываем файл как URL, чтобы передать в кроппер
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.addEventListener('load', () => {
      setCropImageSrc(reader.result?.toString() || null);
    });
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    e.target.value = ''; // Сбросить инпут
  };

  const handleCropComplete = async (croppedFile: File) => {
    setCropImageSrc(null);
    setUploadingAvatar(true);
    const url = await uploadAvatar(user!.id, croppedFile);
    setUploadingAvatar(false);

    if (url) {
      setEditForm({ ...editForm, avatar_url: url });
      // Если не в режиме редактирования, сохраним сразу
      if (!isEditing) {
        await updateProfile(user!.id, { avatar_url: url });
        setProfile(prev => prev ? { ...prev, avatar_url: url } : null);
      }
      toast.success('Фото успешно обновлено!');
    } else {
      toast.error('Ошибка при загрузке фото.');
    }
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="flex min-h-[85vh] items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-500 animate-spin" /></div>;
  if (!profile) return <div className="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">Профиль не найден.</div>;

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-8 max-w-5xl mx-auto min-h-[85vh]">
      <motion.div 
        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        className="bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-xl rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-8 border border-white/20 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-2xl relative mb-8"
      >
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-tr from-indigo-500/10 via-purple-500/5 to-rose-500/10 pointer-events-none rounded-3xl" />
        
        <div className="relative flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center md:items-start gap-8 mb-8">
          <div className="relative group shrink-0">
            {/* Анимированное кольцо прогресса уровня */}
            <svg className="absolute -inset-2 w-[calc(100%+16px)] h-[calc(100%+16px)] -rotate-90 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(99,102,241,0.5)]">
              <circle cx="50%" cy="50%" r="48%" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" fill="transparent" className="text-slate-200/50 dark:text-slate-800/50" />
              <circle cx="50%" cy="50%" r="48%" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" fill="transparent" strokeDasharray={`${levelInfo.progress * 3.01} 301`} strokeLinecap="round" className="text-indigo-500 transition-all duration-1000 ease-out" />
            </svg>

            <div className={`w-32 h-32 md:w-40 md:h-40 rounded-full border-[6px] border-white dark:border-slate-900 shadow-xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 relative z-10 ${profile.is_pro ? 'ring-4 ring-amber-400 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(251,191,36,0.6)]' : ''}`}>
              {profile.avatar_url ? (
                <img src={profile.avatar_url} alt="avatar" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center">
                  <UserCircle2 className="w-16 h-16 md:w-20 md:h-20 text-slate-400" />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            
            {isMyProfile && (
              <label className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white rounded-full flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(79,70,229,0.6)] transition-all hover:scale-110 border-2 border-white dark:border-slate-900 z-10">
                {uploadingAvatar ? <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" /> : <Camera className="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6" />}
                <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={handleFileChange} disabled={uploadingAvatar} />
              </label>
            )}

            <div className="absolute -bottom-3 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-purple-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black tracking-widest shadow-lg border-2 border-white dark:border-slate-900 whitespace-nowrap">
              LVL {levelInfo.level}
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex-1 text-center md:text-left pt-2">
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center md:justify-start gap-3 mb-2">
              <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">{profile.full_name || 'Студент'}</h1>
              {profile.is_pro && (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center md:items-start">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-amber-600 text-white rounded-lg shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(251,191,36,0.5)]">
                    <Star className="w-4 h-4 fill-current" />
                    <span className="text-xs font-black tracking-widest uppercase">PRO</span>
                  </div>
                  {profile.pro_until && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1 uppercase tracking-widest font-bold">
                      до {new Date(profile.pro_until).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            {profile.university && (
              <div className="flex items-center justify-center md:justify-start gap-2 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 font-semibold mb-3">
                <GraduationCap className="w-5 h-5" />
                {profile.university}
              </div>
            )}
            {profile.role === 'teacher' && (
              <div className="inline-flex mb-4 items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider border border-amber-500/20">
                Преподаватель
              </div>
            )}

            {!isMyProfile ? (
              <div className="mt-4 flex justify-center md:justify-start gap-3">
                {friendStatus === 'none' && (
                  <button onClick={handleAddFriend} className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 hover:bg-indigo-500 transition shadow-lg hover:shadow-indigo-500/25">
                    <UserPlus className="w-5 h-5" /> Добавить в друзья
                  </button>
                )}
                {friendStatus === 'pending' && (
                  <button disabled className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-white/50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 cursor-not-allowed border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700">
                    <Check className="w-5 h-5" /> Заявка отправлена
                  </button>
                )}
                {friendStatus === 'accepted' && (
                  <button className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 border border-emerald-500/20">
                    <Check className="w-5 h-5" /> Уже в друзьях
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="mt-4 flex justify-center md:justify-start">
                <button onClick={() => setIsEditing(!isEditing)} className={`px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 transition shadow-sm border ${isEditing ? 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 border-rose-500/20 hover:bg-rose-500/20' : 'bg-white/50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:bg-white dark:hover:bg-slate-800'}`}>
                  {isEditing ? <X className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Edit3 className="w-4 h-4" />}
                  {isEditing ? 'Отменить редактирование' : 'Редактировать профиль'}
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Форма редактирования (плавная) */}
        {isEditing && (
          <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -20, height: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, height: 'auto' }} className="mb-8 p-6 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-xl relative z-20">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mb-4">Редактирование профиля</h3>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">Имя и Фамилия</label>
                  <input type="text" value={editForm.full_name} onChange={e => setEditForm({...editForm, full_name: e.target.value})} className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none transition shadow-inner" placeholder="Иван Иванов" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">Университет / Школа</label>
                  <UniversitySelect value={editForm.university} onChange={(val) => setEditForm({...editForm, university: val})} />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">О себе (Био)</label>
                <textarea value={editForm.bio} onChange={e => setEditForm({...editForm, bio: e.target.value})} className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none transition shadow-inner min-h-[100px] resize-none" rows={3} placeholder="Расскажите немного о себе..." />
              </div>
              <div className="w-full md:w-1/2 relative z-20">
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">Роль в системе</label>
                <CustomSelect 
                  value={editForm.role} 
                  onChange={val => setEditForm({...editForm, role: val})} 
                  className="px-4 py-3 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 shadow-inner"
                  options={[
                    { value: 'student', label: 'Студент' },
                    { value: 'teacher', label: 'Преподаватель' }
                  ]}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-end pt-4">
                <button onClick={handleSaveProfile} className="px-8 py-3 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-indigo-500 transition shadow-lg shadow-indigo-500/30 hover:-translate-y-1">Сохранить изменения</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {!isEditing && profile.bio && (
          <div className="mb-8 p-5 bg-white/40 dark:bg-slate-950/40 rounded-2xl border border-white/20 dark:border-slate-800/40 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed italic relative">
            <span className="absolute top-2 left-2 text-4xl text-indigo-500/20 font-serif leading-none">"</span>
            <div className="relative z-10 px-4">{profile.bio}</div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* BENTO GRID STATISTICS */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          <div className="col-span-2 md:col-span-2 row-span-2 p-6 bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-500 to-purple-600 rounded-3xl text-white shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col justify-center">
            <div className="absolute -top-10 -right-10 w-40 h-40 bg-white/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
            <div className="absolute -bottom-10 -left-10 w-40 h-40 bg-black/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
            <div className="relative z-10">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-white/20 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center backdrop-blur-sm">
                  <Star className="w-5 h-5 text-yellow-300 fill-yellow-300" />
                </div>
                <div className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-indigo-100">Опыт</div>
              </div>
              <div className="text-5xl font-black mb-1">{xp} <span className="text-xl text-indigo-200">XP</span></div>
              <div className="text-sm font-medium text-indigo-100 mb-4">{levelInfo.name}</div>
              
              <div className="h-2 w-full bg-black/20 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                <div className="h-full bg-white rounded-full transition-all duration-1000" style={{ width: `${levelInfo.progress}%` }} />
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between mt-2 text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-100 uppercase tracking-wider">
                <span>Ур. {levelInfo.level}</span>
                <span>{levelInfo.currentXP} / {levelInfo.nextXP} XP</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div className="p-5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800/50 backdrop-blur-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/50 rounded-3xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center shadow-sm hover:-translate-y-1 transition-transform cursor-default">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <Trophy className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-500" />
            </div>
            <div className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{profile.streak_days || 0}</div>
            <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">Огненный Стрик</div>
          </div>
          
          <div className="p-5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800/50 backdrop-blur-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/50 rounded-3xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center shadow-sm hover:-translate-y-1 transition-transform cursor-default">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-emerald-500/10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <BookOpen className="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-500" />
            </div>
            <div className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{profile.total_materials || 0}</div>
            <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">Конспектов</div>
          </div>

          <div className="col-span-2 p-5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800/50 backdrop-blur-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/50 rounded-3xl flex items-center gap-4 shadow-sm hover:-translate-y-1 transition-transform cursor-default">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-sky-500/10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <GraduationCap className="w-7 h-7 text-sky-500" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white leading-none mb-1">{profile.total_reviews || 0}</div>
              <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest">Пройдено повторений и экзаменов</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isMyProfile && (
          <div className="mt-8 pt-8 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-4">Настройки устройства</h3>
            <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/40 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3">
                <div className={`shrink-0 w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center ${pushEnabled ? 'bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-950/40' : 'bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800'}`}>
                  {pushEnabled ? <Bell className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600" /> : <BellOff className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-500" />}
                </div>
                <div className="text-left">
                  <p className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm">Push-уведомления (PWA)</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-tight mt-0.5">Ежедневные напоминания о материалах, требующих повторения.</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button 
                onClick={handleTogglePush}
                disabled={pushLoading || pushEnabled}
                className={`w-full sm:w-auto shrink-0 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold transition ${
                  pushLoading ? 'bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-600 cursor-not-allowed' :
                  pushEnabled ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/30 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 cursor-default border border-indigo-100 dark:border-indigo-950' :
                  'bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-650 dark:hover:bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                }`}
              >
                {pushLoading ? 'Загрузка...' : pushEnabled ? 'Включено' : 'Включить'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </motion.div>

      {/* Входящие заявки в друзья */}
      {isMyProfile && incomingRequests.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl p-6 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-4">Входящие заявки в друзья ({incomingRequests.length})</h2>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {incomingRequests.map(req => (
              <div key={req.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/40 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  {req.friend_avatar ? (
                    <img src={req.friend_avatar} className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover" alt="avatar" />
                  ) : (
                    <UserCircle2 className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-400" />
                  )}
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">{req.friend_name}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <button onClick={() => handleRespondRequest(req.id, 'accepted')} className="p-2 bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 hover:bg-emerald-200 dark:hover:bg-emerald-900/50 rounded-lg transition" title="Принять">
                    <UserCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => handleRespondRequest(req.id, 'declined')} className="p-2 bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950/40 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 hover:bg-rose-200 dark:hover:bg-rose-900/50 rounded-lg transition" title="Отклонить">
                    <UserX className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {cropImageSrc && (
        <ImageCropper 
          imageSrc={cropImageSrc} 
          onCropComplete={handleCropComplete} 
          onClose={() => setCropImageSrc(null)} 
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/UpdatePassword.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { GraduationCap, Lock, ArrowRight, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';

export default function UpdatePassword() {
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
    // Listen for auth state changes to make sure we are handling the password recovery event
    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(
      async (event, session) => {
        if (event == 'PASSWORD_RECOVERY') {
          // User is ready to update their password
        }
      }
    );
    return () => {
      authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);

  const handleUpdate = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    if (password.length < 6) {
      setError('Пароль должен содержать минимум 6 символов');
      return;
    }
    
    if (password !== confirm) {
      setError('Пароли не совпадают');
      return;
    }
    
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({
      password: password
    });
    
    if (error) {
      setError('Произошла ошибка при обновлении пароля.');
    } else {
      setSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => {
        navigate('/dashboard');
      }, 2000);
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f8fafc] dark:bg-slate-950 flex text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
      {/* Left Column: Form */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center px-6 sm:px-12 lg:px-24 xl:px-32 relative">
        <button onClick={() => navigate('/')} className="absolute top-8 left-6 sm:left-12 flex items-center gap-2 group">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-indigo-600 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center shadow-md">
            <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
          </div>
          <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 text-lg tracking-tight group-hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors">StudyForge</span>
        </button>

        <motion.div 
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
          className="w-full max-w-sm mx-auto"
        >
          <div className="mb-8">
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 tracking-tight mb-2">Новый пароль</h1>
            <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">Придумайте надежный пароль для вашего аккаунта</p>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }} className="bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/30 border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-900/50 text-rose-600 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium mb-6">
              {error}
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {success ? (
            <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }} className="bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-900 text-emerald-600 px-4 py-6 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold mb-6 flex flex-col items-center text-center gap-3 shadow-sm shadow-emerald-100 dark:shadow-none">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-1">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <p>Пароль успешно изменён! Перенаправляем...</p>
            </motion.div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleUpdate} className="space-y-5">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1.5">Новый пароль</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                    <Lock className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
                  </div>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="block w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-3 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 outline-none transition-all shadow-sm"
                    placeholder="••••••••"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1.5">Подтвердите пароль</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                    <Lock className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
                  </div>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    value={confirm}
                    onChange={e => setConfirm(e.target.value)}
                    className="block w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-3 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 outline-none transition-all shadow-sm"
                    placeholder="••••••••"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="group relative w-full flex justify-center py-3.5 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-white bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition-all shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 hover:shadow-indigo-300 disabled:opacity-70 mt-4"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2">Сохраняем... <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin" /></span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2">Сохранить <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" /></span>
                )}
              </button>
            </form>
          )}
        </motion.div>
      </div>

      {/* Right Column: Branded Visual (Hidden on mobile) */}
      <div className="hidden lg:flex flex-1 relative bg-indigo-600 overflow-hidden items-center justify-center p-12">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-500 to-indigo-800" />
        <div className="absolute top-[-10%] right-[-10%] w-96 h-96 bg-white/10 rounded-full blur-3xl" />
        
        <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-10" style={{ backgroundImage: 'radial-gradient(white 1px, transparent 1px)', backgroundSize: '24px 24px' }} />

        <motion.div 
          initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.9, rotateY: -15 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, rotateY: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.8, type: "spring" }}
          className="relative w-full max-w-md bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-xl border border-white/20 rounded-3xl p-8 shadow-2xl"
          style={{ transformStyle: "preserve-3d", perspective: 1000 }}
        >
          <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-white/20 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-6 border border-white/20 shadow-inner">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-6 h-6 text-white" />
          </div>
          
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-white leading-tight mb-4 tracking-tight">
            Финальный шаг
          </h2>
          
          <p className="text-indigo-100 leading-relaxed text-sm mb-6">
            Ваши данные будут надежно зашифрованы. Для большей безопасности используйте разные пароли на разных сервисах.
          </p>
        </motion.div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/SocialHub.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Globe, Trophy, Heart, Download, UserCircle2, Loader2, Search, X, CheckCircle2, Eye, FileText, Layers } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { getLeaderboard, getPublicMaterials, toggleLike, copyMaterialToLibrary, getPublicMaterialPreview, LeaderboardEntry, PublicMaterial } from '../api/social';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';
import { searchUsers } from '../api/chat';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkMath from 'remark-math';
import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex';
import 'katex/dist/katex.min.css';

export default function SocialHub() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'feed' | 'leaderboard' | 'search'>('feed');

  const [feed, setFeed] = useState<PublicMaterial[]>([]);
  const [leaderboard, setLeaderboard] = useState<LeaderboardEntry[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [myMaterials, setMyMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);

  // Поиск пользователей
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);

  // Уведомления о копировании
  const [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Предпросмотр
  const [previewMaterial, setPreviewMaterial] = useState<PublicMaterial | null>(null);
  const [previewData, setPreviewData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [previewLoading, setPreviewLoading] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    setLoading(true);
    getMaterials(user.id).then(setMyMaterials);
    if (activeTab === 'feed') {
      getPublicMaterials(user.id).then(data => { setFeed(data); setLoading(false); });
    } else if (activeTab === 'leaderboard') {
      getLeaderboard().then(data => { setLeaderboard(data); setLoading(false); });
    } else {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [user?.id, activeTab]);

  // Поиск с дебаунсом
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user || activeTab !== 'search') return;
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) { setSearchResults([]); return; }
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      setSearching(true);
      const results = await searchUsers(searchQuery, user.id);
      setSearchResults(results);
      setSearching(false);
    }, 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [searchQuery, user?.id, activeTab]);

  const handleLike = async (m: PublicMaterial) => {
    if (!user) return;
    const isLiked = m.is_liked_by_me;
    setFeed(prev => prev.map(item =>
      item.id === m.id ? { ...item, is_liked_by_me: !isLiked, likes_count: item.likes_count + (isLiked ? -1 : 1) } : item
    ));
    await toggleLike(user.id, m.id, !!isLiked);
  };

  const handleCopy = async (m: PublicMaterial) => {
    if (!user) return;
    const ok = await copyMaterialToLibrary(m.id, user.id);
    if (ok) {
      setCopiedId(m.id);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedId(null), 3000);
    }
  };

  const handlePreview = async (m: PublicMaterial) => {
    setPreviewMaterial(m);
    setPreviewData(null);
    setPreviewLoading(true);
    const data = await getPublicMaterialPreview(m.id);
    setPreviewData(data);
    setPreviewLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-8 max-w-5xl mx-auto min-h-[85vh]">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-8">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 tracking-tight flex items-center gap-3">
            <Globe className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-600" />
            Study Network
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">Глобальное сообщество студентов</p>
        </div>

        {/* Табы */}
        <div className="flex bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-900 p-1 rounded-xl overflow-x-auto custom-scrollbar">
          {[
            { key: 'feed', label: 'Маркетплейс' },
            { key: 'leaderboard', label: 'Лидерборд' },
            { key: 'search', label: '🔍 Найти' },
          ].map(tab => (
            <button
              key={tab.key}
              onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.key as any)}
              className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                activeTab === tab.key ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-slate-200'
              }`}
            >
              {tab.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Поиск пользователей ── */}
      {activeTab === 'search' && (
        <div>
          <div className="relative mb-6">
            <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-5 h-5 text-slate-400" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Найдите студентов по имени..."
              className="w-full pl-12 pr-4 py-3.5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/50 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 shadow-sm"
              autoFocus
            />
            {searchQuery && (
              <button onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')} className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600">
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {searching && <div className="flex justify-center py-10"><Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-500 animate-spin" /></div>}

          {!searching && searchResults.length === 0 && searchQuery && (
            <p className="text-center text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-505 py-10">Никого не найдено по запросу «{searchQuery}»</p>
          )}

          {!searching && searchResults.length === 0 && !searchQuery && (
            <p className="text-center text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-505 py-10">Введите имя студента, чтобы найти его</p>
          )}

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {searchResults.map(u => (
              <motion.div
                key={u.id}
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 5 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl p-4 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow"
              >
                <button onClick={() => navigate(`/profile/${u.id}`)} className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-1 text-left">
                  {u.avatar_url ? (
                    <img src={u.avatar_url} className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full object-cover" alt="avatar" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-850 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                      <UserCircle2 className="w-7 h-7 text-slate-400" />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">{u.full_name}</div>
                    {u.university && <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{u.university}</div>}
                  </div>
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => navigate(`/profile/${u.id}`)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/30 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 font-bold text-sm rounded-xl hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/50 transition"
                >
                  Профиль →
                </button>
              </motion.div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Лента / Лидерборд ── */}
      {activeTab !== 'search' && (
        loading ? (
          <div className="flex justify-center py-20">
            <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-500 animate-spin" />
          </div>
        ) : activeTab === 'feed' ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:gap-6">
            {feed.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="col-span-2 text-center py-20 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                <Globe className="w-12 h-12 text-slate-200 dark:text-slate-700 mx-auto mb-4" />
                Пока никто не опубликовал материалы. Откройте свои в Библиотеке!
              </div>
            ) : (
              feed.map((m, i) => (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  transition={{ delay: i * 0.04 }}
                  key={m.id}
                  className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl p-6 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center mb-4">
                    <button onClick={() => navigate(`/profile/${m.user_id}`)} className="flex items-center gap-3 group flex-1">
                      {m.author_avatar ? (
                        <img src={m.author_avatar} alt="avatar" className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                          <UserCircle2 className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-400" />
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <div className="text-left">
                        <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 group-hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors text-sm">{m.author_name}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">{new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleDateString('ru')}</div>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="mb-5">
                    <div className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-500 dark:text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">{m.subject || 'Без предмета'}</div>
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 line-clamp-2 break-words">{m.title}</h3>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/60">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleLike(m)}
                      className={`flex items-center gap-2 font-medium transition-colors text-sm ${
                        m.is_liked_by_me ? 'text-pink-600' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-pink-600'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <Heart className={`w-4 h-4 ${m.is_liked_by_me ? 'fill-pink-600' : ''}`} />
                      {m.likes_count}
                    </button>

                    {String(m.user_id) !== String(user?.id) && (
                      <div className="flex flex-col xl:flex-row gap-2 items-stretch xl:items-center w-full sm:w-auto">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handlePreview(m)}
                          className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 font-bold text-sm transition-colors px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700"
                        >
                          <Eye className="w-4 h-4" /> <span>Предпросмотр</span>
                        </button>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleCopy(m)}
                          disabled={copiedId === m.id || myMaterials.some(my => my.title === m.title + ' (копия)')}
                          className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-2 font-bold text-sm transition-colors px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${
                            (copiedId === m.id || myMaterials.some(my => my.title === m.title + ' (копия)'))
                              ? 'bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/30 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400'
                              : 'bg-indigo-600 dark:bg-indigo-700 text-white hover:bg-indigo-700 dark:hover:bg-indigo-600'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {(copiedId === m.id || myMaterials.some(my => my.title === m.title + ' (копия)')) ? (
                            <><CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" /> Сохранено!</>
                          ) : (
                            <><Download className="w-4 h-4" /> <span>Сохранить</span></>
                          )}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </motion.div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* Лидерборд */
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
            <div className="p-6 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/30">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Trophy className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-500" />
                Топ 50 студентов
              </h2>
            </div>
            <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800">
              {leaderboard.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="p-10 text-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Нет данных для рейтинга.</div>
              ) : (
                leaderboard.map((u, i) => (
                  <div key={u.id} className="p-4 sm:px-6 flex items-center gap-4 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/50 transition-colors">
                    <div className={`w-8 font-black text-center shrink-0 ${
                      i === 0 ? 'text-amber-500 text-2xl' :
                      i === 1 ? 'text-slate-400 text-xl' :
                      i === 2 ? 'text-amber-700 text-xl' : 'text-slate-300 text-base'
                    }`}>
                      {i < 3 ? ['🥇','🥈','🥉'][i] : `#${i+1}`}
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={() => navigate(`/profile/${u.id}`)} className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1 text-left">
                      {u.avatar_url ? (
                        <img src={u.avatar_url} alt="avatar" className="w-11 h-11 rounded-full object-cover shadow-sm" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-11 h-11 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
                          <UserCircle2 className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400" />
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors">{u.full_name}</div>
                        <div className="text-sm font-medium text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md inline-block mt-0.5">
                          {u.total_xp.toLocaleString()} XP
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )
      )}

      {/* Модалка предпросмотра */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {previewMaterial && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
              onClick={() => setPreviewMaterial(null)}
              className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm"
            />
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 10 }}
              className="relative w-[calc(100%-32px)] md:w-full max-w-3xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 pb-4">
                <div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-500 dark:text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">
                    {previewMaterial.subject || 'Без предмета'}
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 break-words line-clamp-3">{previewMaterial.title}</h3>
                  <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <UserCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" /> Автор: {previewMaterial.author_name}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setPreviewMaterial(null)}
                  className="p-2 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 rounded-full transition text-slate-400"
                >
                  <X className="w-5 h-5" />
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar pr-2 space-y-6">
                {previewLoading ? (
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-500 animate-spin mb-4" />
                    <p>Загрузка данных для предпросмотра...</p>
                  </div>
                ) : previewData ? (
                  <>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                      <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-950/30 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
                          <Layers className="w-5 h-5" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">Карточки</p>
                          <p className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            {previewData.flashcards_json?.flashcards?.length || 0} шт.
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950/30 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
                          <FileText className="w-5 h-5" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">Тесты</p>
                          <p className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            {previewData.quiz_json?.quiz?.length || 0} шт.
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {previewData.summary_json && (
                      <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5">
                        <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                          <Eye className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-500" /> Отрывок конспекта
                        </h4>
                        <div className="text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed markdown-content max-h-64 overflow-y-hidden relative">
                          <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkMath]} rehypePlugins={[rehypeKatex]}>
                            {typeof previewData.summary_json === 'string' 
                              ? previewData.summary_json.slice(0, 500) + '...'
                              : (previewData.summary_json.content?.slice(0, 500) || '') + '...'}
                          </ReactMarkdown>
                          <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-24 bg-gradient-to-t from-slate-50 dark:from-slate-950 to-transparent pointer-events-none" />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <div className="text-center py-10 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    Не удалось загрузить данные
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/80 flex gap-3">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setPreviewMaterial(null)}
                  className="flex-1 py-3 px-4 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  Закрыть
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (!(copiedId === previewMaterial.id || myMaterials.some(my => my.title === previewMaterial.title + ' (копия)'))) {
                      handleCopy(previewMaterial);
                      setPreviewMaterial(null);
                    }
                  }}
                  disabled={copiedId === previewMaterial.id || myMaterials.some(my => my.title === previewMaterial.title + ' (копия)')}
                  className={`flex-1 py-3 px-4 rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${
                    (copiedId === previewMaterial.id || myMaterials.some(my => my.title === previewMaterial.title + ' (копия)'))
                      ? 'bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/30 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400'
                      : 'bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white shadow-lg shadow-indigo-100'
                  }`}
                >
                  {(copiedId === previewMaterial.id || myMaterials.some(my => my.title === previewMaterial.title + ' (копия)')) ? (
                    <><CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5" /> Сохранено!</>
                  ) : (
                    <><Download className="w-5 h-5" /> Сохранить себе</>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          </div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/QuickPrep.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Zap, BookOpen, HelpCircle, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, ArrowRight, ChevronRight, ChevronDown, X, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkMath from 'remark-math';
import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex';
import 'katex/dist/katex.min.css';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { useConfirm } from '../contexts/ConfirmContext';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';
import { checkAILimit, incrementAIUsage } from '../api/limits';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

interface QuickPrepData {
  key_points: string[];
  cheatsheet: string;
  questions: { question: string; answer: string }[];
}

type Phase = 'select' | 'loading' | 'cheatsheet' | 'quiz' | 'done';

export default function QuickPrep() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { alert } = useConfirm();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  // Восстановление состояния из sessionStorage
  const savedStateStr = sessionStorage.getItem('studyforge_quickprep_state');
  const savedState = savedStateStr ? JSON.parse(savedStateStr) : null;

  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [isDropdownOpen, setIsDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string>(savedState?.selectedId || '');
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>(
    savedState?.phase === 'loading' ? 'select' : (savedState?.phase || 'select')
  );
  const [data, setData] = useState<QuickPrepData | null>(savedState?.data || null);
  const [quizIndex, setQuizIndex] = useState<number>(savedState?.quizIndex || 0);
  const [showAnswer, setShowAnswer] = useState<boolean>(savedState?.showAnswer || false);
  const [timer, setTimer] = useState<number>(savedState?.timer !== undefined ? savedState.timer : 300); // 5 минут

  // Сохранение состояния в sessionStorage
  useEffect(() => {
    sessionStorage.setItem('studyforge_quickprep_state', JSON.stringify({
      selectedId,
      phase,
      data,
      quizIndex,
      showAnswer,
      timer
    }));
  }, [selectedId, phase, data, quizIndex, showAnswer, timer]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    getMaterials(user.id).then(setMaterials).catch(console.error);
  }, [user?.id]);

  // Таймер
  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase !== 'cheatsheet' && phase !== 'quiz') return;
    if (timer <= 0) return;
    const t = setInterval(() => setTimer(p => p - 1), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [phase, timer]);

  const selectedMaterial = materials.find(m => m.id === selectedId);

  const startPrep = async () => {
    if (!selectedMaterial) return;
    setPhase('loading');
    setTimer(300);
    setQuizIndex(0);
    setShowAnswer(false);

    const summary = typeof selectedMaterial.summary_json === 'string'
      ? selectedMaterial.summary_json
      : (selectedMaterial.summary_json?.content || '');
    const context = (selectedMaterial.original_text || summary || '').slice(0, 15000);

    if (user?.id) {
      const limitCheck = await checkAILimit(user.id, 5);
      if (!limitCheck.allowed) {
        setPhase('select');
        await alert({ title: 'Энергия ИИ исчерпана', message: 'Не хватает энергии ИИ (нужно 5). Перейдите на PRO-тариф!', type: 'danger' });
        navigate('/pricing');
        return;
      }
      await incrementAIUsage(user.id, 5);
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/quick-prep', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ context }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
      const d = await res.json();
      setData(d);
      setPhase('cheatsheet');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      await alert({ title: 'Ошибка', message: 'Не удалось подготовить материал. Попробуйте ещё раз.', type: 'danger' });
      setPhase('select');
    }
  };

  const formatTime = (s: number) => `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto min-h-[80vh] flex flex-col justify-center">
      {/* Заголовок */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6 justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-950/50 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
            <Zap className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Повтор за 5 минут</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Быстрое освежение памяти перед парой</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {phase !== 'select' && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 md:gap-3">
            <div className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 md:px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold ${timer < 60 ? 'bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950/40 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400' : 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400'}`}>
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
              {formatTime(timer)}
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setPhase('select');
                setSelectedId('');
                setData(null);
                setQuizIndex(0);
                setShowAnswer(false);
                setTimer(300);
              }}
              className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1 md:gap-1.5 px-2 md:px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/30 hover:text-rose-600 dark:hover:text-rose-400 rounded-lg text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 transition"
            >
              <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Выйти
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {/* ВЫБОР МАТЕРИАЛА */}
        {phase === 'select' && (
          <motion.div
            key="select"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -15 }}
            className="card p-6 md:p-8 text-center"
          >
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <Zap className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-500" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2">Осталось 5 минут до пары?</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-sm mx-auto mb-6">
              Выберите лекцию — AI мгновенно создаст шпаргалку и 2 разогревочных вопроса. Идеально для освежения памяти.
            </p>
            <div className="max-w-sm mx-auto space-y-4">
              <div className="relative text-left">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsDropdownOpen(!isDropdownOpen)}
                  className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 transition shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between font-medium"
                >
                  <span>{selectedMaterial ? selectedMaterial.title : 'Выберите лекцию'}</span>
                  <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 transition-transform duration-200 ${isDropdownOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
                </button>
                <AnimatePresence>
                  {isDropdownOpen && (
                    <>
                      <div className="fixed inset-0 z-40" onClick={() => setIsDropdownOpen(false)} />
                      <motion.div
                        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                        exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
                        className="absolute left-0 right-0 mt-2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl shadow-2xl z-50 max-h-60 overflow-y-auto"
                      >
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => { setSelectedId(''); setIsDropdownOpen(false); }}
                          className="w-full text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-sm hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-900 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 transition-colors border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-900"
                        >
                          Выберите лекцию
                        </button>
                        {materials.map(m => (
                          <button
                            key={m.id}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => { setSelectedId(m.id); setIsDropdownOpen(false); }}
                            className={`w-full text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-sm transition-colors flex items-center justify-between ${
                              selectedId === m.id
                                ? 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 font-bold'
                                : 'hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-900 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'
                            }`}
                          >
                            <span>{m.title}</span>
                          </button>
                        ))}
                      </motion.div>
                    </>
                  )}
                </AnimatePresence>
              </div>
              <button
                disabled={!selectedId}
                onClick={startPrep}
                className="w-full py-3.5 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-amber-100 dark:shadow-amber-950/20"
              >
                <Zap className="w-5 h-5" /> Подготовить за 5 минут
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ЗАГРУЗКА */}
        {phase === 'loading' && (
          <motion.div
            key="loading"
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center"
          >
            <div className="relative mb-6">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-amber-100 dark:border-amber-950/50 border-t-amber-500 rounded-full animate-spin" />
              <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Zap className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-500 animate-pulse" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 mb-1">AI составляет экспресс-шпаргалку...</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 max-w-xs">Выжимаем самое главное из лекции — займёт пару секунд</p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ШПАРГАЛКА */}
        {phase === 'cheatsheet' && data && (
          <motion.div
            key="cheatsheet"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.98 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-5"
          >
            {/* Прогресс */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {[
                { label: '1. Шпаргалка', active: true, done: false },
                { label: '2. Вопросы', active: false, done: false },
                { label: '3. Готов!', active: false, done: false },
              ].map((step, i) => (
                <div key={i} className={`flex-1 text-center text-xs font-bold py-2 rounded-lg transition ${step.active ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white' : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500'}`}>
                  {step.label}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Ключевые тезисы */}
            <div className="card p-5">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
                Топ-3 самых важных тезиса
              </h3>
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {data.key_points.map((point, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-3">
                    <span className="w-6 h-6 bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-950/50 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-black shrink-0 mt-0.5">{i + 1}</span>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 leading-relaxed">{point}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Мини-шпаргалка */}
            <div className="bg-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-800/80 text-white rounded-2xl p-5">
              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">⚡ Экспресс-шпаргалка</p>
              <div className="text-sm text-slate-300 leading-relaxed markdown-content white-text">
                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkMath]} rehypePlugins={[rehypeKatex]}>{data.cheatsheet}</ReactMarkdown>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={() => { setPhase('quiz'); setQuizIndex(0); setShowAnswer(false); }}
              className="w-full py-3.5 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-amber-100 dark:shadow-amber-950/20"
            >
              Перейти к вопросам <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ВОПРОСЫ */}
        {phase === 'quiz' && data && (
          <motion.div
            key={`quiz-${quizIndex}`}
            initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-5"
          >
            {/* Прогресс */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {[
                { label: '1. Шпаргалка', active: false, done: true },
                { label: `2. Вопрос ${quizIndex + 1}/${data.questions.length}`, active: true, done: false },
                { label: '3. Готов!', active: false, done: false },
              ].map((step, i) => (
                <div key={i} className={`flex-1 text-center text-xs font-bold py-2 rounded-lg transition ${step.active ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white' : step.done ? 'bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400' : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500'}`}>
                  {step.done ? '✓ ' : ''}{step.label}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="card p-5 md:p-6 space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <HelpCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-500" />
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Разогревочный вопрос</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 leading-relaxed">
                {data.questions[quizIndex]?.question}
              </p>

              {!showAnswer ? (
                <button
                  onClick={() => setShowAnswer(true)}
                  className="w-full py-3 border-2 border-dashed border-amber-300 dark:border-amber-700 hover:border-amber-400 dark:hover:border-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-50 dark:hover:bg-amber-950/30 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 rounded-xl font-bold transition text-sm"
                >
                  Показать ответ
                </button>
              ) : (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                  style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}
                  className="bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/30 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-900/50 rounded-xl"
                >
                  <div className="p-4">
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" /> Правильный ответ:
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 leading-relaxed">{data.questions[quizIndex]?.answer}</p>
                  </div>
                </motion.div>
              )}

              {showAnswer && (
                <motion.button
                  initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (quizIndex + 1 >= data.questions.length) {
                      setPhase('done');
                    } else {
                      setQuizIndex(q => q + 1);
                      setShowAnswer(false);
                    }
                  }}
                  className="w-full py-3 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  {quizIndex + 1 >= data.questions.length ? 'Я готов! 🎯' : 'Следующий вопрос'} <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                </motion.button>
              )}
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФИНАЛ */}
        {phase === 'done' && (
          <motion.div
            key="done"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            className="card p-8 md:p-10 text-center bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-50 to-orange-50 dark:from-amber-950/20 dark:to-orange-950/20 border-amber-100 dark:border-amber-900/30"
          >
            <div className="text-6xl mb-4">🎯</div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 mb-2">Ты готов к паре!</h2>
            <p className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 text-sm max-w-sm mx-auto mb-6">
              Главное — помни ключевые тезисы. Ты потратил менее 5 минут и освежил память по «{selectedMaterial?.title}».
            </p>
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 justify-center">
              <button
                onClick={() => { setPhase('select'); setSelectedId(''); }}
                className="flex-1 py-3 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition"
              >
                Другая лекция
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')}
                className="px-6 py-3 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
              >
                На дашборд
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Messages.tsx
================================================================================
import { Material } from '../types';
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { getFriends, Friendship, getMessages, sendMessage, subscribeToMessages, DirectMessage } from '../api/chat';
import { Send, UserCircle2, MessageSquare, Loader2, BookOpen, Link2, ChevronLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';

export default function Messages() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [friends, setFriends] = useState<Friendship[]>([]);
  const [activeFriend, setActiveFriend] = useState<Friendship | null>(null);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<DirectMessage[]>([]);
  const [newMessage, setNewMessage] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showInvite, setShowInvite] = useState(false);
  const [myMaterials, setMyMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    getFriends(user.id).then(f => {
      setFriends(f.filter(x => x.status === 'accepted'));
      setLoading(false);
    });
    getMaterials(user.id).then(setMyMaterials);
  }, [user?.id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user || !activeFriend) return;
    const friendId = activeFriend.friend_id!;
    getMessages(user.id, friendId).then(setMessages);

    const unsubscribe = subscribeToMessages(user.id, (msg) => {
      if (
        (msg.sender_id === user.id && msg.receiver_id === friendId) ||
        (msg.sender_id === friendId && msg.receiver_id === user.id)
      ) {
        setMessages(prev => prev.find(m => m.id === msg.id) ? prev : [...prev, msg]);
      }
    });
    return () => { unsubscribe(); };
  }, [user?.id, activeFriend]);

  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [messages]);

  const handleSend = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!user || !activeFriend || !newMessage.trim()) return;
    const content = newMessage.trim();
    setNewMessage('');
    const msg = await sendMessage(user.id, activeFriend.friend_id!, content);
    if (msg) setMessages(prev => [...prev, msg]);
  };

  const handleSendInvite = async (material: Material) => {
    if (!user || !activeFriend) return;
    const inviteText = `📚 Приглашаю изучить материал: «${material.title}» (${material.subject || 'Без предмета'}) — открой в своей библиотеке или найди в Study Network!`;
    setShowInvite(false);
    const msg = await sendMessage(user.id, activeFriend.friend_id!, inviteText);
    if (msg) setMessages(prev => [...prev, msg]);
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="flex h-[85vh] items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-500 animate-spin" /></div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-[calc(100vh-80px)] md:h-[85vh] bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 md:rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 overflow-hidden md:m-6 m-0 relative">

      {/* Левая панель — список диалогов */}
      <div className={`w-full md:w-80 border-r border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/40 shrink-0 ${activeFriend ? 'hidden md:flex md:flex-col' : 'flex flex-col'}`}>
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900">
          <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 flex items-center gap-2">
            <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-500" />
            Сообщения
          </h2>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {friends.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-6 text-center">
              <UserCircle2 className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-200 dark:text-slate-800 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">У вас пока нет друзей.</p>
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate('/network')}
                className="mt-3 text-sm text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 font-bold hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300"
              >
                Найти в Хабе →
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            friends.map(f => (
              <button
                key={f.id}
                onClick={() => setActiveFriend(f)}
                className={`w-full p-4 flex items-center gap-3 text-left transition-colors border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/40 ${
                  activeFriend?.id === f.id ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40' : 'hover:bg-white dark:hover:bg-slate-800/30'
                }`}
              >
                {f.friend_avatar ? (
                  <img src={f.friend_avatar} alt="avatar" className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover shrink-0" />
                ) : (
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <UserCircle2 className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-500" />
                  </div>
                )}
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm truncate">{f.friend_name}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">Нажмите, чтобы открыть чат</div>
                </div>
              </button>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Правая панель — чат */}
      <div className={`w-full md:flex-1 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 relative min-w-0 ${!activeFriend ? 'hidden md:flex md:flex-col' : 'flex flex-col'}`}>
        {activeFriend ? (
          <>
            {/* Хедер */}
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center gap-3 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 shadow-sm z-10">
              <button onClick={() => setActiveFriend(null)} className="md:hidden p-2 -ml-2 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-800 dark:hover:text-slate-200 transition">
                <ChevronLeft className="w-6 h-6" />
              </button>
              {activeFriend.friend_avatar ? (
                <img src={activeFriend.friend_avatar} alt="avatar" className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full object-cover shrink-0" />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <UserCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-400" />
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="flex-1">
                <div className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">{activeFriend.friend_name}</div>
              </div>
              {/* Кнопка "Пригласить к материалу" */}
              <div className="relative">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setShowInvite(v => !v)}
                  className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/50 transition px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg"
                  title="Поделиться материалом"
                >
                  <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
                  Поделиться материалом
                </button>

                {showInvite && (
                  <div className="absolute right-0 top-10 w-72 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-2xl shadow-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 z-20 overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="p-3 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700">
                      <p className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-250">Отправить материал в чат</p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-slate-50 dark:divide-slate-700">
                      {myMaterials.length === 0 ? (
                        <p className="p-4 text-sm text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 text-center">У вас нет материалов</p>
                      ) : myMaterials.map(m => (
                        <button
                          key={m.id}
                          onClick={() => handleSendInvite(m)}
